# DeepQuarry deep audit — TRIAGED findings

Coverage: **100% — all 5,486 .dm files read.** Every raw finding adversarially re-verified by a second agent that re-read the cited code.

Result: **816 confirmed real / 23 rejected as false positives** (out of 839).
Confirmed by severity: **2 critical, 149 high, 335 medium, 330 low.**

## CONFIRMED — CRITICAL
### code/game/machinery/computer/guestpass.dm:79-86  [wrong-variable / global state corruption]
- **What:** In /obj/item/card/id/guest/process(), line 85 executes `world.time = expiration_time`, assigning to the global game clock. `world.time` is the server's master tick counter; writing the pass's expiration timestamp into it is never intended here.
- **Confirmed:** real: guestpass.dm line 85 world.time = expiration_time writes the global tick counter on pass expiry, corrupting the clock (timers/cooldowns/SS scheduling); line 84 expired=1 already does the needed state change.
- **Fix:** null

### code/modules/mob/mob_planes.dm:277-279  [lifecycle / duplicate definition / ref leak]
- **What:** A second full definition of /mob/Destroy() exists here (`/mob/Destroy()` -> `. = ..()` then `GLOB.entopic_users -= src`). mob.dm line 1 already defines /mob/Destroy() with all the real cleanup (unset_machine, clearing screen objs, ghostize, QDEL_NULL of soulgem/dna/plane_holder/hud_used, clearing alerts/viruses, stop_pulling, vore_organs, motiontracker_unsubscribe, mind cleanup, update_client_z, etc.). mob_planes.dm is #included AFTER mob.dm (deepquarry.dme lines 3162 vs 3168), so this stub redefinition wins and entirely replaces the real one.
- **Confirmed:** real: /mob/Destroy() defined twice — full cleanup in mob.dm:1 and a stub (.=..();GLOB.entopic_users-=src) in mob_planes.dm:277, included after mob.dm. Duplicate proc def: either last-wins skips all real cleanup or build hard-errors.
- **Fix:** null
