- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/passive_gate.dm:195-196** [wrong-variable / copy-paste] — In receive_signal(), the `"set_flow_rate" in signal.data` branch assigns `regulate_mode = text2num(signal.data["set_flow_rate"])` instead of `set_flow_rate`. The preceding branch (`"set_regulate_mode"`) already sets regulate_mode; this one clobbers regulate_mode with the flow-rate value and never updates set_flow_rate.
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/passive_gate.dm:195-196** [wrong-variable / copy-paste] — In receive_signal(), the `"set_flow_rate" in signal.data` branch assigns `regulate_mode = text2num(signal.data["set_flow_rate"])` instead of `set_flow_rate`. The preceding branch (`"set_regulate_mode"`) already sets regulate_mode; this one clobbers regulate_mode with the flow-rate value and never updates set_flow_rate.
- **code/__defines/verdigris/_verdigris.dm:17-23** [dead/unreachable code (duplicate proc definition)] — This file defines a full `/world/New()` (which calls `verdigris_init()`, `verdigris_cleanup()`, logs the loaded version, then `..()`). But `code/game/world.dm` line 128 also defines `/world/New()` (the real game-boot proc). Both are compiled into the build: in deepquarry.dme, `_verdigris.dm` is included at line 377 and `code/game/world.dm` at line 1049. Under BYOND the last-compiled definition of a proc path wins, so `code/game/world.dm`'s `/world/New()` overrides and completely discards the verdigris one. `verdigris_init()` is never called at boot (grep confirms it is invoked nowhere else; only the unit test calls `verdigris_version()`/`verdigris_features()`). Under OpenDream this is worse — `__pragmas.dm` sets `DuplicateProcDefinition error`, so it would fail to compile.
- **code/_helpers/gender.dm:21-22** [infinite-recursion] — /datum/p_Theirs(temp_gender) returns capitalize(p_Theirs(temp_gender)) - it calls ITSELF instead of the lowercase p_theirs(). Every other capitalized variant in this block correctly delegates to its lowercase counterpart (e.g. p_Their -> p_their).
- **code/_helpers/gender.dm:454** [wrong-variable/type-error] — In /mob/living/carbon/human/get_visible_gender(), the face-hiding check reads `(wear_mask && (wear_mask & HIDEFACE))`. `wear_mask` is an /obj/item/clothing object reference, not a bitfield; it should be `wear_mask.flags_inv & HIDEFACE`. Every other site in the codebase (human.dm:345, examine.dm:63, timeclock.dm:252, id_restorer.dm:51) uses `wear_mask.flags_inv & HIDEFACE`.
- **code/_helpers/icons/flatten.dm:157-162, 180-185** [copy-paste / missing break (logic bug)] — In _get_flat_icon(), the insertion-sort loops that place overlays and underlays into the `gathered` assoc list lack a `break` after `gathered.Insert(i, copying)`. The loop `for(i in 1 to gathered.len) ... if(current_layer < gathered[comparing]) gathered.Insert(i, copying)` keeps iterating after the insert, so the same appearance can be inserted at multiple positions, and the list length grows during iteration. The correct/parallel implementation in code/_helpers/icons.dm (PROCESS_OVERLAYS_OR_UNDERLAYS macro, lines 121-128) has a `break` immediately after the Insert.
- **code/controllers/subsystems/atoms.dm:203 (with 76-77)** [wrong qdel target / resource leak] — prepare_deletion() stores `WEAKREF(target)` into queued_deletions (line 203), but InitializeAtoms() later iterates that list and calls `qdel(queued_deletion)` directly (lines 76-77). The list entries are /datum/weakref instances, so qdel() deletes the weakref wrapper, never the atom it points to (qdel of a weakref does not resolve it).
- **code/controllers/subsystems/explosions.dm:127** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In fire_prepare_explosions(), the third lateral spread direction is computed as `T = get_step(src, turn(direction,-90))`, passing `src` (the explosions subsystem datum) where the two sibling branches on lines 121 and 124 correctly pass `epicenter` (the turf). get_step() with a non-turf/non-movable reference returns null.
- **code/controllers/subsystems/nightshift.dm:58-70** [wrong-variable / global-list corruption] — update_nightshift() sets `currentrun = GLOB.apcs` (a reference alias, not a copy) on the `!resumed` path, then in the processing loop does `currentrun -= apc` for every APC it touches. Because currentrun IS GLOB.apcs, this permanently removes every processed APC from the global APC registry. The `forced = TRUE` admin path additionally skips MC_TICK_CHECK, so the entire loop runs in one fire and empties GLOB.apcs completely.
- **code/controllers/subsystems/transcore.dm:104-108** [runtime/null-deref + wrong-variable] — In process_backups(), the invalid-record guard `if(!curr_MR)` then executes `log_runtime("Tried to process [name]...")` and `db.backed_up -= curr_MR.mindname`. `name` is not a variable in this proc's scope (it only exists in the !resumed builder loop above), and `curr_MR.mindname` dereferences curr_MR immediately after confirming it is null.
- **code/datums/components/antags/changeling/powers/shriek.dm:134** [wrong-variable / state corruption] — In changeling_dissonant_shriek(), the recursive-enhancement branch sets `changeling.recursive_enhancement = FALSE` after applying the doubled range. This permanently clears the changeling's Recursive Enhancement upgrade after a single Dissonant Shriek use. The parallel changeling_resonant_shriek() (line 52-54) correctly does NOT clear it.
- **code/datums/components/orbiter.dm:116-138** [use-after-qdel / missing return] — In move_react(), when get_turf(master) returns null the code calls qdel(src) but does not return. Execution falls through and continues to dereference oldloc/orbited and then iterates the component's orbiters list and calls forceMove on each, all on a component that has just been queued for deletion.
- **code/datums/components/radioactive_exposure.dm:64** [wrong istype usage / broken condition] — on_exited() does `if(istype(get_area(parent), radioactive_areas))` where radioactive_areas is a `var/list` (set in Initialize). istype()'s second argument must be a type path, not a list; the intended semantics are 'is the new area's type in radioactive_areas'.
- **code/datums/components/species/shadekin/powers/dark_tunnel/dark_tunnel_structures.dm:165** [wrong-variable/arg-mismatch] — SK.shadekin_adjust_energy(user, -10) passes two arguments, but the proc is defined as /datum/component/shadekin/proc/shadekin_adjust_energy(amount) (single arg, see comp_helpers.dm line 210). The mob `user` is bound to `amount` and the intended `-10` is dropped. shadekin_adjust_energy then does isnum(user) which is FALSE and returns early.
- **code/datums/components/traits/gargoyle.dm:114** [signal/proc-ref bug] — RegisterSignal(parent, COMSIG_MOVABLE_MOVED, /datum/component/gargoyle/proc/unpause) passes a raw type-path instead of a proc-ref string. Everywhere else in this file uses PROC_REF(...) (e.g. line 25). The signal API expects a PROC_REF/TYPE_PROC_REF result, not a bare typepath.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/fever.dm:73-75** [arithmetic-error] — set_body_temp does `M.bodytemperature = min(M.bodytemperature + max((3 * power) * A.stage, (BODYTEMP_HEAT_DAMAGE_LIMIT - 1)))`. `min()` has only one argument (so it is a no-op), and the inner `max(stageValue, ~340K)` always returns ~340K. Net effect: each activation ADDS ~339K to bodytemperature instead of warming by a small amount and capping at the damage limit.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/flesh_eating.dm:67-69** [inverted-condition/wrong-variable] — In Flesheat(), `if(ishuman(M)) return` causes an early return when M IS human, so the wound-creation code below (which casts M to /mob/living/carbon/human and reads H.organs) only ever runs for NON-humans. On a non-human mob the human cast is wrong and `pick(H.organs)` / `O.createwound()` will runtime or do nothing.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/robot.dm:72-120** [broken-switch/type-comparison] — In Replace(), `switch(O)` compares the organ instance O against type-path constants (`if(/obj/item/organ/internal/brain)` etc.). A datum instance never equals a type path, so no case ever matches and the entire `replaceorgans` branch robotizes nothing.
- **code/datums/elements/turf_transparency.dm:48** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In update_multiz(), the second underlay is built as `var/mutable_appearance/plating_underlay = mutable_appearance(...)` but the next line is `plating_underlay = RESET_ALPHA | RESET_COLOR` instead of `plating_underlay.appearance_flags = RESET_ALPHA | RESET_COLOR`. This overwrites the mutable_appearance reference with an integer bitflag, then `our_turf.underlays += plating_underlay` adds that integer to the underlays list.
- **code/datums/ghost_spawn.dm:105** [arithmetic/operator-precedence] — In become_drone(): `if(!chosen_fabricator.drone_progress >= 100)` parses as `(!chosen_fabricator.drone_progress) >= 100`. `!x` is always 0 or 1, which is never >= 100, so this condition is always FALSE and never returns.
- **code/datums/status_effects/debuffs/fire_stacks.dm:73-83** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In fire_handler/on_creation, the merge-collection loop does `merge_effects += merge_effects` (line 77) instead of `merge_effects += merge_effect`. It appends the accumulator list to itself rather than the found merge_effect status-effect instance.
- **code/datums/uplink/announcements.dm:72-78** [runtime/null-deref + inverted condition] — In fake_crew_arrival/get_goods, the block `if(!random_general_record)` is entered precisely when `random_general_record` is null, yet every line inside dereferences `random_general_record.fields[...]` (citizenship, faction, fingerprint, home_system, birthplace, religion). The condition is inverted: it should be `if(random_general_record)` to copy fields *from* the picked record.
- **code/datums/wires/wires.dm:55-59, 452-458** [null-deref / lifecycle] — Destroy() sets `holder = null` (line 56) BEFORE iterating colors and calling detach_assembly() (line 58). detach_assembly() ends with `S.forceMove(holder.drop_location())` (line 457), dereferencing the now-null holder.
- **code/game/antagonist/outsider/raider.dm:99-100, 242-246** [runtime / wrong-type] — raider_guns contains two entries that are themselves lists: `list(/obj/item/gun/projectile/luger,...)` and `list(/obj/item/gun/projectile/deagle,...)`. equip_weapons() does `var/new_gun = pick(raider_guns)` then `new new_gun(T)`. When pick() returns one of those nested lists, `new (a /list)(T)` is invalid.
- **code/game/gamemodes/endgame/supermatter_cascade/universe.dm:133-142** [wrong-type loop / dead code] — PlayerSet() does `for(var/datum/mind/M in GLOB.player_list)`, but GLOB.player_list is a list of /mob (clients attached), not /datum/mind (confirmed via code/_helpers/global_lists.dm). The typed for-loop filters to /datum/mind instances, of which there are none, so the loop body (Weaken, flash_eyes, SSantag_job.clear_antag_roles) never runs.
- **code/game/gamemodes/meteor/meteors.dm:397-401** [copy-paste bug / wrong type path] — `/obj/effect/meteor/flaming/` (note trailing slash) re-opens the BASE flaming meteor type and overrides its name to "flaming meatyore", icon to meaty_flaming, and meteordrop to meatsteak. It was clearly meant to define `/obj/effect/meteor/flaming/meatyore` like all the sibling meaty variants. The subtypes at lines 403/407 reference `/obj/effect/meteor/flaming/meatyore`, which now has no distinct vars and inherits the corrupted base.
- **code/game/gamemodes/nuclear/pinpointer.dm:163-165** [null-deref / wrong-variable] — In advpinpointer toggle_mode 'Item' branch: `var/datum/objective/steal/itemlist` is declared and immediately self-assigned `itemlist = itemlist` (assigns null to itself), then `itemlist.possible_items` is dereferenced for tgui_input_list and again at `locate(itemlist.possible_items[targetitem])`. itemlist is always null.
- **code/game/gamemodes/objective.dm:141-153** [inverted/always-true condition (dead code) + ':' operator misuse] — `/datum/objective/anti_revolution/demote/check_completion()` guards with `ishuman(target)` where `target` is a `/datum/mind`, which is never a human, so the entire ID-checking body is unreachable and the proc always falls through to `return 1`. It should test `ishuman(target.current)`. Line 142 also uses the forbidden `:` operator (`target.current:wear_id`) to reach a subtype member without an istype cast.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/mend_organs.dm:41** [operator-precedence / wrong-condition] — The limb-healing loop uses `if(!O.robotic < ORGAN_ROBOT) continue`. Unary `!` binds tighter than `<`, so this parses as `(!O.robotic) < ORGAN_ROBOT`. `!O.robotic` is 0 or 1, and ORGAN_ROBOT is a non-zero status constant, so the expression is essentially always true, causing `continue` to fire for every external organ.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/modifier/mend_all.dm:10** [copy-paste / wrong-variable] — The `/obj/item/spell/modifier/mend_all` object sets `modifier_type = /datum/modifier/technomancer/mend_life`, but a dedicated `/datum/modifier/technomancer/mend_all` modifier is defined immediately below (lines 13-35) and is never referenced anywhere.
- **code/game/jobs/job/assistant.dm:154-166** [copy-paste/type-collision] — `/datum/job/assistant` is re-opened a second time in the same file (first at line 4 as the real Assistant, again at line 154 as the "Visitor"). The second block overrides the shared type's vars: title = JOB_ALT_VISITOR, supervisors = "nobody! You don't work here", requestable = FALSE, and adds a New() that sets total/spawn_positions from CONFIG_GET(number/limit_visitors). Because DM merges re-opened type definitions (last assignment wins), the single /datum/job/assistant datum ends up being the Visitor, not the Assistant.
- **code/game/machinery/cloning.dm:113-115** [wrong-operator (istype vs ispath) / always-false condition] — growclone() iterates `for(var/modifier_type in BR.genetic_modifiers)` and tests `if(istype(modifier_type, /datum/modifier/no_clone))`. genetic_modifiers stores TYPE PATHS, not instances (per its own definition comment: 'only the type is saved, not an instance'), so istype() on a path is always FALSE.
- **code/game/machinery/computer/supply.dm:467-485** [missing permission re-validation] — The "send_shuttle" tgui_act action (send_away/send_to_station/cancel_shuttle/force_shuttle) never checks `authorization & SUP_SEND_SHUTTLE`, unlike every order action which checks `SUP_ACCEPT_ORDERS`. The base ordering console (/obj/machinery/computer/supplycomp) has authorization = 0 (no SUP_SEND_SHUTTLE) and the UI hides these controls, but a crafted tgui_act call can still launch/recall/force the supply shuttle from an ordering console.
- **code/game/machinery/doors/airlock_electronics.dm:93-95** [missing input re-validation / trusting client value] — The "access" tgui_act action calls `toggle_access(params["access"])` without validating that the supplied access id is within get_available_accesses(usr). get_available_accesses restricts non-engineers to only their own ID's accesses, but that restriction is only used to build the UI list — tgui_act trusts the client-supplied access id directly.
- **code/game/machinery/suit_storage/suit_storage_units.dm:13-54** [copy-paste / wrong-variable bug] — For the engineering, hazmat, mining, mining_alt, medical, emt, security, and riot Suit Storage Unit subtypes, suit_type and helmet_type are swapped: suit_type is set to a /obj/item/clothing/head/helmet/... path and helmet_type is set to a /obj/item/clothing/suit/... path. In suit_storage.dm Initialize(), SUIT = new suit_type(src) and HELMET = new helmet_type(src), so each of these units spawns a helmet object stored in the SUIT slot and a suit object stored in the HELMET slot.
- **code/game/machinery/syndicatebeacon.dm:177-197** [security / unvalidated Topic + null-deref] — /obj/machinery/syndicate_beacon/virgo/Topic() does not call ..() (so the base Topic href/permission validation is skipped), then does var/mob/M = locate(href_list["traitormob"]) with no istype(M) check and dereferences M.mind.tcrystals (no null-safe operator). The base /obj/machinery/syndicate_beacon/Topic at least does istype(M) and uses M.mind?.special_role.
- **code/game/machinery/transportpod.dm:23-26** [off-by-one / out-of-bounds] — process() picks `locNum = rand(0, 7)` and indexes `xc[locNum]` / `yc[locNum]`. The lists `xc` and `yc` each have 8 elements (DM lists are 1-indexed, valid indices 1-8). `rand(0,7)` returns 0 through 7, so when locNum==0 the access `xc[0]`/`yc[0]` throws a runtime out-of-bounds error, and index 8 (the last entry) is never reachable.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/tools/energy_relay.dm:12-14** [wrong-variable/copy-paste + ref leak] — A Destroy() override is defined for the WRONG type inside this file: `/obj/item/mecha_parts/mecha_equipment/repair_droid/Destroy()` (lines 12-14) calls STOP_PROCESSING(SSfastprocess, src). But repair_droid actually processes on SSobj (see repair_droid.dm START/STOP_PROCESSING(SSobj, src)), so this stops the wrong subsystem and never cancels its real SSobj processing. Conversely tesla_energy_relay — the type this file is about, which truly runs on SSfastprocess (Topic START_PROCESSING(SSfastprocess)) — has NO Destroy() at all.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/tools/repair_droid.dm:71-76** [runtime/null-deref] — process() reads AC.integrity (line 74) and HC.integrity (line 76) before the null guards `if(AC)`/`if(HC)` at lines 81/84. AC = chassis.internal_components[MECH_ARMOR] and HC = chassis.internal_components[MECH_HULL] can legitimately be null: code/game/mecha/components/_component.dm:128 sets internal_components[component_type] = null when a component is removed.
- **code/game/mecha/space/shuttle.dm:63-67** [copy-paste / wrong-variable null-deref] — In shuttlecraft/update_icon() the central_paint block guards mask creation with the wrong variable: `if(central_paint)` then `if(!engine_paint_mask)` (should be `if(!central_paint_mask)`) before doing `central_paint_mask.color = central_paint`.
- **code/game/objects/effects/alien/aliens.dm:290-294** [missing return after qdel / null-deref] — /obj/effect/alien/acid/tick(): when `!target`, it calls `qdel(src)` but does not `return`. Execution continues to `ticks += 1`, the visible_message/hearers loops, and the `switch(target_strength - ticks)` block, all of which reference the now-null `src.target`.
- **code/game/objects/effects/countdown.dm:57-64** [runtime/null-deref] — process() calls qdel(src) when attached_to is null/QDELETED but does NOT return; execution falls through to `forceMove(get_turf(attached_to))` and `get_value()` which dereference the now-null/deleted attached_to.
- **code/game/objects/effects/map_effects/effect_emitter.dm:16-18** [resource/ref-leak] — Destroy() is declared on path `/obj/effect/map_effect/interval/effect_emitter/interval/Destroy()` — note the extra `/interval/` segment. The real base type is `/obj/effect/map_effect/interval/effect_emitter`, so this Destroy belongs to a phantom subtype that is never instantiated. The actual effect_emitter and its mapped subtypes (smoke, sparks, steam, etc.) therefore never run QDEL_NULL(effect_system).
- **code/game/objects/effects/step_triggers.dm:311** [inverted/always-true condition] — `if(H.species.name == SPECIES_VOX || SPECIES_ZADDAT)` is parsed as `(H.species.name == SPECIES_VOX) || SPECIES_ZADDAT`. SPECIES_ZADDAT is the non-empty string constant "Zaddat", so the whole condition is ALWAYS TRUE for every species.
- **code/game/objects/items/devices/communicator/cartridge.dm:794, 849** [wrong-type / malformed type path] — Two `new /obj.item/analyzer(src)` instantiations use `/obj.item/analyzer` (a dot instead of a slash) rather than the valid `/obj/item/analyzer`. Line 794 is in /obj/item/commcard/head/ce/Initialize() and line 849 is in /obj/item/commcard/head/captain/Initialize(). The correct type `/obj/item/analyzer` exists and is used everywhere else.
- **code/game/objects/items/devices/holowarrant.dm:45-48** [null-deref] — In attackby(), when a warrant is loaded (active set), the code does `var/obj/item/card/id/I = W.GetIdCard()` then immediately `if(ACCESS_HOS in I.GetAccess())` with no null check. GetIdCard() (defined /atom/movable/proc/GetIdCard) returns null for items that are not / do not contain an ID card.
- **code/game/objects/items/devices/paicard.dm:902-904** [null-deref] — digest_act() does `if(pai.digestable)` with no null check on `pai`. Other lifecycle procs in this file (e.g. ex_act at 382) correctly guard with `if(pai)`. An empty pAI card (no personality) has pai == null.
- **code/game/objects/items/ghost_hunting/weapons.dm:78-92** [null-deref] — In /obj/item/ghost_catcher/afterattack, when the target is an /obj/item/ghost_trap, the code does `var/atom/movable/entity = grabbed_entity.resolve()` without a null check. `grabbed_entity` is only set during an active scan (line 119) and is null otherwise, so clicking a deployed trap while not holding an entity dereferences a null var (`null.resolve()`).
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/capture_crystal.dm:203-206** [infinite recursion / broken control flow] — In update_icon(), the busy branch is written as `spawn(activate_cooldown)` on one line followed by `update_icon()` at the SAME indentation. The spawn body is therefore empty, and update_icon() is called synchronously and unconditionally every time the crystal is on cooldown. Because cooldown_check() is still false during that same call, this recurses immediately and re-enters the same branch.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/material/shards.dm:96** [wrong-args/operator-precedence] — `user.apply_damage(no_glove_d + will_break ? break_damage : 0, BRUTE, active_hand, 0, 0, src.sharp, src.edge, src)` has the same `+`/`?:` precedence bug AND one extra positional argument compared to the gloved branch at line 92. apply_damage's signature is (damage, damagetype, def_zone, blocked, sharp, edge, used_weapon, projectile). The extra `0` shifts every later arg: `sharp` becomes 0, `edge` becomes src.sharp, `used_weapon` becomes src.edge, and `projectile` becomes src.
- **code/game/objects/mail.dm:439** [wrong-variable / broken validation] — Identity verification compares a value to itself: `if(recipient.current.dna.unique_enzymes != recipient.current.dna.unique_enzymes)`. This expression is always false, so the check never triggers.
- **code/game/objects/mob_spawner.dm:159,167** [wrong-variable/ref-leak] — In /obj/structure/mob_spawner/scanner/do_spawn(), spawned mobs/mimics are assigned `L.nest = spawn_turf` (a /turf) and `O.nest = spawn_turf`, instead of `nest = src` as the base /obj/structure/mob_spawner/do_spawn() does. The death-report cleanup in /mob/living/death() only calls get_death_report() when `istype(nest, /obj/structure/mob_spawner)`; since nest is a turf, that branch is never taken.
- **code/game/objects/structures/medical_stand.dm:183-195, 388-391, 438** [runtime/null-deref] — attack_hand()'s "Remove tank" branch removes the tank (tank = null) without closing the valve or checking valve_opened. If the valve was opened first (Toggle valve, which started SSobj processing), valve_opened stays TRUE after the tank is gone. On the next process() tick the `else if (valve_opened)` path runs `tank.remove_air(0.01)` (line 389) and the kill check at line 438 evaluates `tank.distribute_pressure`, both dereferencing a null tank.
- **code/game/objects/structures/reflectors.dm:87-100** [broken state machine / deadlock] — bullet_act() sets bullet_act_in_progress = TRUE at the top, but on the early returns at lines 93 (return ..()) and 95 (return ..()) it never resets the flag to FALSE. It is only cleared at line 96, which is unreachable when those returns fire.
- **code/game/objects/structures/transit_tubes.dm:52-70** [wrong-type/copy-paste bug] — ex_act() is defined on the base type /obj/structure (not /obj/structure/transit_tube_pod as the preceding comment 'When destroyed by explosions, properly handle contents' clearly intends). The path is missing the /transit_tube_pod segment.
- **code/modules/admin/DB ban/ban_panle_ui.dm:116** [runtime/null-deref] — In tgui_act("confirmBan"), after DB_ban_record(...), the code does `if((bantype == BANTYPE_PERMA || bantype == BANTYPE_TEMP) && playermob.client)`. `playermob` is found by scanning GLOB.player_list for an online mob and is left null when the banned player is offline (the common case for ckey/IP/CID bans, explicitly handled by the `if(!playermob)` branch a few lines above). Reading `playermob.client` then dereferences null.
- **code/modules/admin/admin.dm:27-38** [wrong-variable/control-flow] — In /proc/admin_notice(message, rights), the loop `for(var/mob/M in GLOB.mob_list)` does `var/C = M.client` then `if(!C) return`. It `return`s out of the entire proc on the first mob that has no client instead of `continue`-ing to the next mob.
- **code/modules/admin/functions/modify_traits.dm:9-31** [wrong-variable / broken logic] — The "Add" branch builds `availible_traits` with `availible_traits += GLOB.traits_by_type[key]` (line 15). GLOB.traits_by_type[key] is an ASSOCIATIVE list (name -> trait_value, e.g. "TRAIT_NODROP" = TRAIT_NODROP), and `list += assoc_list` copies only the KEYS as flat elements, dropping the associations. tgui_input_list then returns a name string, and line 26 `chosen_trait = availible_traits[chosen_trait]` indexes that flat list by the name, which has no association and returns null. So ADD_TRAIT(D, null, source) is called.
- **code/modules/admin/player_effects.dm:379-381** [copy-paste / inverted logic] — The 'damage_organ' action's 'Damage' effect prompts "Add how much damage?" but then executes `our_organ.damage = max((our_organ.damage - organ_damage), 0)` — identical to the heal formula used by 'repair_organ' at line 432. It SUBTRACTS the entered amount instead of adding it.
- **code/modules/admin/topic.dm:680-692** [null-deref] — The `forcespeech` Topic handler locates `M = locate(href_list["forcespeech"])` and, when `!ismob(M)`, calls `to_chat(usr, ...)` but does NOT `return`. Execution falls through to `tgui_input_text(... key_name(M) ...)` and then `M.say(speech)` on a null/non-mob M.
- **code/modules/admin/view_variables/modify_variables.dm:247-250** [inverted condition] — In the 'DELETE FROM LIST' branch of /client/proc/mod_list, the rejection check is `if (O.vv_edit_var(objectvar, L)) { to_chat(...rejected...); return }`. Every other vv_edit_var call in this file uses `if (O.vv_edit_var(...) == FALSE)` / `if (!O.vv_edit_var(...))` to detect rejection. vv_edit_var returns FALSE on rejection, so this condition is inverted.
- **code/modules/admin/view_variables/topic_basic.dm:125-131** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In the component/element removal loop `for(var/datum/target_to_remove_from as anything in targets_to_remove_from)`, the element branch calls `target._RemoveElement(lst)` using the original single `target` instead of the loop variable `target_to_remove_from`. The component branch directly below correctly uses `target_to_remove_from.GetComponents(path)`.
- **code/modules/assembly/assembly.dm:18** [wrong-variable / behavior-changing regression] — The base assembly control var was renamed to `wires_type` (used by pulsed()/pulse() at lines 36/38/43/45 to decide whether a pulse calls activate() or propagates), but every assembly subtype still sets the OLD name `wires`: igniter.dm:8 (`wires = WIRE_RECEIVE`), proximity.dm:6 (`wires = WIRE_PULSE`), infrared.dm:9 (`wires = WIRE_PULSE`), timer.dm:7 (`wires = WIRE_PULSE`), signaler.dm:7 (`wires = WIRE_RECEIVE | WIRE_PULSE | WIRE_RADIO_PULSE | WIRE_RADIO_RECEIVE`). `wires` is the inherited /atom var (code/game/atom/_atom.dm:57, typed /datum/wires) — assigning an integer to it compiles but is completely disconnected from wires_type. As a result NO subtype's wiring config reaches the base logic; pulsed()/pulse() always see the default `wires_type = WIRE_RECEIVE | WIRE_PULSE`.
- **code/modules/asset_cache/iconforge/universal_icon.dm:96-110** [inverted/always-true condition] — map_colors_inferred() uses OR conditions that are tautologies: `if(num_args <= 20 || num_args >= 16)` is true for every integer, and `else if(num_args <= 12 || num_args >= 9)` is likewise always true. The first branch (map_colors_rgba) is therefore taken for ANY argument count, and the rgb / rgba_hex / rgb_hex branches below are dead code.
- **code/modules/blob2/overmind/types/energized_jelly.dm:27-37** [null-deref] — on_chunk_tick fetches carrier = B.get_carrier() inside the mob loop but never guards against a falsy result. get_carrier() returns FALSE when the chunk is on a turf/area (not held). When carrier is FALSE, line 36 carrier.visible_message(...) and line 37 P.launch_projectile(L, BP_TORSO, carrier) dereference/pass a non-mob.
- **code/modules/casino/board_games/board_table.dm:6-15** [copy-paste bug / wrong-value mapping] — The static possible_games list maps GAME_SWEEPER => /datum/board_game/four_row and GAME_FOUR_ROW => /datum/board_game/vore_sweeper. The key/value pairs for minesweeper and four-in-a-row are swapped relative to every other entry (which map key=>matching type).
- **code/modules/casino/casino.dm:852-854** [wrong-variable] — do_tf path resolves the wearer into local sentient_prize via collar.wearer?.resolve(), but then passes the unresolved weakref collar.wearer (not sentient_prize) to do_item_tf(). do_item_tf expects a mob/living and immediately calls tgui_input_text / tf_into on it.
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/modules.dm:177** [operator-precedence / always-false condition] — check_power_cost is called with `istype(usr,/mob/living/silicon ? 1 : 0)`. The ternary `/mob/living/silicon ? 1 : 0` is evaluated as istype's second argument: a type path is always truthy, so it resolves to `istype(usr, 1)`, which is always FALSE because `1` is not a valid type. The intended expression was `istype(usr,/mob/living/silicon) ? 1 : 0`.
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/specific/passive_protection.dm:263** [wrong-variable] — In faraday_shield/deactivate(), the wearer's siemens is restored with `holder.siemens_coefficient = siemens_coefficient`. `siemens_coefficient` here is the rig_module's own var (not set anywhere on this module, so ~0), not the saved `stored_siemens_coefficient`. The intended restore value was captured in stored_siemens_coefficient during activate().
- **code/modules/economy/price_list.dm:983-984** [infinite recursion / wrong-call] — /obj/item/pizzabox/get_item_cost() is `return get_item_cost(pizza)`. get_item_cost is defined as /atom/movable/proc/get_item_cost() taking no arguments, and the unqualified call resolves to src.get_item_cost(pizza) — i.e. the pizzabox calls its own override again. This recurses infinitely on itself (the `pizza` arg is ignored by the no-param proc). The clear intent was to delegate to the contained pizza's price (pizza.get_item_cost()).
- **code/modules/economy/vending.dm:568-583** [broken state machine / soft-lock] — In vend(), vend_ready is set FALSE at line 565, then for a CAT_COIN product with no coin inserted the proc returns early (lines 569-571) without restoring vend_ready = TRUE (the non-coin/string branches that fall through to the rest of vend do reset it elsewhere, but this early return does not). Coin-category products are typically priced 0, so tgui_act's `R.price <= 0` branch calls vend(R) directly; if the user vends such an item without a coin, the machine returns here.
- **code/modules/events/camera_damage.dm:33** [runtime/infinite-recursion] — acquire_random_camera() recurses with `acquire_random_camera(remaining_attempts--)`. The post-decrement evaluates to the CURRENT value before decrementing, and the decrement only mutates the local (passed by value), so the recursive call always receives the same `remaining_attempts`.
- **code/modules/events/money_hacker.dm:36** [logic/always-false condition] — `if(affected_account && !affected_account)` is a contradiction (a value AND its own negation), so it is ALWAYS false. The entire 'hacker wins' branch (lines 37-57: deducting 80% of the account's money, writing the taunting transaction log) is dead/unreachable code, and the event always falls through to the 'crew wins' else branch.
- **code/modules/food/kitchen/cooking_machines/_appliance.dm:530-532** [wrong-variable / runtime] — In combination_cook(), the filling-color blending math references an undefined variable `y`: `var/t = buffer.total_volume + S.reagents.total_volume` then `t = buffer.total_volume / y`. There is no `y` declared anywhere in the proc or type; the intended divisor was almost certainly the `t` computed on the prior line (the combined volume).
- **code/modules/gamemaster/event2/events/security/security_advisement.dm:10** [inverted/always-true condition] — In /datum/event2/meta/security_screening/get_weight(), the guard reads `if(!sec < 2) return 0`. Due to operator precedence `!sec` is evaluated first (yielding 0 or 1), and both 0 and 1 are `< 2`, so the whole condition is ALWAYS TRUE.
- **code/modules/holomap/mapper.dm:103** [wrong-variable bug] — In /obj/item/mapping_unit/Initialize the 'bad' fallback map is built into a local `var/icon/canvas` (cropped and DrawBox'd to a solid color), but then `tmp.icon = icon` assigns the object's global `icon` var (the device sprite 'icons/obj/device.dmi') instead of `canvas`. The freshly-drawn `canvas` is never used.
- **code/modules/hydroponics/seed_storage.dm:378-383** [broken loop / dangling ref] — In tgui_act() the "purge" branch runs `qdel(O); piles -= N; qdel(N)` *inside* the `for(var/obj/O in N.seeds)` loop. The intent is to qdel every seed in the pile and then drop the pile, but the pile removal and `qdel(N)` are executed on every iteration. After the first iteration N is already qdel'd while the loop continues to read `N.seeds`. Additionally, for a contraband pile (which lives in `piles_contra`, not `piles`), `piles -= N` removes nothing, so the qdel'd pile remains referenced in `piles_contra`.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/subtypes/input.dm:254** [`:` operator on non-narrowed type (runtime risk)] — In examiner/do_work(), H is typed `/atom/movable`. After the `H in view(T)` check, `set_pin_data(IC_OUTPUT, 8, H:stat == DEAD ? FALSE : TRUE)` uses the `:` operator to read `stat`, a /mob var, without an istype/ismob guard. The earlier `ismob(H)` guard at line 239 only protects line 239, not line 254.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/subtypes/input.dm:254** [`:` operator on non-narrowed type (runtime risk)] — In examiner/do_work(), H is typed `/atom/movable`. After the `H in view(T)` check, `set_pin_data(IC_OUTPUT, 8, H:stat == DEAD ? FALSE : TRUE)` uses the `:` operator to read `stat`, a /mob var, without an istype/ismob guard. The ismob(H) guard at line 239 only protects line 239, not line 254.
- **code/modules/maps/reader.dm:249** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In /datum/parsed_map/New(), the coordinate-bounds accumulation writes `bounds[MAP_MINZ] = min(bounds[MAP_MINZ], curr_y)`. It uses curr_y (the y coordinate) to populate the MIN-Z slot. The min-Z bound should be derived from curr_z, and curr_y has no business being written to MAP_MINZ (MAP_MINY is correctly set later at line 268 from gridSet.ycrd). MAP_MINZ is therefore never populated with the real minimum z; it instead holds the minimum y of the map (lines 248-250 set MINX from curr_x, MINZ from curr_y, MAXZ from curr_z).
- **code/modules/medical/conditions/_condition.dm:463-464** [infinite-loop / logic error] — roll_symptoms() trims an over-sized symptom set with `while(length(picked) > max_symptoms) picked.Cut(rand(1, length(picked)), rand(1, length(picked)) + 1)`. The start and end of the Cut are computed from two INDEPENDENT rand() calls. When the second rand() yields a value such that the end index is <= the start index (e.g. Cut(5, 2)), BYOND's list.Cut removes nothing. Because `picked`'s length is then unchanged, the `while` loop condition never becomes false.
- **code/modules/mob/_modifiers/modifiers.dm:136-147** [wrong-argument / broken logic] — In add_modifier(), the duplicate-detection loop uses `if(ispath(modifier_type, M))` where M is a /datum/modifier INSTANCE, not a type path. ispath()'s second argument must be a type path; passing an instance makes the check always fail. The correct pattern (used on lines 179 and 186, and in machinery.dm) is `ispath(modifier_type, M.type)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/bot/mulebot.dm:294-301** [broken state machine / stuck flag] — In /mob/living/bot/mulebot/proc/load(), busy is set to 1 (line 294), then after sleep(2) the proc does `if(C.loc != loc) return` (lines 298-299) without resetting busy to 0. The normal success path and all other early returns clear busy, but this mid-proc return leaves busy = 1 permanently.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/human.dm:211, 223** [inverted/always-false condition (operator precedence)] — In ex_act(), `if (!get_ear_protection() >= 2)` parses as `(!get_ear_protection()) >= 2`. The `!` operator binds tighter than `>=`, so the left side is always 0 or 1 and can never be >= 2; the condition is therefore always FALSE.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/human_defense.dm:17** [wrong-variable/null-deref] — bullet_act() calls get_organ() with no argument instead of get_organ(def_zone), so it always fetches BP_TORSO regardless of where the projectile hit.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/species/species.dm:811-818** [always-true condition] — In produceCopy(), the guard `if(selects_bodytype == SELECTS_BODYTYPE_CUSTOM || SELECTS_BODYTYPE_ZORREN)` is always true. SELECTS_BODYTYPE_ZORREN is `#define`d to 3 (a truthy constant), so the `|| SELECTS_BODYTYPE_ZORREN` term forces the whole condition to TRUE regardless of `selects_bodytype`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/pai/pai_folding.dm:148** [logic/precedence bug] — is_folding_unsafe() has a misplaced parenthesis: `istype(check_location, /obj/item/storage/vore_egg || istype(check_location, /obj/item/pda))`. The `||` and inner istype are evaluated as the *type argument* of the outer istype. `/obj/item/storage/vore_egg` is a truthy type path so the `||` short-circuits to that path, collapsing the expression to `istype(check_location, /obj/item/storage/vore_egg)` and the `/obj/item/pda` test is never performed.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_modules/station.dm:190-202** [logic/wrong-variable bug] — In add_item_with_reagents(), the local var `found` is declared FALSE and tested by `if(!found)` at the end, but it is NEVER set to TRUE on the matching path. When a matching synth IS found, the code does `item_synths += synth; break` without setting `found = TRUE`. Therefore `if(!found)` is always true after the inner loop, so a brand-new matter_synth is always created and added even when an existing one already matched (and item_synths then gets both the matched synth AND a new duplicate).
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_ui_module.dm:150-156, 178-179, 193** [null-deref] — The `confirm` tgui_act case calls R.apply_module(sprite_datum, selected_module) and R.transform_module() without verifying that `sprite_datum` is non-null. apply_module() dereferences `sprite_datum.name` (line 179, for non-drones) and transform_module() calls `sprite_datum.do_equipment_glamour(module)` (line 193). A client can send the `confirm` action having only picked a module (pick_module) but never a sprite (pick_icon), leaving sprite_datum null.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/defense.dm:71, 75, 162, 200, 229, 233** [wrong-variable / duplicate named argument] — Every apply_damage() call in this file passes the named argument `blocked` twice, e.g. `apply_damage(damage = real_damage, damagetype = hit_dam_type, def_zone = null, blocked = armor, blocked = resistance, ...)`. The proc signature is `/mob/living/proc/apply_damage(damage, damagetype, def_zone, blocked, sharp, edge, used_weapon, projectile)` — there is only ONE `blocked` parameter and no `resistance` parameter. In DM, when the same named argument is supplied twice the later value wins, so `blocked = armor` is silently overwritten by `blocked = resistance`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/defense.dm:71, 75, 162, 200, 229, 233** [wrong-variable / duplicate named argument] — Every apply_damage() call in this file passes the named argument `blocked` twice, e.g. `apply_damage(damage = real_damage, damagetype = hit_dam_type, def_zone = null, blocked = armor, blocked = resistance, ...)`. The proc signature is `/mob/living/proc/apply_damage(damage, damagetype, def_zone, blocked, sharp, edge, used_weapon, projectile)` — there is only ONE `blocked` parameter and no `resistance`/`soaked` parameter. In DM, when the same named argument is supplied twice the later value wins, so `blocked = armor` is silently overwritten by `blocked = resistance`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/borer/borer.dm:97-103** [lifecycle / Destroy ordering] — Destroy() calls `. = ..()` FIRST (line 98) and only afterward runs motiontracker_unsubscribe(), QDEL_NULL(ghost_check), detatch() and leave_host(). Cleanup must occur before the parent Destroy() runs.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/borer/borer_powers.dm:32-33** [wrong-variable / discarded return] — In knockout_victim(), when choices.len > 1 the result of tgui_input_list(...) is not assigned to anything: `tgui_input_list(src, "Who do you wish to dominate?", ...)`. attack_target was set to choices[1] earlier and is never updated, so the player's selection is ignored.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/giant_spider/nurse.dm:200-216** [broken state machine / stuck flag] — lay_eggs() sets `laying_eggs = TRUE` (line 200) but on the do_after-failure return (line 202-205) and the spamclick-protection return (line 208-209) it returns FALSE WITHOUT resetting laying_eggs back to FALSE. Only the success path (line 215) clears it. The spamclick-protection path (line 208) also fails to reset ai_brain.busy (set TRUE at 198).
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/sif/leech.dm:259-267** [null-deref] — In do_infest(), when the target's infest_target organ is missing/stump/robotic, the code calls to_chat() but does NOT return. Execution falls through to line 263 `var/list/covering_clothing = E.get_covering_clothing()`, dereferencing E which is null in exactly that branch.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/sif/leech.dm:312-321** [null-deref] — leave_host() unconditionally does `host_bodypart.implants -= src` and `host_bodypart = null`. But host_bodypart is only ever assigned for ishuman() hosts (do_infest line 291-294), while `host` is set for any /mob/living/carbon (line 285). For a non-human carbon host, host gets set but host_bodypart stays null.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/sif/leech.dm:356-367** [null-deref] — In poison_inject(), both the `if(!istype(H) || H.isSynthetic())` guard (line 356) and the `if(!E || E.is_stump() || ...)` guard (line 360) print a message but do NOT return. Line 359 reads `H.organs_by_name[infest_target]` on a possibly non-human H, and line 363 calls `E.get_covering_clothing()` on a possibly-null E.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/humanoid/possessed.dm:85-89** [missing ..() in lifecycle override] — /mob/living/simple_mob/humanoid/possessed/Life() overrides the base /mob/living/simple_mob/Life() (code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/life.dm) but never calls ..(). The override only handles its idle breathing sound and returns; it does not run updatehealth(), the stun/weaken/paralyse/sleep handlers, handle_special(), do_healing(), or the dead-check.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/mechanical/hivebot/boss.dm:59** [wrong-argument / copy-paste bug] — In launch_microsingularity(), `G.throw_at(G.throw_range, G.throw_speed, src)` passes the grenade's numeric throw_range as the first argument, which throw_at(atom/target, range, speed, mob/thrower) expects to be the target atom. The sibling proc launch_rockets() (line 70) correctly calls `G.throw_at(target, G.throw_range, G.throw_speed, src)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/mechanical/hivebot/orginalAlts.dm:45-49** [wrong-variable / type vs instance] — /mob/living/simple_mob/mechanical/hivebot/tyr/meteor/bullet_act() does `projectiletype = Proj`, assigning the incoming projectile *instance* to projectiletype, which is meant to hold a type path. combat.dm line 142 later does `new projectiletype(src.loc)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/metroid/metConsumption.dm:9** [wrong-operator / broken state machine] — Same bug as the xenobio slime: `power_charge = min(power_charge++, 10)`. The post-increment side effect is overwritten by the assignment of `min(old_value, 10)`, so power_charge never increases while below 10.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/metroid/metJuvenile.dm:51-55** [behavior-changing dead code / empty spawn / broken state machine] — In the queen branch of `evolve()`, `spawn(50)` has an empty body. The lines that follow (`new /obj/effect/metroid/egg(...)`, `adjust_nutrition(-500)`, `paralysis = 0`, `return`) are at the same indentation, so they execute immediately. The egg is laid instantly and `paralysis = 7998` set two lines above is reset to 0 in the same tick.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/occult/unknown.dm:239-256** [broken state machine / behavior-changing dead code] — `do_special_attack()` reads `next_special_attack` but it is never reassigned anywhere in the codebase (only declared `= GA_ADS` and read). So the glitch boss always picks GA_ADS (the fake-ad-popup attack). The `recently_used_attack` var and `all_special_attacks` list are vestigial and unused for selection.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/slime/xenobio/consumption.dm:9** [wrong-operator / broken state machine] — `power_charge = min(power_charge++, 10)` uses a post-increment whose side effect is immediately discarded. `power_charge++` evaluates to the OLD value, increments power_charge, then `min(old, 10)` is assigned back over the increment. For any value below 10 the net result is that power_charge is reassigned its old value and never grows.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/swoopie.dm:245-249** [runtime/null-deref] — In ClickOn(), the disposal branch declares `var/obj/machinery/disposal/D` but never assigns it (it should be `= A`). The very next line dereferences `D.flushing`, and later `\the [D]` is used. `D` is always null here, so as soon as a swoopie uses GRAB intent on a disposal bin the proc runtimes on `D.flushing`.
- **code/modules/mob/mob_grab.dm:400-405** [runtime / null-deref] — In /obj/item/grab/Destroy(), `animate(affecting, ...)` and especially `affecting.reset_plane_and_layer()` are called unconditionally BEFORE the `if(affecting)` guard on the next line. The code itself anticipates affecting may be null (it guards with `if(affecting)`), but reset_plane_and_layer() is dereferenced first.
- **code/modules/mob/mob_helpers.dm:594-597** [wrong-variable / broken safety check] — /mob/living/carbon/human/assess_perp does `var/threatcount = ..()` then `if(. == SAFE_PERP) return SAFE_PERP`. It checks `.` (the implicit return var, which is still null at that point) instead of `threatcount`. Since SAFE_PERP is -50, `null == -50` is always FALSE, so the short-circuit never fires.
- **code/modules/modular_computers/computers/modular_computer/damage.dm:8-17** [wrong qdel usage / resource leak] — break_apart() ends with a bare qdel() call (no argument). qdel() requires a target as its first parameter; /proc/qdel(datum/to_delete, force) treats a null target as a no-op (returns at the isnull(to_delete) check in garbage.dm line 352-353). The computer is therefore never deleted after it 'breaks apart'.
- **code/modules/modular_computers/computers/modular_computer/damage.dm:8-17** [wrong qdel usage / resource leak] — break_apart() ends with a bare qdel() call (no argument). qdel() requires a target as its first parameter; /proc/qdel(datum/to_delete, force) treats a null target as a no-op (returns at the isnull(to_delete) check in garbage.dm line 352-353). The computer is therefore never deleted after it 'breaks apart'.
- **code/modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/generic/ntnrc_client.dm:156, 160-161** [off-by-one / runtime null/index error] — `last_message = channel.messages[channel.messages.len - 1]` (and the identical expression on line 161) indexes the *second-to-last* element. DM lists are 1-indexed, so the last element is `messages[messages.len]`. When a channel has exactly one message (len==1) this evaluates `messages[0]`, which is an out-of-bounds access and throws a runtime. When len>=2 it silently returns the wrong (previous) message.
- **code/modules/multiz/movement.dm:619-638** [copy-paste/wrong-variable] — /mob/living/carbon/human/CanParachute() checks s_store, belt, wear_suit, w_uniform for isParachute() but every branch calls `back.handleParachute()` instead of the slot actually being checked (e.g. the s_store branch should call `s_store.handleParachute()`). Only the `back` branch is correct.
- **code/modules/multiz/movement.dm:823-825** [inverted-condition] — In /turf/simulated/proc/climb_wall, after the tgui_alert for climbing without tools (options "Bring it!" / "Stay grounded"), the guard is `if(sure || sure == "Stay grounded") return`. Because `sure` is truthy for BOTH choices, choosing "Bring it!" also returns/cancels. The analogous climb_down proc (line 949) correctly uses `if(!sure || sure == "Stay grounded") return`.
- **code/modules/nifsoft/nif.dm:699-708** [wrong-variable/null-deref] — In /obj/item/nif/attack (promethean torso install), `var/obj/item/organ/external/eo = T.get_organ(BP_TORSO)` is fetched, but the guard checks the wrong variable: `if(!T)` (T is the already-validated target mob, never null here) instead of `if(!eo)`. The 'regrow their torso first' abort therefore never fires.
- **code/modules/organs/misc.dm:33-39** [null-deref/lifecycle] — `/obj/item/organ/internal/borer/removed` calls `..()` first (the base `removed()` sets `owner = null` at its end), then immediately dereferences `owner.has_brain_worms()` and reads `owner.ckey`. After `..()` owner is always null here.
- **code/modules/organs/subtypes/diona.dm:133-134** [null-deref/inverted-condition] — `if(!istype(H) || !H.organs || !H.organs.len) H.death()` — the `||` short-circuits so the branch is entered precisely when `!istype(H)` is true (H is null or not a human), and it then calls `H.death()` on that null/invalid H. H is captured from `owner` before `..()`; if the organ had no owner (e.g. spawned/removed while not in a body), H is null and this runtimes.
- **code/modules/organs/subtypes/diona.dm:149-150** [null-deref/inverted-condition] — Same defect as above in `/obj/item/organ/internal/diona/removed`: `if(!istype(H) || !H.organs || !H.organs.len) H.death()` dereferences H when `!istype(H)` is true.
- **code/modules/organs/subtypes/diona.dm:233-234** [null-deref/inverted-condition] — Same defect in `/obj/item/organ/internal/brain/cephalon/removed`: `if(!istype(H) || !H.organs || !H.organs.len) H.death()` calls death() on H when H is null/non-human.
- **code/modules/organs/subtypes/machine.dm:73-95** [null-deref/ordering] — `/obj/item/organ/internal/mmi_holder/update_from_mmi()` uses `owner.real_name` at lines 79-81 inside the `if(!stored_mmi.brainmob)` block, but the `if(!owner) return` guard is not until line 83 (after that block). If brainmob is missing and owner is null, line 79 dereferences a null owner.
- **code/modules/overmap/overmap_shuttle.dm:28-35** [logic/state-machine] — In fuel_check(), when try_consume_fuel() returns 0 (insufficient fuel) the code enters the if-block and loops over mobs in shuttle areas, but the `return 0` (failure) is placed INSIDE the inner `for(var/mob/living/M in A)` loop. If no living mob is present in any shuttle area, neither loop body executes and control falls through to `return 1` (success).
- **code/modules/paperwork/filingcabinet.dm:133-149** [runtime/null-deref] — In /obj/structure/filingcabinet/security/populate(), the inner search may leave `S` (the matched security record) null when a general record `G` has no corresponding security record. Unlike the medical variant (which guards with `if(M)` at line 174), the security version unconditionally dereferences `S.fields[...]` at lines 142+ and `while(S.fields["com_[counter]"])` at line 144.
- **code/modules/paperwork/photography.dm:217** [off-by-one / null index runtime] — In /obj/item/camera/get_icon the loop `for(var/i; i <= sorted.len; i++)` declares `i` without initializing it (i is null≈0), so the first iteration indexes `sorted[0]`/`sorted[null]`.
- **code/modules/persistence/effects/trash.dm:8-13** [wrong-variable / always-false condition] — In /datum/persistent/filth/trash/CheckTurfContents the spitwad/spitgum guard uses `istype(T, /obj/item/trash/spitwad)` / `istype(T, /obj/item/trash/spitgum)` where `T` is the turf, not the iterated `trash` item.
- **code/modules/persistence/paintings.dm:24-30** [missing return / inverted result] — /datum/persistent/paintings/CheckTokenSanity only `return FALSE` when the PNG is missing; on the success path (file exists) it falls through and returns null. In Initialize, `if(!CheckTokenSanity(token)) tokens -= token` treats null as failure.
- **code/modules/power/fusion/core/core_field.dm:663-678 (and 680-695)** [null-deref / wrong qdel usage] — MagneticQuench() calls `Destroy()` directly (line 676) and then `owned_core.Shutdown()` (line 677). The same pattern exists in BluespaceQuenchEvent() (Destroy() at 693, owned_core.Shutdown() at 694). Destroy() (line 521-523) sets `owned_core = null`, so the subsequent `owned_core.Shutdown()` dereferences a null var. Additionally, MRC()/QuantumFluxCascade()/MagneticQuench()/BluespaceQuenchEvent() invoke `Destroy()` directly instead of `qdel(src)`, leaving the object half-torn-down rather than properly garbage-collected.
- **code/modules/power/port_gen.dm:81-91** [wrong-operator / behavior bug] — In /obj/machinery/power/port_gen/emp_act the BROKEN bit is set with `stat &= BROKEN` (lines 81, 84, 87, 90) instead of `stat |= BROKEN`. `stat &= BROKEN` is `stat = stat & BROKEN`, which CLEARS every stat bit except BROKEN (and only keeps BROKEN if it was already set), rather than turning the BROKEN bit on. Line 93 correctly uses `stat |= EMPED`, confirming the intended pattern.
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile.dm:420-423** [runtime/null-deref] — In preparePixelProjectile(), the guard `if(targloc || !params)` then dereferences `targloc.y`/`targloc.x`. When `targloc` is null AND `params` is null, the condition is true (via `!params`) yet `targloc.y - curloc.y` dereferences a null turf, runtiming instead of falling through to the graceful `else` at line 435 that qdels the projectile.
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile/hook.dm:126** [invalid syntax / null-or-wrong-type] — `var/target_mob = pick(/mob/living in target.contents)` is not a valid pick-with-filter. DM `pick()` takes a list; the `TYPE in LIST` form is a boolean membership test, not a filtered selection. The expression evaluates the membership test and passes its result (0/1) to pick, so target_mob is a number (or null), never a mob.
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile/trace.dm:39-43** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In /obj/item/projectile/test/Bump, when the bumped atom A is a turf, the two inner loops iterate over objects (var/obj/O) and mobs (var/mob/living/M) inside the turf but add the TURF `A` to the hit list each time (`hit |= A`) instead of the iterated `O` / `M`. The actual contents are never recorded; only the turf is.
- **code/modules/reagents/machinery/chem_master.dm:425-427** [null-deref / trusting client-supplied value] — In the 'print' tgui_act branch, `from_beaker = text2num(params["beaker"]) || FALSE` then `reagent_list = from_beaker ? beaker.reagents.reagent_list : reagents.reagent_list`. When the client sends `beaker` truthy but no beaker container is loaded (`beaker == null`), `beaker.reagents.reagent_list` dereferences null. The `if(. || !beaker) return` guard that would catch a missing beaker is at line 453, AFTER this branch executes. The same pattern exists in the 'analyze' modal at line 151 (`from_beaker ? beaker.reagents.reagent_list : reagents.reagent_list`).
- **code/modules/reagents/reagents/food_drinks.dm:6000-6003** [wrong-signature/runtime] — /datum/reagent/drink/coffee/nukie/mega/nega/affect_ingest is declared as `affect_ingest(mob/living/carbon/human/M)` — only ONE parameter — while the base proc (and the actual caller in _reagents.dm:195) is `affect_ingest(mob/living/carbon/M, alien, removed)` invoked with three args. The override silently drops `alien` and `removed`, and its `..()` call forwards only `M` to the parent chain (drink -> ...), so the parent runs with `removed = null` and `alien = null`.
- **code/modules/reagents/reagents/virology.dm:20** [wrong-variable/typo] — The mix_data override is defined on /datum/reagent/vaccines (plural) instead of /datum/reagent/vaccine (singular). The actual reagent type defined in this file (line 1) is /datum/reagent/vaccine. Grep confirms /datum/reagent/vaccines appears nowhere else in the codebase, so this override is attached to a phantom type that is never instantiated.
- **code/modules/research/tg/machinery/destructive_analyzer.dm:300-301** [runtime/null-deref] — In destroy_item_individual(), get_silo_material_container_datum(FALSE) is called and its result assigned to `materials`, then `materials.insert_item(thing, decon_mod, src, FALSE)` is dereferenced unconditionally. get_silo_material_container_datum() returns null when rmat.mat_container is null (no silo link) or when rmat.on_hold() is TRUE (lines 160-170). The MouseDrop_T recycler path guards this exact return with `if(!materials) return FALSE` (lines 138-140), but this deconstruction path has no null check.
- **code/modules/resleeving/autoresleever.dm:94-101** [runtime/null-deref] — chosen_species is only assigned inside `if(pref_species)` (lines 96-97), but line 99 unconditionally reads `chosen_species.spawn_flags`. The comment on line 96 ("In case we somehow don't have a species set here") acknowledges pref_species can be null, yet the code then dereferences chosen_species regardless.
- **code/modules/samples/samples.dm:174-178** [off-by-one] — On crushing a sample: `var/i = rand(min_ore,max_ore)` then `while(i>1){ ...new ore...; i-- }`. The loop condition `i>1` causes it to spawn only `i-1` ores. For /obj/item/research_sample/bluespace (min_ore=1, max_ore=1) i=1, so `while(1>1)` is immediately false and ZERO ore is produced — crushing a bluespace anomaly yields nothing. All other tiers also yield one fewer ore than intended.
- **code/modules/shieldgen/emergency_shield.dm:41** [runtime/null-deref] — /obj/machinery/shield/Destroy() unconditionally calls `our_owner.resolve()`, but `our_owner` is null for any shield not created by a shieldgen. The malfai subtype (/obj/machinery/shield/malfai, spawned at code/game/gamemodes/malfunction/newmalf_ability_trees/tree_manipulation.dm:126) and any admin/bus-spawned /obj/machinery/shield never set our_owner.
- **code/modules/shuttles/shuttles_web.dm:382-390** [inverted-condition] — In check_docking(), the force-launch confirmation dialog offers list("Force Launch", "Cancel"), but the result is tested with `if(choice || choice == "Cancel") return 0`. Any non-empty answer (including the user actually clicking "Force Launch") makes `choice` truthy, so the proc always returns 0. The condition should be `if(!choice || choice == "Cancel") return 0`.
- **code/modules/spells/spell_code.dm:187-192** [null-deref] — In cast_check(), when get_turf(user) returns null the code only sends a warning (`to_chat(user, ...)`) but does not return. Execution then reaches `if(spell_flags & Z2NOCAST && (user_turf.z in using_map.admin_levels))`, dereferencing user_turf.z on a null value.
- **code/modules/surgery/bones.dm:8-20** [null-deref] — /datum/surgery_step/bones/can_use checks `if(!ishuman(target)) return FALSE` then immediately does `var/obj/item/organ/external/affected = target.get_organ(target_zone)` and dereferences it on the next line `if(!(affected.status & ORGAN_BROKEN))` with no null check. get_organ() returns null for a missing/amputated limb zone. Every other surgery base can_use in this module (generic.dm, face.dm, dehusk.dm, encased.dm, repairflesh, internal, robotics) explicitly guards `if(affected == null) return 0` / `if(!affected) return 0`, confirming null is a real possibility.
- **code/modules/surgery/neck.dm:29** [logic-error/operator-precedence] — In /datum/surgery_step/brainstem/can_use the guard reads `!target.op_stage.brainstem == 5`. DM's `!` binds tighter than `==`, so this parses as `(!target.op_stage.brainstem) == 5`. The `!` operator yields 0 or 1, which can never equal 5, so the whole sub-expression is ALWAYS FALSE. The intended check was `target.op_stage.brainstem != 5`.
- **code/modules/tgui/modules/teleporter.dm:50-54** [inverted-condition/dead-code] — In the tracking-implant branch of the select_target action, the guard is inverted: `var/turf/T = get_turf(M)` followed by `if(T) continue`. When the implanted mob is on a turf (the normal case) the loop skips it; when T is null it falls through to `if(!(T in using_map.station_levels)) continue` (null in list -> continue). The result is that tracking implants are NEVER added to the lock-in list. Additionally `T in using_map.station_levels` compares a turf against a z-level list and should be `T.z in ...`.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/belly_obj.dm:1642-1646** [wrong-variable / variable shadowing] — get_belly_name(original) declares a local `var/display_name = ""` that shadows the instance var /obj/belly/var/display_name. The subsequent `return display_name ? display_name : name` always evaluates the local (always empty string, falsy), so it unconditionally returns `name` and the belly's configured display_name is never used.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/inbelly_spawn.dm:25-30** [behavior-changing missing return] — When the target belly is set to DM_DIGEST, the code prompts the predator 'Are you sure you want to spawn prey there?'. If the answer is not 'Yes' it sends 'Inbelly spawn cancelled.' messages but does NOT `return` — execution falls through and the spawn continues anyway.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/panel_databackend/vorepanel_set_attribute.dm:329-332** [inverted/always-true condition] — The message-reset confirmation guard is `if(!confirm == "DELETE")`. Due to operator precedence `!` binds tighter than `==`, so this parses as `(!confirm) == "DELETE"`. `confirm` is a non-empty string ("Cancel" or "DELETE"), so `!confirm` is 0, and `0 == "DELETE"` is always FALSE. The `return FALSE` is therefore never taken.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/soulcatcher_mob.dm:9-16** [null-deref] — In /mob/living/carbon/brain/caught_soul/vore/Destroy(), `gem` is set to null at line 12 (inside the `if(gem)` block), but then line 15 calls `gem.notify_holder("[name] ended SR projection.")` after gem is already null. Any captured soul that still has an active eyeobj when deleted will runtime null-dereference.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/effect_master.dm:248-266** [copy-paste/wrong-initial-value] — In on_bullet(), `var/triggered = TRUE` is initialized to TRUE (the sibling on_exact() correctly uses FALSE). The loop only re-sets it to TRUE when an effect actually matches, but since it already starts TRUE, the final `if(triggered) return COMPONENT_CANCEL_ATTACK_CHAIN` always fires.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/effects/poltergeist.dm:29 and 46** [wrong-argument-order] — DoEffectAura() and DoEffectPulse() call oview(get_turf(holder), effectrange) — arguments reversed. BYOND's signature is oview(Dist, Center); here Dist is a turf and Center is an integer. Every other artifact effect in this module (resurrect.dm, vampire.dm, electric_field.dm) correctly uses oview(effectrange, get_turf(holder)).
- **code/modules/xenobio/items/extracts.dm:943-945** [wrong-variable] — green_radpulse/on_reaction() does START_PROCESSING(SSobj, src) where src is the singleton reaction decl (/datum/decl/chemical_reaction/instant/slime/green_radpulse), not the slime extract. The intended target is holder.my_atom (the /obj/item/slime_extract/green), as evidenced by the immediately preceding istype(holder.my_atom, /obj/item/slime_extract/green) check.
- **maps/common/common_shuttles_crew.dm:106-118** [dead/duplicate code (override shadowing)] — /obj/machinery/status_display/shuttle_display defines Initialize(mapload) twice: the first body (lines 106-108) does `GLOB.shuttdisp_list |= src`, the second body (lines 115-118) calls get_my_shuttle()/update(). In DM the last-compiled definition of a proc on a type fully replaces the earlier one, so the first Initialize (the one that registers the display into GLOB.shuttdisp_list) never runs.
- **maps/common_submaps/overmap/planets/kara/kara.dm:46-67** [duplicate-definition / dead code] — /obj/effect/overmap/visitable/planet/kara has TWO Initialize(mapload) overrides defined in the same file: one at lines 46-51 (which resolves the aerostat z-level via GLOB.map_templates_loaded[Z_NAME_KARA_AEROSTAT_CH] and sets map_z = list(Z_NAME_KARA_AEROSTAT_CH)), and a second at lines 59-67 (which only sets up the atmosphere mix and nulls docking_codes). DM cannot have two bodies for the same proc on the same type; the last-compiled one wins and the first is silently discarded.

## CONFIRMED — MEDIUM
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/pipeturbine.dm:39-50** [resource/memory - dangling ref] — /obj/machinery/atmospherics/pipeturbine/Destroy() qdels network1/network2 and nulls node1/node2, but never nulls network1/network2. The binary base Destroy nulls them, but pipeturbine overrides Destroy and omits this. (_real: pipeturbine defines its own network1/2 (18-19) and Destroy qdels without nulling; dangling refs remain post-qdel._)
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/pipeturbine.dm:39-50** [resource/memory - dangling ref] — /obj/machinery/atmospherics/pipeturbine/Destroy() qdels network1/network2 and nulls node1/node2, but never nulls network1/network2. The binary base Destroy nulls them, but pipeturbine overrides Destroy and omits this. (_real: pipeturbine/Destroy() (own type, not binary) qdels network1/2 (lines 43,46) but only nulls node1/node2 (48-49); network1/2 left as dangling refs. Reasoning misattributes base but defect exists._)
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/pipes/cap.dm:138-146** [copy-paste bug / duplicate type definition] — In the 'hidden' endcap section the type is declared as `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/pipe/cap/visible/fuel` instead of `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/pipe/cap/hidden/fuel`. This is a duplicate of the earlier visible/fuel type (lines 93-101). The hidden scrubbers/supply/aux variants directly above and below it (lines 118-156) all correctly use the `hidden` path; only the fuel one says `visible`. (_real: cap.dm:138 declares cap/visible/fuel (dup of line 93) where siblings use hidden; reopens visible/fuel overwriting icon_state to 'cap-f-fuel', and no hidden/fuel type exists._)
- **code/__defines/rust_g.dm:433-439** [wrong-variable / error-handling] — In /proc/rustg_sound_length, after `var/ret = RUSTG_CALL(RUST_G, "sound_len")(file_path)` and `var/as_num = text2num(ret)`, the error guard checks `if(isnull(ret))` instead of `if(isnull(as_num))`. `ret` is the raw string returned by call_ext and is essentially never null; on a decode error rust-g returns a non-null error string, so text2num(ret) yields null but the CRASH branch is never taken. (_real: rust_g.dm:435 guard is if(isnull(ret)) but should check as_num; ret is non-null error string, so decode failure caches null and never CRASHes._)
- **code/_helpers/gender.dm:466** [signature-mismatch/dropped-argument] — /mob/living/carbon/human/p_theirs(capitalized, temp_gender) declares a spurious leading `capitalized` argument that the base /atom/p_theirs(temp_gender) (and /datum, /client, /mob, /obj/item/clothing) does not have. Callers (e.g. GET_TARGET_PRONOUN in REPLACE_PRONOUNS does call(target, ...)(gender)) pass the gender positionally as the first arg, so on a human it lands in `capitalized`, and the `temp_gender ||= get_visible_gender()` + `..()` then forwards a null/wrong gender up the chain. (_real: gender.dm:466 /human/p_theirs(capitalized, temp_gender) has spurious leading arg; base /atom/p_theirs(temp_gender) (line 363) and all others take only temp_gender. GET_TARGET_PRONOUN calls (gender) positionally so gender lands in capitalized and is dropped._)
- **code/_helpers/mobs.dm:79-82** [indentation / wrong-scope return] — In random_facial_hair_style(), `return f_style` is indented one level too deep, placing it INSIDE the `if(valid_facialhairstyles.len)` block. When there are no valid facial-hair styles for the gender/species, the proc falls through and returns null instead of the intended default "Shaved" (the `f_style` local). Compare with the sibling proc random_hair_style() (lines 55-58) where the `return h_style` sits at proc scope and correctly returns the "Bald" default. (_real: `return f_style` at line 82 is indented inside `if(valid_facialhairstyles.len)`; empty-list path falls through to proc end returning null instead of the "Shaved" default. Sibling random_hair_style returns at proc scope._)
- **code/_onclick/drag_drop.dm:23** [wrong-variable / type-confusion] — Inside `/atom/MouseDrop(...)` the guard is `if(is_incorporeal(usr)) return`. `is_incorporeal()` is a 0-arg proc defined only on /atom (and overridden on /mob); there is no global proc of that name. So a bare `is_incorporeal(usr)` call inside an /atom method resolves to `src.is_incorporeal()` — i.e. it checks the DRAGGED atom, not `usr` — and the `usr` argument is silently discarded. (_real: inside /atom/MouseDrop, bare `is_incorporeal(usr)` resolves to src.is_incorporeal() (the dragged atom), discarding usr; checks wrong atom._)
- **code/_onclick/hud/action/item_action.dm:21-26** [wrong-variable / always-true condition] — In `/datum/action/item_action/vv_edit_var(var_name, var_value)`, after confirming `var_name == NAMEOF(src, button_icon_state)`, the code branches on `if(isnull(var_name))` to decide whether to (re)add the item-overlay component or qdel it. But `var_name` is the variable NAME string and is never null in this branch (it just compared equal to "button_icon_state"). The test should be on `var_value`, the newly-assigned value. (_real: after `var_name == NAMEOF(src, button_icon_state)` it branches on `isnull(var_name)` which is never null in this branch; should test var_value. AddComponent path is dead, overlay never restored via VV._)
- **code/_onclick/hud/human.dm:315-322** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — The 'use held item on self' HUD button is built into local var `aw` (lines 315-319: new screen, sets icon/icon_state/name/screen_loc), but lines 320-322 then operate on the stale `using` var instead of `aw`: `using.color = HUD.ui_color`, `using.alpha = HUD.ui_alpha`, and `adding |= using`. `using` at this point still points to the last hand-swap inventory screen ('hand2', from line 210). So `aw` never has its color/alpha set and is never added to `adding`, while `using` is redundantly re-added and gets its color/alpha clobbered. (_real: 'use held item on self' button built into `aw` (315-319) but lines 320-322 set color/alpha and `adding |= using` on stale `using` (hand2); aw never added to adding, hand2 button clobbered._)
- **code/_onclick/hud/screen_objects.dm:1052-1053** [wrong-call / missing argument] — In `/atom/movable/screen/ammo/update_hud(mob/living/user, obj/item/gun/G)`, when the gun is invalid or has no ammo counter, it calls `remove_hud()` with no arguments. `remove_hud(mob/living/user)` then sees `user == null` and executes `user?.client?.screen -= src`, which is a no-op. (_real: line 1053 calls remove_hud() with no arg; remove_hud(user) runs `user?.client?.screen -= src` with user null → no-op, so stale ammo HUD never removed (src confirmed present at 1049)._)
- **code/controllers/subsystem.dm:214** [operator precedence / always-false condition] — In enqueue(), the condition `if (queue_node_flags & (SS_TICKER|SS_BACKGROUND) == SS_TICKER)` is missing parentheses around the bitmask. In DM `==` binds tighter than `&`, so this parses as `queue_node_flags & ((SS_TICKER|SS_BACKGROUND) == SS_TICKER)`. Since (SS_TICKER|SS_BACKGROUND) != SS_TICKER, the inner expression is 0, making the whole test `queue_node_flags & 0` — always false. The sibling line 215 correctly parenthesizes: `(SS_flags & (SS_TICKER|SS_BACKGROUND)) != SS_TICKER`. (_real: `queue_node_flags & (SS_TICKER|SS_BACKGROUND) == SS_TICKER` — == binds tighter than &, so inner expr is 0 (FALSE) making test always false; sibling line 215 parenthesizes correctly._)
- **code/controllers/subsystems/research.dm:222** [inverted/always-false condition (misplaced parenthesis)] — `if(islist(techweb_nodes_starting && clearall))` evaluates `islist(techweb_nodes_starting && clearall)` — the `&&` is inside the islist() call. The inner expression resolves to either FALSE (when clearall is FALSE) or the value of clearall/TRUE (when clearall is TRUE), neither of which is a list, so islist(...) is always FALSE and `techweb_nodes_starting.Cut()` never runs. (_real: `islist(techweb_nodes_starting && clearall)` — && is inside islist(), arg is never a list so islist() is always FALSE and .Cut() never runs. Latent (no clearall caller) but condition is wrong._)
- **code/controllers/subsystems/supply.dm:237-257** [runtime/null-deref] — approve_order() looks up `adm_order` by matching ordernum in adm_order_history, but if no match is found (adm_order stays null) it unconditionally writes adm_order.status / adm_order.approved_by / adm_order.approved_at at lines 255-257. deny_order() has the same unguarded pattern (lines 266-286). (_real: approve_order (255-257) and deny_order (284-286) write adm_order.* unguarded; if no ordernum match adm_order stays null → null-deref; approve already deducted points (260) before the crash path on the next call._)
- **code/datums/components/antags/changeling/powers/cryo_sting.dm:16-23** [TOCTOU / cooldown checked after side effects] — changeling_cryo_sting() calls changeling_sting(20, ...) on line 16 FIRST, which (per changeling.dm:306-308) already deducts 20 chems, resets sting_range and sets the generic sting cooldown and performs the stealth sting. Only afterward (line 20) does it check the CRYO_STING cooldown and `return` if still on cooldown — by which point the chems are already spent and no cryotoxin is injected. (_real: changeling_sting (changeling.dm 306-308) deducts chem_charges + sets sting cooldown before cryo_sting checks CRYO_STING cooldown at line 20; chems spent on no-op when on cooldown._)
- **code/datums/components/antags/changeling/powers/panacea.dm:47,53** [arithmetic error (min vs max)] — Germ-level healing uses `G.germ_level = min(0, G.germ_level - germ_heal)`. Because `germ_heal` (heal_amount*100 = 500 or 1000) is large, `germ_level - germ_heal` is usually negative, and min(0, negative) returns the negative value; even when positive it is clamped to 0. The intended clamp is `max(0, ...)` (as used elsewhere, e.g. horror.dm:562 `max(0, I.germ_level - 25)`). (_real: lines 47 and 53 use min(0, germ_level - germ_heal); with large germ_heal result is negative, min(0,neg)=neg; should be max(0,...)._)
- **code/datums/components/antags/changeling/powers/transform_sting.dm:26-31** [wrong-variable / discarded return] — When the ling has more than one absorbed DNA, the menu result is discarded: `tgui_input_list(...)` is called on line 27 but its return value is never assigned to `S` (the assignment exists only in the single-DNA else branch on line 29). `S` is therefore null, so `changeling.GetDNA(S)` returns null and the proc returns at line 33 — the sting never works for any ling with 2+ DNA, which is the normal case. (_real: line 27 tgui_input_list return is discarded; S only assigned in else (single-DNA) branch, so with 2+ DNA S is null and GetDNA(null) returns null -> returns at line 33._)
- **code/datums/components/dry.dm:14-17** [wrong-type deref / runtime risk] — step_dry() assigns `T = get_turf(parent)` to a var typed `/turf/simulated` and then calls `T.wet_floor_finish()`. wet_floor_finish() is defined only on /turf/simulated. get_turf() can return a base /turf (e.g. space or other unsimulated turf). (_real: T typed /turf/simulated, wet_floor_finish only defined on /turf/simulated (simulated.dm:50); get_turf can return base /turf (space) -> runtime._)
- **code/datums/components/reagent_hose/connector.dm:62-68** [inverted condition] — process() comment says 'Return reagents to source if no hose, lossy to avoid exploits', but the guard is `if(!reagents.total_volume)` — it only runs the trans_to_holder/clear_reagents path when the connector is already empty (a no-op). When the connector actually holds reagents and the hose is gone, nothing is returned. (_real: line 65 if(!reagents.total_volume) only drains back when connector already empty (no-op); non-empty disconnected connector never returns reagents, contradicting the comment._)
- **code/datums/components/species/xenochimera.dm:141, 150, 172** [arg-mismatch] — update_xenochimera_hud is defined as proc/update_xenochimera_hud(danger, feral) (2 params, line 222), but these three call sites invoke it as update_xenochimera_hud(owner, danger, feral_state) (3 args). The mob `owner` is bound to the `danger` param (always truthy) and the real danger value is bound to `feral`; the third arg is dropped. The correct call at line 220 passes (danger, feral_state). (_real: update_xenochimera_hud(danger,feral) defined 2-param (line 222); calls at 141/150/172 pass (owner,danger,feral_state) 3 args, shifting owner->danger(truthy), danger->feral; line 220 call is correct._)
- **code/datums/components/traits/gargoyle.dm:43-48** [null-deref] — In process_component(), when transformed is TRUE but statue is null, line 44-45 sets transformed = FALSE but does NOT return; execution falls through to line 48 statue.damage(-0.5) and line 54+ statue/gargoyle healing using the null statue. (_real: line 44-45 if(!statue) transformed=FALSE has no return; falls through to line 48 statue.damage(-0.5) dereferencing null statue._)
- **code/datums/components/traits/low_sugar.dm:38** [inverted min/max] — living_guy.drowsyness = max(100, living_guy.drowsyness+30) uses max() where a cap with min() was clearly intended. max(100, ...) forces drowsyness to be at least 100 every time this branch fires. (_real: line 38 drowsyness = max(100, drowsyness+30) forces drowsyness>=100 (instant near-passout); min() was intended._)
- **code/datums/crafting/items.dm:109-113** [copy-paste bug] — /datum/material/shellchitin defines stack_type = /obj/item/stack/material/dreamscale, but the dedicated /obj/item/stack/material/shellchitin stack type exists (defined right below at line 124). The shellchitin material's stack_type points at the wrong (dreamscale) stack. - **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/hair.dm:36-38** [inverted-condition] — In /datum/symptom/shedding/Activate, `if(ishuman(A.affected_mob)) return` returns early when the mob IS human, so all the hair-loss logic below (which casts affected_mob to /mob/living/carbon/human and manipulates h_style/f_style) only executes for non-humans where the cast is invalid.
