- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/language.dm:42** [wrong-condition] — In Start(), `if(A.resistance)` enables gibberish whenever the disease's resistance is non-zero. The default resistance for this symptom is 2, so gibberish is essentially always on. The threshold_descs documents this as a 'Resistance 5' threshold. - **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/mlem.dm:64** [wrong-variable] — In Activate(), `if(resistance >= 5)` references the symptom datum's own `resistance` var (constant 3 for mlem), not `A.resistance` (the disease stat). Since 3 >= 5 is always false, the 'mlemming spree' (extra emotes + addtimers) never triggers. - **code/datums/diseases/lycancoughy.dm:36, 39, 44, 50, 58, 65** [wrong-argument / copy-paste bug] — `take_damage` on external organs has signature `take_damage(brute, burn, sharp, edge, ...)` (organ_external.dm:396). The disease calls `stomach.take_damage(BRUTE, rand(...))` where `BRUTE` is the string "brute" (damage_organs.dm:3). So the string "brute" is passed as the numeric `brute` amount (coerces to 0) and the intended random amount lands in the `burn` slot. The limb takes burn damage instead of brute, and zero brute. Note line 54 calls `stomach.take_damage(rand(0, 5))` (no BRUTE) which is the correct form, showing the BRUTE-prefixed calls are mistaken. (_real: lycancoughy.dm:36/39/44/58/65 stomach.take_damage(BRUTE, rand) passes string "brute" as numeric brute (->0) and amount into burn slot; signature is take_damage(brute,burn,...) per organ_external.dm:396; line 54 uses correct form_)
- **code/datums/diseases/roanoke.dm:65-66, 77-78** [null-deref / stale-ref] — At stage 5 the instance var `O` is assigned only inside `if(prob(2))` (line 62-63), but is dereferenced in a separate, independent `if(prob(1))` block: `O.take_damage(rand(1,3))` (line 65-66). The same pattern repeats at stage 6 (O set at line 73-74, dereferenced at line 77-78). On any tick where the assigning prob() fails but the dereferencing prob() fires, `O` holds whatever value it had last tick (a possibly-gibbed/removed organ) or is null on the first stage-5/stage-6 tick if no prior prob() ever assigned it. (_real: roanoke.dm instance var O (line 19) assigned only in if(prob(2)) blocks but derefed O.take_damage in independent if(prob(1)) blocks (lines 65-66,77-78); null on first tick or stale organ ref_)

- **code/datums/soul_link.dm:130-137** [broken state machine / missing loop break] — `/datum/soul_link/multi_sharer/replacement_pool/owner_died` is documented as putting ONE of the sharers into the owner's body ('winner-stays-on'), but the for-loop has no break after a successful transfer. (_real: Holiday_Game_Start line 267 switch(GLOB.Holiday) but GLOB.Holiday is a list (GLOBAL_LIST_EMPTY, line 2); string case line 270 never matches, Christmas_Game_Start dead._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/events/holidays/Holidays.dm:279-302** [wrong type in switch / unreachable code] — Holiday_Random_Event() does `switch(GLOB.Holiday)` with string cases ("End of the World", "Christmas", "Christmas Eve"), but GLOB.Holiday is a list. The switch compares the list to strings and never matches, so GameOver()/ChristmasEvent() never fire. (_real: Holiday_Random_Event line 279 switch(GLOB.Holiday) with string cases (lines 298/301) against a list; GameOver/ChristmasEvent unreachable._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/malfunction/newmalf_ability_trees/HELPERS.dm:202-205** [wrong-variable] — In `get_other_ais()`, the loop `for(var/mob/living/silicon/ai/AT in GLOB.mob_list)` tries to skip the calling AI with `if(L == A)` — comparing the result list `L` to the AI `A` instead of `if(AT == A)`. The condition is never true, so the calling AI itself is always added to the returned list. (_real: get_other_ais line 203 if(L == A) compares result list L to AI A instead of AT==A; never true, calling AI always added to returned list._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/malfunction/newmalf_ability_trees/tree_manipulation.dm:182-200** [ordering / TOCTOU resource bug] — In `machine_overload`, the machine is damaged first — `M.use_power(2000000)`, APC EMP/cell destruction and `set_broken()` (lines 182-196) — and only afterwards is `ability_pay(user, price)` called (line 199). If payment fails (CPU drained between prechecks and here, or APU engaged), the proc returns having already burned the APC/SMES without charging the AI. (_real: machine_overload damages machine (use_power/EMP/cell-destroy/set_broken lines 182-196) before ability_pay (line 199); payment failure leaves machine damaged for free._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/aura/biomed_aura.dm:20-25** [null-deref / missing-return-after-qdel] — process() does `if(!pay_energy(75)) qdel(src)` without a following `return`. qdel runs /obj/item/spell/aura/Destroy() (which STOP_PROCESSINGs) and then /obj/item/spell/Destroy() which nulls `owner` and `core`. Execution falls through to `range(calculate_spell_power(4), owner)`; calculate_spell_power returns null when core is null, and owner is null, so range(null,null) runtimes. (_real: biomed process line 21-22 `if(!pay_energy(75)) qdel(src)` no return; aura Destroy STOP_PROCESSING + spell Destroy nulls owner/core, then line 25 range(calculate_spell_power(4),owner) uses nulled refs._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/aura/fire_aura.dm:20-23** [null-deref / missing-return-after-qdel] — Same defect as biomed_aura: `if(!pay_energy(100)) qdel(src)` with no return, then `range(round(calculate_spell_power(4)), owner)` executes after owner/core have been nulled by Destroy(). (_real: fire_aura line 21-22 qdel without return, then line 23 range(round(calculate_spell_power(4)),owner) runs after owner/core nulled._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/aura/frost_aura.dm:20-23** [null-deref / missing-return-after-qdel] — Same defect: `if(!pay_energy(100)) qdel(src)` with no return, then proceeds to `range(...,owner)` and uses calculate_spell_power after owner/core are nulled. (_real: frost_aura line 21-22 qdel without return, then line 23 range(...,owner) and calculate_spell_power after nulling._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/aura/shock_aura.dm:19-22** [null-deref / missing-return-after-qdel] — Same defect: `if(!pay_energy(500)) qdel(src)` with no return, then `range(calculate_spell_power(4), owner)` runs after owner/core have been nulled. (_real: shock_aura line 20-21 qdel without return, then line 22/26 range(calculate_spell_power(...),owner) after owner/core nulled._)
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/aura/unstable_aura.dm:20-23** [null-deref / missing-return-after-qdel] — Same defect: `if(!pay_energy(200)) qdel(src)` with no return, then `range(calculate_spell_power(14), owner)` runs after owner/core nulled. - **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/resurrect.dm:28** [inverted/always-false condition (wrong variable)] — The "dead for too long" guard is `if(L.tod > world.time + 30 MINUTES)`. `tod` is the legacy time-of-death var which is null on death (the numeric time-of-death is `timeofdeath`, set to world.time in code/modules/mob/death.dm:132); `tod` is only ever assigned a *text* string (stationtime2text()) in toxins.dm. So this compares 0 (or a string) against world.time+30min, which is always false. - **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/summon/summon_creature.dm:53-55** [wrong-variable / wrong receiver] — In on_summon(mob/living/simple_mob/summoned), `adjust_scale(new_size)` is called with no explicit receiver. adjust_scale is defined on /atom/movable (code/game/atoms_movable.dm:544), so the implicit receiver is `src` — the /obj/item/spell/summon spell object — not the summoned mob. Every other line in this proc correctly operates on `summoned`, and the comment above says "Makes their new pal big and strong". (_real: line 55 `adjust_scale(new_size)` has implicit receiver src (the spell obj), not `summoned`; every other line operates on summoned. Size buff applied to spell item._)
- **code/game/machinery/Sleeper.dm:491-495** [null-deref] — go_out() guards with `if(!occupant || occupant.loc != src)` and inside that block immediately calls `occupant.cozyloop.stop()` and `occupant = null`. When `occupant` is null (the first disjunct), `occupant.cozyloop` dereferences null and runtimes. (_real: go_out() does if(!occupant || ...) then occupant.cozyloop.stop(); when occupant is null the body null-derefs occupant.cozyloop._)
- **code/game/machinery/air_alarm.dm:510-511, 798-799, 841-842** [wrong-variable / broken propagation] — Three loops iterate sibling alarms `for(var/obj/machinery/alarm/AA in alarm_area.air_alarms)` but the loop body calls bare `update_icon()` (implicitly on `src`) instead of `AA.update_icon()`. The loop variable AA is never used, so src's icon is refreshed N times and the other alarms in the area are never updated. (_real: lines 511/799/842 loop for(AA in alarm_area.air_alarms) but call bare update_icon() on src instead of AA.update_icon(); sibling alarms never refresh._)
- **code/game/machinery/atmo_control.dm:278-293** [broken logic / wrong-key removal] — configure_sensors() 'Remove' branch builds `sensor_names` mapping name->name (line 282: `sensor_names[sensors[tag]] = sensors[tag]`), then at line 292 does `sensors -= sensor_names[to_remove]`. `sensors` is an assoc list keyed by sensor *tag* (id_tag/id), with the display *name* as value. Subtracting a name value from a list keyed by tags removes nothing (the name is not a key). Also line 281 `for(tag in sensors)` uses an undeclared loop variable `tag` (missing `var/`). (_real: sensors keyed by tag; sensor_names maps name->name, so sensors -= sensor_names[to_remove] subtracts a name (not a key) and removes nothing; also for(tag in sensors) missing var/._)
- **code/game/machinery/computer/bodycamera.dm:103-109** [logic bug / broken loop (wrong variable)] — show_thing()'s 'walk to topmost atom' loop never advances: `var/atom/recursive_loc = thing; while(--tries){ recursive_loc = thing.loc; if(!istype(recursive_loc, /atom/movable)) break }`. It reads `thing.loc` every iteration instead of `recursive_loc.loc`, so it only ever inspects the first-level loc and never climbs the containment chain. (_real: while loop sets recursive_loc = thing.loc each iteration (thing constant) instead of recursive_loc.loc, never climbing the containment chain past level one._)
- **code/game/machinery/computer/cloning.dm:222** [null-deref risk] — tgui_act() does `var/mob/living/carbon/human/scanner_occupant = scanner.get_occupant()` unconditionally, before any of the per-action `!scanner` guards. `scanner` is assigned from findscanner(), which returns 0 when no DNA scanner is adjacent/in-area, so `scanner` can legitimately be null/0. (_real: line 222 scanner.get_occupant() runs unconditionally before per-action !scanner guards; findscanner() returns 0 when none found, so scanner can be 0/null and derefs._)
- **code/game/machinery/computer/message.dm:190-197** [null-deref] — The "auth" tgui_act action does `if(linkedServer.decryptkey == dkey)` with no null check on linkedServer. linkedServer is only set in LateInitialize if a message server exists; if none exists (or it was deleted) linkedServer is null and this dereference runtimes. The null guard at line 225 only protects the *second* switch block, which runs after auth. (_real: auth action line 193 if(linkedServer.decryptkey == dkey) has no null check; linkedServer can be null; guard at line 225 only protects the second switch._)
- **code/game/machinery/computer/security.dm:264-272** [null-deref] — In the "del_r_2" action, `set_temp("All records for [active1.fields["name"]] deleted.")` dereferences active1 BEFORE the `if(active1)` null-guard that immediately follows it. - **code/game/machinery/cryopod.dm:796-805** [wrong-variable/inverted logic] — In log_special_item, the key-acquisition block is broken: `if(ishuman) { if(H.original_player) loaded_from_key = H.original_player }` then `if(!loaded_from_key && to_despawn.mind && to_despawn.mind.loaded_from_ckey) { loaded_from_key = ... } else { loaded_from_key = "INVALID" }`. When original_player was set, the second `if` is false, so control falls into the `else` and overwrites the valid key with "INVALID". A successfully acquired key is always clobbered. (_real: if H.original_player sets loaded_from_key (798-799), then if(!loaded_from_key && ...) is false and falls into else loaded_from_key="INVALID" (805), clobbering the valid key._)
- **code/game/machinery/door_control.dm:163** [wrong-variable] — In /obj/machinery/button/remote/blast_door/bear/attack_hand, the access check is `if(!allowed(user) && (wires & 1))` but the access-flag var on this button type is `wires_num` (see the parent attack_hand at line 46 which correctly uses `wires_num & 1`). `wires` is not the intended bitflag here (it is the atom wiring datum / unrelated), so the ID-check gating is wrong. (_real: bear/attack_hand uses `wires & 1` but `wires` is the /datum/wires ref (atom _atom.dm:57, null here); access var is `wires_num` (=3), as parent attack_hand line 46 uses correctly._)
- **code/game/machinery/doors/firedoor.dm:380-402** [reference-aliasing / copy-paste bug] — `var/old_alerts = dir_alerts` copies the list reference, not a snapshot. Line 397 mutates `dir_alerts[index]` in place, which also mutates the list `old_alerts` points to. The subsequent comparison `if(dir_alerts != old_alerts)` (line 399) compares the list to itself and is therefore always false. (_real: dir_alerts is var/list/dir_alerts[4]; old_alerts aliases same list, line 397 mutates dir_alerts[index] in place, so `dir_alerts != old_alerts` is always false._)
- **code/game/machinery/event/christmas.dm:62-72** [wrong-variable / logic bug] — The double-gift guard checks `ckey_log[T.ckey]` (the recipient's ckey) but the record-write on line 72 stores `ckey_log[user.ckey] = TRUE` (the Santa/giver's ckey). The recipient's ckey is never written into ckey_log, so the `if(ckey_log[T.ckey])` check on line 62 never matches. (_real: guard checks ckey_log[T.ckey] (recipient) but line 72 writes ckey_log[user.ckey] (giver); recipient ckey never recorded so anti-double-gift check never fires._)
- **code/game/machinery/flasher.dm:88** [operator-precedence bug] — `if(!H.eyecheck() <= 0)` parses as `(!H.eyecheck()) <= 0` due to `!` binding tighter than `<=`. This collapses eyecheck()'s value to a boolean before comparison, so any nonzero eyecheck (including negative 'reduced protection' values) is treated identically. (_real: `if(!H.eyecheck() <= 0)` parses as `(!eyecheck()) <= 0`; any nonzero eyecheck (incl negative reduced-protection) yields 0<=0 true -> skips flash, bucketed with full protection._)
- **code/game/machinery/floodlight.dm:26-45** [broken state machine / logic bug] — process() only consumes battery via `cell.use(use*CELLRATE)` inside flicker_restore() (line 45), which is only scheduled by the rare flicker branch (5% chance, and only when cell.percent() < 10). During normal operation the floodlight never drains its cell. (_real: cell.use only in flicker_restore (line 45), scheduled solely by rare flicker branch (percent<10 && prob(5)); normal operation never drains cell._)
- **code/game/machinery/floorlayer.dm:44-48** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In attackby() when the user clicks the machine with a tile stack `W`, the code calls `user.drop_item(T)` and `TakeTile(T)` using `T` (the machine's own internally-stored tile var) instead of the item the user actually clicked with, `W`. - **code/game/machinery/holoposter.dm:34-36** [cleanup / dangling-timer-ref-leak] — /obj/machinery/holoposter/Destroy() removes src from GLOB.holoposters but never deletes `mytimer`. - **code/game/machinery/newscaster.dm:537** [null-deref] — In tgui_act, the "cancel_wanted" action does `if(GLOB.news_network.wanted_issue.is_admin_message)` without first checking that `wanted_issue` is non-null. - **code/game/machinery/petrification.dm:99-108** [missing-return / null-or-wrong-type-deref] — In petrify(), the validation messages at lines 99-100 (`!istext(...)` -> popup_msg "Invalid options.") and lines 102-103 (`!istype(T)` -> popup_msg "They must be visible...") emit a notice but do NOT `return`. - **code/game/objects/items/weapons/grenades/concussion.dm:29-36** [wrong-variable / discarded return value] — In concussion_blast(), `var/ear_safety = 0` is declared and then `H.get_ear_protection()` is called on its own line (line 31) with its return value thrown away. `ear_safety` is never assigned the protection value, so it stays 0 for the entire proc. - **code/game/objects/items/weapons/material/shards.dm:92** [arithmetic/operator-precedence] — `user.apply_damage(light_glove_d + will_break ? break_damage : 0, ...)` — in DM `+` binds tighter than the ternary `?:`, so this parses as `(light_glove_d + will_break) ? break_damage : 0`. Since light_glove_d is always >=2, the condition is always truthy and the call always deals exactly `break_damage` (4), completely ignoring the intended base cut damage `light_glove_d`. (_real: `light_glove_d + will_break ? break_damage : 0` parses as `(light_glove_d+will_break) ? break_damage : 0`; always-truthy, always deals flat break_damage_)

- **code/modules/admin/spawn_panel/spawn_handling.dm:99,149** [wrong-operator] — `spawn_atom()` uses `istype(atom_to_spawn, /turf)` (line 99) and `istype(atom_to_spawn, /mob)` (line 149), but `atom_to_spawn` is normally a typepath (set from `text2path()` in spawn_panel.dm `selected-atom-changed`, and the function's own guard on line 22 uses `ispath(...)`). `istype()` of a typepath value is always FALSE. - **code/modules/admin/verbs/change_appearance.dm:42** [copy-paste-bug] — In the editappear verb, the hair-color block is gated on the wrong variable: `if(new_facial)` immediately after the hair-color picker `new_hair = tgui_color_picker(...)`. - **code/modules/admin/verbs/secrets.dm:46** [always-true condition / broken permission gate] — `if((action != "admin_log" || action != "show_admins") && !check_rights(R_ADMIN)) return`. The subexpression `(action != "admin_log" || action != "show_admins")` is always TRUE (an action string can never equal both literals at once), so the whole guard reduces to `if(!check_rights(R_ADMIN)) return`. (_real: line 46 (action != "admin_log" || action != "show_admins") is always true, so the carve-out is dead and admin_log/show_admins are also blocked without R_ADMIN._)
- **code/modules/admin/view_variables/mass_edit_variables.dm:84** [operator-precedence logic bug] — `if(!class || !new_value == null && class != VV_NULL)`. DM precedence parses this as `(!class) || (((!new_value) == null) && (class != VV_NULL))`. `(!new_value)` is always 0 or 1, never null, so `(!new_value) == null` is always FALSE and the entire right-hand guard is dead; the check effectively becomes just `if(!class)`. (_real: line 84 !class || !new_value == null && class != VV_NULL parses as (!class) || (((!new_value)==null) && ...); (!new_value)==null is always false, so the null-value guard is dead, leaving only if(!class)._)
- **code/modules/anomalies/anomaly.dm:216-219** [wrong-variable / resource leak] — /obj/item/assembly/signaler/anomaly/choice/Destroy() does `for(var/obj/effect/anomaly/anom in options) qdel(anom)`, but `options` is an integer var (declared `var/options = 3` at line 188), not a list. Iterating an integer yields nothing, so the loop is a no-op. The anomaly objects actually created live in the `choices` assoc list (lines 207-208: `var/obj/effect/anomaly/anom = new type` ... `choices[capitalize(anom.name)] = type`). (_real: Destroy() line 217 iterates 'options' which is integer 3 (line 188), a no-op; spawned anom objects (new type, line 207) are stored only as type-paths in choices and leak with no qdel reference._)
- **code/modules/anomalies/anomaly_harvester.dm:103-104** [null-deref] — In attach_anomaly(): `if(stats.attached_harvester)` only checks that the weakref exists, then `var/obj/machinery/anomaly_harvester/harvester = stats.attached_harvester.resolve()` followed by `harvester.harvested = null` / `harvester.update_icon()` with no null/istype guard. WEAKREF.resolve() returns null if the previously-attached harvester has since been qdel'd. (_real: line 102 only checks the weakref exists, then lines 104-105 deref harvester.harvested/update_icon() with no null/istype guard after resolve(), which returns null if the old harvester was qdel'd._)
- **code/modules/asset_cache/iconforge/universal_icon.dm:588-591** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — After cropping the flattened icon to fit a new overlay, the running flat bounds are reassigned with X/Y transposed: `flatX1 = addX1` (ok), `flatX2 = addY1` (should be addX2), `flatY1 = addX2` (should be addY1), `flatY2 = addY2` (ok). flatX2 and flatY1 receive the wrong axis values. (_real: lines 589-590 transpose axes — flatX2 = addY1 and flatY1 = addX2 instead of addX2/addY1, corrupting flat bounds for subsequent overlay blends._)
- **code/modules/balloon_alert/balloon_alert.dm:25-27** [null-deref / inverted order] — In balloon_alert_visible(), `runechat_enabled = M.client.prefs?.read_preference(...)` dereferences M.client on line 25 BEFORE the `if(M.client && !runechat_enabled)` guard on line 27. get_mobs_in_view returns mobs that may be clientless, so M.client can be null. (_real: line 25 M.client.prefs?.read_preference derefs M.client before the if(M.client...) guard on line 27; clientless mobs from get_mobs_in_view runtime._)
- **code/modules/blob2/blobs/core.dm:116-118** [null-deref] — core/Destroy() does `new /obj/item/blobcore_chunk(T, overmind.blob_type)` which dereferences `overmind.blob_type` unconditionally, yet the lines immediately below (121) guard `if(overmind)` because overmind can be null (it is only assigned in LateInitialize; an AI core whose overmind creation failed, or a core qdel'd before LateInitialize, has overmind == null). (_real: Destroy() line 118 new blobcore_chunk(T, overmind.blob_type) unconditionally derefs overmind, but line 121 guards if(overmind); player cores (ai_controlled=FALSE) get overmind async via ghost query so it can be null._)
- **code/modules/blob2/overmind/types/ravenous_macrophage.dm:42-43** [null-deref] — on_chunk_tick does var/mob/living/L = locate() in range(world.view, B) then if(prob(5) && !L.stat). locate() returns null when no mob is in range, so L.stat dereferences null. The analogous on_pulse (line 26) correctly guards with if(L && ...). - **code/modules/casino/board_games/board_table.dm:28-33** [null-deref] — attack_hand calls pick_game(user) when game_ui is null, then unconditionally calls game_ui.tgui_interact(user). pick_game returns early (without assigning game_ui) if game_state != GAME_SETUP or if the user cancels the tgui_input_list, leaving game_ui null. (_real: attack_hand line 31-33 calls pick_game then unconditional game_ui.tgui_interact; pick_game returns early (game_ui?.game_state != GAME_SETUP, or input cancel) leaving game_ui null → null deref._)
- **code/modules/client/preferences/apply_hooks/name.dm:19-20** [wrong-variable / inverted condition (logic bug)] — The inline FBP detection `var/is_fbp = !(organ_data && organ_data[BP_TORSO] != "cyborg")` does NOT mirror the canonical is_FBP() it claims to. organ_data defaults to an empty list (`/datum/preference/organ_data/create_default_value()` returns `list()`, and pref_deserialize returns `list()` for bad input), and in DM an empty list is falsy. So for the common case (default organic character with no organ customization), `organ_data` is falsy → `!(FALSE && ...)` → is_fbp = TRUE. The canonical check in names.dm (`if(!organ_data) return FALSE; return organ_data[BP_TORSO]=="cyborg"`) correctly yields FALSE for empty/null organ_data. (_real: is_fbp = !(organ_data && organ_data[BP_TORSO]!="cyborg"); empty organ_data (default, create_default_value/deserialize return list()) is falsy so is_fbp=TRUE, inverse of canonical names.dm check (!organ_data -> FALSE). Allows digits in organic names._)
- **code/modules/client/preferences/apply_hooks/organs.dm:52-55** [null-deref] — When applying internal-organ FBP state for the brain, the code does `var/obj/item/organ/external/E = target.get_organ(I.parent_organ)` then `if(E.robotic < ORGAN_ASSISTED)`. `get_organ()` returns `organs_by_name[zone]`, which is null when the parent limb (the head) is absent — and the same hook can amputate external limbs earlier in this very proc (the `status == "amputated"` -> `O.remove_rejuv()` branch). A saved/legacy/forged organ_data that amputates the head while keeping a brain entry produces E == null and dereferences `E.robotic`, runtiming. (_real: line 53-54 E = target.get_organ(I.parent_organ) then E.robotic with no null guard; head can be amputated earlier in same proc (status=="amputated" -> remove_rejuv), get_organ returns null -> runtime on E.robotic._)
- **code/modules/client/preferences/types/character/rlimb_data.dm:18-22** [list-mutation-during-iteration] — pref_deserialize iterates `for(var/limb in input)` and removes the current element with `input -= limb` inside the same loop. In BYOND/DM, removing the element currently being iterated from a list shifts the underlying index and causes the for-in iterator to skip the element immediately following the removed one. (_real: pref_deserialize for(var/limb in input) with input -= limb (line 21) inside loop skips the key after each removed one; adjacent invalid robolimb keys partially survive sanitization._)
- **code/modules/client/spam_prevention.dm:12** [arithmetic/wrong-operator] — `spam_alert = max(0, spam_alert--)` uses post-decrement: `spam_alert--` returns the OLD (un-decremented) value, which is then assigned back to spam_alert by the outer `=`. The decrement is immediately overwritten, so spam_alert never actually decreases on the non-spam path. (_real: spam_alert = max(0, spam_alert--) (line 12); post-decrement returns old value, then = overwrites the decrement, so the counter never decays on the non-spam path._)
- **code/modules/clothing/accessories/accessory.dm:966-979** [null-deref / wrong-type access] — In /obj/item/clothing/accessory/collar/shock/bluespace/receive_signal (and the /modified and /malfunctioning variants at ~1064-1100 and ~1136-1179), M is assigned from loc/loc.loc and may be null or a non-human mob, but is immediately cast `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = M` and dereferenced via `if(!H.resizable)` with no null/istype guard. (_real: bluespace receive_signal (line 976-978) casts var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = M then H.resizable with no null/istype guard; M is null when on a turf or non-human._)
- **code/modules/clothing/accessories/armor.dm:156-177** [wrong-variable/dead-code] — handle_shield() laser/beam reflection is defined on type `/obj/item/clothing/accessory/armor/armorplate/ablative`, but the actual ablative plate (named 'ablative armor plate', icon 'armor_ablative') is defined at line 148 as `/obj/item/clothing/accessory/armor/armorplate/laserproof`. No item of type `armorplate/ablative` exists/spawns, so the reflect override never runs on the real ablative plate. No code spawns ablative (only a VR map); worn laserproof plate never reflects beams._)

- **code/modules/clothing/gateway/gateway.dm:130-135** [wrong-slot / dead code (behavior bug)] — /obj/item/clothing/suit/armor/buffvest is a suit-slot item, but its equipped() override only START_PROCESSINGs when `H.head == src`. A suit can never be in the head slot, so the condition is always false and processing never starts. A suit can never be in the head slot, so the condition is always false and processing never starts. - **code/modules/clothing/head/flowercrowns.dm:8-30** [item-loss / null-deref] — /obj/item/clothing/head/woodcirclet/attackby: inside the `if(istype(W, .../grown))` block, the drop_from_inventory(W), drop_from_inventory(src), qdel(W), qdel(src), put_in_hands(complete) calls run unconditionally after the kitchen_tag if/else chain. If the grown food is not poppy/sunflower/lavender/rose, `complete` stays null: both the circlet and the food are destroyed and the player gets nothing (put_in_hands(null)). Additionally `G.seed` is dereferenced (G.seed.kitchen_tag) without a null check, so a grown item with a null seed runtimes. (_real: lines 24-28 drop/qdel W and src then put_in_hands(complete) unconditionally; non-matching grown food leaves complete null = both items lost; G.seed deref'd at line 12 with no null check._)
- **code/modules/clothing/head/flowercrowns.dm:8-30** [item-loss / null-deref] — /obj/item/clothing/head/woodcirclet/attackby: inside the `if(istype(W, .../grown))` block, the drop_from_inventory(W), drop_from_inventory(src), qdel(W), qdel(src), put_in_hands(complete) calls run unconditionally after the kitchen_tag if/else chain. - **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/modules.dm:98** [operator-precedence / inverted condition] — Cable repair gate is `if(!cable.get_amount() >= 5)`. `!` binds tighter than `>=`, so this parses as `(!cable.get_amount()) >= 5`; `!x` is 0 or 1 and is never >= 5, so the condition is always FALSE and the 'need five units of cable' guard never fires. (_real: line 98 `if(!cable.get_amount() >= 5)` parses as `(!x) >= 5`, always false; five-units guard never fires, repair proceeds regardless of stack amount._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/protean.dm:48-59** [null-deref] — In `/obj/item/rig_module/protean/syphon/process()`, `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = holder.wearer` is dereferenced immediately via `istype(H.species, ...)` with no `if(!H)` guard. Similarly `P` (from `P?:humanform`) may be null when `P.nutrition` is read on line 57. (_real: protean/syphon/process line 52 derefs H.species with no if(!H) guard (sibling armor/healing guard it); P from P?:humanform can be null at line 57 P.nutrition._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/specific/crusher_gauntlets.dm:25-29** [null-deref / ignored return value] — `/obj/item/rig_module/gauntlets/activate()` calls `..()` without checking its return, then does `var/mob/living/M = holder.wearer` and `if(M.l_hand && M.r_hand)` with no null check on M. The base activate() may return 0 (engage failed, e.g. no wearer/suit not initialized) yet this override proceeds regardless, dereferencing a possibly-null M. (_real: gauntlets/activate line 26 calls ..() ignoring return (base can return 0 on engage fail), then line 29 reads M.l_hand with M=holder.wearer unchecked for null._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/specific/passive_protection.dm:231** [null-deref] — faraday_shield/activate() reads `stored_gloves_siemens_coefficient = gloves.siemens_coefficient` unconditionally, before the `if(gloves)` null check on the following lines. gloves is `holder.gloves` which can be null (e.g. rigs with glove_type = null such as /obj/item/rig/robotics, /obj/item/rig/internalaffairs, /obj/item/rig/zero). (_real: line 231 reads gloves.siemens_coefficient before the if(gloves) checks below; gloves=holder.gloves can be null on glove-less rigs, runtimes._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/specific/rescue_pharm.dm:66-67** [broken state / wrong-variable] — In engage(): `else if(charge.charges < chems_to_use) chems_to_use = charge.charges` permanently overwrites the instance var `chems_to_use` (default 5) with the (smaller) remaining charge amount. It is never reset back to 5. The intent was to clamp the dose for this one injection using a local variable. (_real: lines 66-67 overwrite instance var chems_to_use with charge.charges and never reset; once a charge runs low all future injections use the reduced dose._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/specific/self_destruct.dm:36** [inverted/always-false condition] — The not-worn guard `if(!holder || !holder.wearer || !holder.wearer.wear_suit == holder)` mis-parses due to operator precedence: `!holder.wearer.wear_suit == holder` is `(!holder.wearer.wear_suit) == holder`, i.e. comparing a boolean (0/1) to an object, which is essentially always false. The intended check was `holder.wearer.wear_suit != holder`. (_real: line 36 `!holder.wearer.wear_suit == holder` parses as `(!wear_suit) == holder`, always false; not-worn guard collapses to null checks only, can ash an unworn-but-inventory rig._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/rig.dm:611** [inverted/always-false condition] — In toggle_piece(): `if((!istype(wearer) || (!wearer.back == src && !wearer.belt == src)) && !forced)`. The sub-expression `!wearer.back == src` parses as `(!wearer.back) == src` (and likewise for belt), comparing a boolean to src/the rig object — always false. The intent was `wearer.back != src && wearer.belt != src`. (_real: line 611 `!wearer.back == src` parses as `(!wearer.back) == src`, always false (booleans vs object); worn-slot guard reduced to !istype(wearer)._)
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/rig_verbs.dm:24, 54, 82, 98, 114, 133, 186, 213, 249, 284** [inverted/always-false condition] — The worn-check `(!wearer.back == src && !wearer.belt == src)` is repeated across nearly every verb (toggle_vision, toggle_helmet, toggle_gauntlets, toggle_boots, deploy_suit, toggle_seals_verb, alter_voice, select_module, toggle_module, engage_module). Each parses as `((!wearer.back) == src) && ((!wearer.belt) == src)`, comparing booleans to the rig object, which is always false. The intended check was `wearer.back != src && wearer.belt != src`. (_real: grep confirms `!wearer.back == src && !wearer.belt == src` at lines 24,54,82,98,114,133,186,213,249,284; each parses booleans==src, always false; not-worn rejection is dead._)
- **code/modules/clothing/suits/reactive_armour_dimensional_themes.dm:47-66** [wrong-variable/dropped-argument] — place_barriers() computes custom_materials (line 55-57) and calls place_barrier(place_turf, custom_materials) with two arguments (line 61), but proc/place_barrier is defined with only one parameter: /datum/armour_dimensional_theme/proc/place_barrier(turf/source) (line 65). The custom_materials arg is silently discarded by DM, and the proc instead does `new barricade(source, material.name)` — passing the material *name string* as the barricade's second constructor arg rather than the intended custom_materials list. (_real: place_barrier defined with one param (turf/source) at line 65 but called with custom_materials at line 61; arg silently dropped, line 66 uses material.name instead, custom_materials computation is dead._)
- **code/modules/detectivework/forensics.dm:150-153** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In /datum/data/record/forensic/merge(), the blood-merge block reads BOTH operands from `other`: `var/list/blood = other.fields["blood"]` and `var/list/o_blood = other.fields["blood"]`. The first line should read this record's own blood list, i.e. `fields["blood"]`. Compare the correctly-written prints block (lines 136-143) and fibers block (lines 145-148) which read `fields[...]` for self and `other.fields[...]` for the other. (_real: line 150 var/list/blood = other.fields["blood"] should be fields["blood"]; both blood and o_blood read from other, so self's blood DNA is discarded on merge (prints/fibers blocks read self correctly)._)
- **code/modules/economy/cash_register.dm:257-302** [TOCTOU / missing re-validation after sleeping input] — In scan_card(), the PIN is requested via tgui_input_number() at line 261, which sleeps. After it returns, transaction_amount, src, linked_account, and the swiped card's presence are NOT re-validated before the charge is computed and applied (lines 271-296 use the live `transaction_amount`). The sibling EFTPOS.dm scan_card() was explicitly hardened against exactly this (it snapshots charge_amount before the PIN prompt and re-checks `charge_amount != transaction_amount`, card presence, adjacency, and QDELETED after the sleep); cash_register has no such guards. (_real: scan_card sleeps on tgui_input_number(line 261) then uses live transaction_amount (271-296) with no re-validation; sibling EFTPOS.dm snapshots charge_amount and re-checks QDELETED/adjacency/amount-unchanged after the sleep._)
- **code/modules/emotes/custom_emote.dm:9-14** [wrong-variable] — In /mob/proc/custom_emote, when no `message` is passed the proc fetches the typed emote into local `input` via tgui_input_text (line 11), but then calls `process_normal_emote(m_type, message, input, range)` with the still-null `message`. process_normal_emote then does `format_emote(src, message)` (custom_emote.dm line 29) using the null `message`, not `input`, so format_emote returns early and the emote is silently discarded. (_real: custom_emote fetches typed text into input but process_normal_emote calls format_emote(src, message) with still-null message (line 29); format_emote returns null on null message (emote_mob.dm:115), emote silently discarded for the input-prompt branch._)
- **code/modules/eventkit/gm_interfaces/fake_pda_conversations.dm:46-48** [wrong-variable/typo] — `FPC.fakeRefs =- ref`, `FPC.fakeJobs =- ref`, `FPC.names =- ref` are parsed as assignment plus unary minus (`= (-ref)`), not list-removal (`-=`). Since ref is text, `-ref` yields null/0. (_real: lines 46-48 `FPC.fakeRefs =- ref` parse as `= (-ref)`, overwriting the lists with -ref (null/0) instead of `-=` list removal, corrupting all stored identities._)
- **code/modules/eventkit/medical_issues.dm:65-66** [inverted min/max] — handle_damage() organ branch: `var/maxtotal = max(maxdamage, affectedorgan.damage)` then `affectedorgan.damage = max((affectedorgan.damage + damagestrength), maxtotal)`. To CAP organ damage at maxtotal the inner expression must use min(), not max(). (_real: line 66 `affectedorgan.damage = max((damage+strength), maxtotal)` uses max not min, so damage is forced to >= maxtotal each tick and grows; should be min() to cap._)
- **code/modules/events/apc_damage.dm:34** [wrong-argument-order] — `for(var/obj/machinery/power/apc/apc in range(epicentre, apcSelectionRange))` passes the epicentre atom as range()'s Dist arg and the number 25 as the Center arg. BYOND's signature is `range(Dist, Center)`. The correct call (used at line 16) is `range(severity_range, A)` (distance, center). - **code/modules/events/canister_leak.dm:13-14** [null/empty-list runtime] — `for(var/i in 1 to 10)` then `var/.../C = pick(all_canisters)` with no check that all_canisters is non-empty. If no canister passed the filter at lines 9-11, all_canisters is empty. - **code/modules/events/grubinfestation.dm:46-51** [null-deref] — In `end()`, `var/area/grub_area = get_area(G)` may be null. - **code/modules/events/meteor_strike.dm:34-40** [null-deref] — In `meteor_fall()`, after `isopenturf(current)` is true, `var/turf/below = GetBelow(src)` may return null when the meteor is already on the lowest z-level. Line 35 then dereferences `below.density` and line 40 calls `forceMove(below)` with a null target. - **code/modules/examine/descriptions/guns.dm:1-40** [resource/memory leak] — In describe_firepower(), for energy guns P is created with `P = new energy_gun.projectile_type()`, but every `switch(P.damage)` arm `return`s a string, so the trailing `qdel(P)` (line 40) is unreachable. - **code/modules/exploration/bossloots.dm:70-74** [wrong-variable / sign error] — tyr_sabre apply_hit_effect, I_HURT branch: on a 2% crit it calls `target.adjustBruteLoss(-50)`. Negative brute loss HEALS the target by 50, while the item's description_info says the harm-intent crit should 'deal guaranteed massive damage'. (_real: line 73 I_HURT crit calls `adjustBruteLoss(-50)` which heals 50, but description_info (line 44) says it should deal guaranteed massive damage; sign inverted._)

- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/core/special_pins/list_pin.dm:33-35, 66-67** [wrong-variable / wrong API usage] — remove_from_list_by_position() and edit_in_list_by_position() receive a numeric `position` (the list index from the TGUI/Topic 'pos' param) but then call `my_list.Find(position)`. List.Find() searches for an element equal to `position` and returns its index, rather than returning the element at index `position`. So the wrong element is targeted (or none, when the position number isn't itself a value in the list). (_real: remove_from_list_by_position line 33 and edit_in_list_by_position line 66 call `my_list.Find(position)` which searches for an element equal to the position number and returns its index, not the element at index position; wrong entry targeted._)

- **code/modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/command/comm.dm:44-50** [resource/memory leak / unbounded global growth] — `/datum/comm_message_listener/New()` adds itself to `GLOB.comm_message_listeners` but the type has no Destroy() that removes it. Each `/datum/computer_file/program/comm` creates a fresh `message_core` listener in its New() (line 19); the comm program's Destroy never qdels `message_core` either. So every comm program instance leaks one listener that lives forever in the global list (and the global ref keeps the listener — and through it the program — alive). (_real: /datum/comm_message_listener/New() does GLOB.comm_message_listeners.Add(src) with no Destroy() to remove it, and comm program has no Destroy to qdel message_core; listeners leak into the global list._)
- **code/modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/generic/ntnrc_client.dm:61-65** [null-deref / unvalidated input] — In `PRG_joinchannel`, `channel = GLOB.ntnet_global.get_chat_channel_by_id(new_target)` is assigned from a client-supplied id (`text2num(params["id"])`) and then immediately dereferenced as `if(!(src in channel.clients) && !channel.password)` with no null check. `get_chat_channel_by_id` returns null for an unknown id. (_real: line 62-63 channel = get_chat_channel_by_id(new_target) then if(!(src in channel.clients) && !channel.password) with no null check; returns null for unknown id, derefs null_)
- **code/modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/generic/nttransfer.dm:50-58** [dangling global reference / ref leak] — `kill_program()` in server mode crashes connected clients and nulls `downloaded_file`, but never removes `src` from `GLOB.ntnet_global.fileservers` (only `PRG_reset` does, at line 148-149). There is also no Destroy() override. A program that was serving a file (added to fileservers at line 170) and is then killed/qdeled without an explicit PRG_reset stays referenced in the global `fileservers` list. (_real: kill_program server branch (50-58) nulls downloaded_file but never Removes src from GLOB.ntnet_global.fileservers (added line 170); no Destroy override; only PRG_reset removes it_)
- **code/modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/security/digitalwarrant.dm:130-136, 111-128, 138-143** [TOCTOU / null-deref after sleeping input] — The `editwarrantcharges`/`editwarrantname`/`editwarrantnamecustom`/`editwarrantauth` actions dereference `activewarrant.fields[...]` without re-checking that `activewarrant` is non-null. `editwarrantcharges` even reads `activewarrant.fields["charges"]` as the default-value argument *before* the input, so it crashes immediately if `activewarrant` is null; the others sleep on `tgui_input_*` and only then write `activewarrant.fields[...]`, by which point `activewarrant` may have been cleared (e.g. via a concurrent `back`/`deletewarrant`). (_real: editwarrant* actions (111-143) deref activewarrant.fields with no non-null re-check; editwarrantcharges reads activewarrant.fields["charges"] as default arg before input; others write after sleeping tgui_input_)
- **code/modules/nifsoft/software/13_soulcatcher.dm:512-514, 526-528** [null-deref] — In soulcatcher_on_mob_death, `var/datum/nifsoft/soulcatcher/SC = HP.nif.imp_check(NIF_SOULCATCHER)` (and the symmetric self-catch case) is immediately used as `SC.catch_mob(...)` with no null/istype check. imp_check() returns FALSE (when the NIF stat != NIF_WORKING) or null (not installed). (_real: lines 513-514 & 527-528 SC=imp_check(NIF_SOULCATCHER) then SC.catch_mob() with no null check; imp_check returns FALSE when stat!=NIF_WORKING (nif.dm:583) or null if not installed; gated only on flag_)
- **code/modules/organs/internal/augment/bio.dm:113-123** [resource-leak/missing-Destroy] — /obj/item/organ/internal/augment/bioaugment/health_scan creates `med_analyzer = new /obj/item/healthanalyzer/advanced` in Initialize with no loc (it is held only by the var, not in src.contents), but the type defines no Destroy() override to qdel it. (_real: Initialize creates med_analyzer = new healthanalyzer/advanced with no loc (line 117), held only by var; no Destroy override in type, so qdel of organ leaks the analyzer_)
- **code/modules/organs/internal/special/heart_anomalock.dm:53-59** [copy-paste/wrong-variable] — In `add_lightning_overlay`, the early branch `if(lightning_overlay)` assigns the `addtimer(...)` return value (a numeric timer id) to `lightning_overlay`, which is typed `/mutable_appearance`. It should store the timer id in `lightning_timer` (as the non-early path does at line 59). It also returns without re-adding/cutting the overlay, and later `clear_lightning_overlay` calls `cut_overlay(lightning_overlay)` with what is now a timer id, not an appearance. (_real: lightning_overlay typed /mutable_appearance (line 11); early branch line 55 assigns addtimer() return (timer id) to lightning_overlay instead of lightning_timer; clear_lightning_overlay later cut_overlay()s a number, timer leaks_)
- **code/modules/organs/misc.dm:11-16** [null-deref] — `/obj/item/organ/internal/borer/process()` does not call `..()` and uses `owner.reagents.get_reagent_amount(...)` / `owner.reagents.add_reagent(...)` with no null-check on `owner`. The base organ process() (skipped here) is what normally nulls a stale owner and early-returns; this override has no such guard. (_real: borer/process() (line 11) omits ..() and uses owner.reagents.get_reagent_amount/add_reagent (15-16) with no null guard; organ is START_PROCESSING'd with owner null after removal -> runtime each tick_)
- **code/modules/organs/organ.dm:403-404** [copy-paste/wrong-signal] — `/obj/item/organ/take_damage` sends `COMSIG_INTERNAL_ORGAN_PRE_DAMAGE_APPLICATION` a second time at the end of the proc (after damage was applied), duplicating the same PRE signal already fired at line 391. This looks like it was meant to be a POST-damage signal; the return value is also ignored. (_real: take_damage fires COMSIG_INTERNAL_ORGAN_PRE_DAMAGE_APPLICATION at line 391 and again at line 404 (post-damage); a POST signal define exists (signals_mob_carbon.dm:207) but is never sent; PRE double-fires (godmode handler)_)
- **code/modules/overmap/ships/computers/helm.dm:273** [logic/operator-precedence] — `if(!get_dist(ui.user, src) > 1 || ui.user.blinded || !linked)` — `!` binds tighter than `>`, so this parses as `(!get_dist(...)) > 1`, which is `(0 or 1) > 1` and is ALWAYS FALSE. The intended distance check (`get_dist(...) > 1`) never fires. (_real: helm.dm:273 `!get_dist(ui.user,src) > 1` parses as `(!get_dist())>1`, always false; distance guard dead._)
- **code/modules/overmap/ships/computers/sensors.dm:92** [logic/operator-precedence] — Same precedence defect as helm.dm: `if(!get_dist(ui.user, src) > 1 || ui.user.blinded || !linked)` parses as `(!get_dist(...)) > 1`, which is always FALSE, so the distance guard for the 'viewing' (remote-view) action is dead code. (_real: sensors.dm:92 same precedence bug `(!get_dist())>1` always false; distance guard dead._)
- **code/modules/pda/game_launcher.dm:121-122** [copy-paste bug / ref leak] — In /datum/data/pda/app/game_launcher/Destroy the ninemens cleanup is gated on the wrong var: `if(checkers) QDEL_NULL(ninemens)`. `checkers` was already QDEL_NULL'd two lines above, so the block never runs. (_real: game_launcher Destroy() line 121 `if(checkers) QDEL_NULL(ninemens)` gated on wrong var (checkers already nulled), so ninemens never qdel'd._)
- **code/modules/pda/pda.dm:449-456** [missing state update / item lost] — In /obj/item/pda/attackby the pen branch calls `user.drop_item(src)` (which just drops the active-hand item, i.e. the pen) then adds the 'pda-pen' overlay, but never moves the pen into the PDA (no `C.forceMove(src)` / `C.loc = src`). (_real: pda attackby pen branch (:454-456) calls drop_item(src) (drops held pen to PDA's turf) and adds overlay but never C.forceMove(src)/C.loc=src; pen not inserted, remove_pen finds none._)
- **code/modules/persistence/datum/persistence_datum.dm:128-132** [copy-paste / duplicate output] — /datum/persistent/proc/GetAdminSummary appends each entry's row twice: once guarded inside `if(!isnull(data))` (line 131) and again unconditionally (line 132). (_real: GetAdminSummary emits row at :131 (guarded by isnull) and again unconditionally at :132; entries duplicated, null data emits malformed `<tr></tr>`._)
- **code/modules/planet/tyr.dm:1, 15** [wrong-variable/type-mismatch (copy-paste bug)] — GLOBAL_DATUM(planet_tyr, /datum/planet/sif) declares the global var planet_tyr with type /datum/planet/sif, but /datum/planet/tyr/New() assigns `GLOB.planet_tyr = src` where src is a /datum/planet/tyr. /datum/planet/tyr is NOT a subtype of /datum/planet/sif (both descend directly from /datum/planet). This is clearly copy-pasted from sif.dm (which uses the same /datum/planet/sif type). The declared type is wrong for the value it always holds. (_real: tyr.dm:1 GLOBAL_DATUM(planet_tyr, /datum/planet/sif) but :15 assigns /datum/planet/tyr src; tyr not a subtype of sif. Declared type wrong (copy-paste from sif)._)
- **code/modules/power/cell.dm:158** [type-safety / wrong istype usage] — In /obj/item/cell/proc/gradual_charge, the adjacency check uses `if(loc.loc && !istype(loc.loc, user))`. istype()'s second argument must be a type path, but `user` is a mob instance, not a type. This does not test 'is loc.loc the same kind as user' or 'is user holding us'; it is a malformed type check that won't behave as intended. (_real: cell.dm:158 `!istype(loc.loc, user)` passes a mob instance as istype's type arg (must be a type path); malformed check, holding-validation broken._)
- **code/modules/power/fusion/core/core_field.dm:213-225** [inverted/unreachable condition (dead code)] — Inside check_instability(), after entering the `percent_unstable > 0.1` block, the severity tiers are tested as `if(percent_unstable > 0.2) ... else if(percent_unstable > 0.5) ... else if(percent_unstable > 0.8)`. Because the chain is ordered low-to-high with `else if`, any value > 0.2 is caught by the first branch, so the `> 0.5` and `> 0.8` branches are unreachable. The intent was clearly escalating severity at higher instability. (_real: else-if chain ordered low-to-high (>0.2 then >0.5 then >0.8) so >0.5/>0.8 branches are unreachable; only mildest >0.2 effect ever fires (lines 213-225)._)
- **code/modules/power/fusion/magpower.dm:39-56** [unbounded list growth / per-tick allocation] — In /obj/machinery/power/hydromagnetic_trap/Search(), `fields_in_range` is never cleared. Each process tick reassigns `things_in_range = range(7, src)` and then `.Add(FFF)` for every nearby em-field into the persistent `fields_in_range` list (line 42). Out-of-range entries are removed (line 49), but in-range fields are re-added every tick, so duplicates of the same field accumulate without bound. `active_field` likewise only grows via `+=` in Link() and is only Cut() when the machine is unanchored. (_real: Search() does fields_in_range.Add(FFF) for every in-range field each tick with no dedup; in-range fields re-added every tick so duplicates accumulate unbounded (line 42)._)
- **code/modules/power/singularity/field_generator.dm:256** [inverted/always-true condition] — start_fields() guards with `if(!src.state == 2 || !anchored)`. Due to operator precedence `!` binds before `==`, so this parses as `((!src.state) == 2) || (!anchored)`. `!src.state` is always 0 or 1, never 2, so the first clause is permanently FALSE; the guard collapses to just `!anchored`. (_real: if(!src.state == 2 || !anchored) parses as ((!state)==2)||(!anchored); !state is 0/1 never 2 so first clause always false; welded(state==2) check dead, state-1 generator projects fields._)
- **code/modules/projectiles/gun.dm:847-857** [runtime/null-deref] — equipped() and dropped() unconditionally dereference user.hud_used (e.g. `user.hud_used.add_ammo_hud(...)`, `user.hud_used.remove_ammo_hud(...)`) without a null check. hud_used is a base mob var (mob_defines.dm:156) that is null for any living mob without a client/HUD (simple mobs, NPCs holding guns, logged-out players). When such a mob equips/drops or picks up a gun, this is a guaranteed null dereference runtime. (_real: equipped (849/851) and dropped (856) deref user.hud_used with no null guard; hud_used (mob_defines 156) is null for client-less living mobs that never run create_mob_hud. Downgraded from high: player guns always have hud_used; simple-mob-in-hand path less common._)
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/energy.dm:99-101, 120-122, 144, 161** [runtime/null-deref] — Several energy-gun procs call `M.hud_used.update_ammo_hud(...)` / `user.hud_used.update_ammo_hud(...)` after only checking that the mob is the right type (or non-null), not that hud_used exists. e.g. process() line 99-101 does `var/mob/living/M = loc; if(istype(M)) M?.hud_used.update_ammo_hud(M, src)` — istype(M) does not guarantee M.hud_used is non-null. consume_next_projectile() (120-122) has the same pattern; load_ammo() (144) and unload_ammo() (161) call user.hud_used.update_ammo_hud with no hud_used guard at all. (_real: process (99-101) and consume_next_projectile (120-122) only istype-check M (the ?. on M is useless post-istype) then deref M.hud_used; load_ammo (144) and unload_ammo (161) deref user.hud_used with no guard; client-less mob holding self-recharge gun runtimes._)
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/energy/cell_loaded/nerd_cells.dm:64-69, 162-171** [runtime/null-deref] — In stabilize/on_hit (line 64-66) and stabilize2/on_hit (line 162-164), the code iterates a fixed list of body part tags and does `var/obj/item/organ/external/O = target.organs_by_name[name]` then immediately `for (var/datum/wound/W in O.wounds)` (and O.is_bandaged()/is_salved() in stabilize2) with no null check on O. A human missing a limb (amputated/dismembered) has a null entry in organs_by_name, so O is null and `O.wounds` is a null dereference. (_real: stabilize on_hit (65-66) and stabilize2 (163-164) do O = target.organs_by_name[name] then for(W in O.wounds)/O.is_bandaged() with no null check; missing limb -> null O -> deref._)
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/projectile.dm:47-51** [logic/state-machine] — In /obj/item/gun/projectile/Initialize, the random_start_ammo trimming `loaded.Cut(0, rand(0,max_shells))` is executed INSIDE the `for(var/i in 1 to max_shells)` loop that is simultaneously building `loaded` via `loaded += new ammo_type(src)`. The list is mutated (cut from the front) on every iteration, so each pass re-trims the partially-built list with a fresh random count. The parallel magazine branch (lines 55-58) correctly does the cut once, after building. Compare: speedloader random ammo is repeatedly/over-trimmed and the final count is effectively garbage rather than a single random reduction. (_real: loaded.Cut(0,rand(0,max_shells)) (51) is inside the for(i in 1 to max_shells) build loop (48-49), re-trimming the partial list every iteration; magazine branch (55-58) correctly cuts once after building._)
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/projectile/revolver.dm:65** [inverted/always-true-condition] — In revolver/detective/rename_gun(), the permission check `if(!M.mind.assigned_role == JOB_DETECTIVE)` parses (operator precedence) as `if((!M.mind.assigned_role) == JOB_DETECTIVE)`. `!assigned_role` is a boolean 0/1 compared to the string JOB_DETECTIVE, so the condition is always false and the role gate never triggers. (_real: `if(!M.mind.assigned_role == JOB_DETECTIVE)` parses as `(!assigned_role)==string`, always false; detective45 at line 92 does it correctly with `job != JOB_DETECTIVE`_)
- **code/modules/projectiles/precursor/eclipse.dm:36-48** [copy-paste/missing-statement] — In /obj/item/grenade/chem_grenade/frost/Initialize(), two beakers B1 and B2 are created and filled (B1: cryoslurry+potassium, B2: phosphorus+sugar), but only `beakers += B1` is added; B2 is never appended to the grenade's `beakers` list. Every other chem_grenade subtype (metalfoam, incendiary, antiweed, cleaner) adds both B1 and B2. (_real: frost/Initialize creates B1 and B2 but only `beakers += B1` at line 48; B2 (phosphorus+sugar) never added so its reaction never fires on detonate_)
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile.dm:362** [wrong-variable] — In fire(), the fallback that resolves a target from xo/yo offsets does `locate(CLAMP(starting + xo, 1, world.maxx), CLAMP(starting + yo, 1, world.maxy), starting.z)`. `starting` is a /turf (set at line 353), so `starting + xo` adds a number to a turf reference instead of using `starting.x + xo` / `starting.y + yo`. (_real: line 362 `locate(CLAMP(starting + xo,...))` where starting is a /turf (set line 353); adds number to turf ref instead of starting.x/.y_)
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile.dm:412** [wrong-variable/type-error] — In the multi-tile source handling of preparePixelProjectile(): `if(get_dist(T, target) < get_turf(curloc))` compares a numeric distance against a `/turf` object (`get_turf(curloc)`), not a distance. It almost certainly should compare against `get_dist(curloc, target)`. (_real: line 412 `if(get_dist(T, target) < get_turf(curloc))` compares numeric distance to a /turf object; closest-tile pick broken for multi-loc shooters_)
- **code/modules/projectiles/projectile/energy.dm:39-45** [wrong-variable / shared-state mutation in loop] — In /obj/item/projectile/energy/flash/on_impact, the loop over `viewers(T, flash_range)` does `flash_strength *= H.species.flash_mod` on the projectile-instance var for every human hit. The species modifier compounds across victims: the first human multiplies flash_strength once, the second multiplies the already-modified value again, etc. (_real: energy/flash/on_impact loops viewers and does `flash_strength *= H.species.flash_mod` on the instance var, compounding across each human hit_)
- **code/modules/random_map/drop/droppod.dm:56** [copy-paste / wrong-variable bug] — `var/on_y_bound = (y == 1 || y == limit_x)` compares the y-coordinate against `limit_x` instead of `limit_y`. It should be `(y == 1 || y == limit_y)`. (_real: line 56 `on_y_bound = (y == 1 || y == limit_x)` should be limit_y; only correct because default pods are square_)
- **code/modules/reagents/holder/holder.dm:347-354** [null-deref] — `trans_type_to(target, rtype, amount)` does `var/datum/reagent/transfering_reagent = get_reagent(rtype)` then unconditionally `amount = min(amount, transfering_reagent.volume)`. If the holder doesn't contain `rtype`, `get_reagent` returns null and `.volume` dereferences null. (_real: trans_type_to does `amount = min(amount, transfering_reagent.volume)` at line 354 with no null-check after get_reagent(rtype) which returns null if absent_)
- **code/modules/reagents/holder/vorebelly.dm:1-7** [null-deref] — `vore_trans_to_mob(...)` declares `obj/belly/target_belly = null` (default null). On the CHEM_VORE path (line 6-7) it immediately does `var/datum/reagents/R = target_belly.reagents` with no null/istype check on `target_belly`. If invoked with the default (or a null belly) on a living target, this dereferences null. (_real: vore_trans_to_mob CHEM_VORE path line 7 `var/datum/reagents/R = target_belly.reagents` with target_belly default null and no istype/null check_)
- **code/modules/recycling/sortingmachinery.dm:34-46** [inverted/always-true condition] — deliveryChute/hitby condition `if(!QDELETED(source) || (isitem(source) || isliving(source)) && !istype(source, /obj/item/projectile))` — due to operator precedence (&& binds tighter than ||) this is `(!QDELETED(source)) || ((isitem||isliving) && !projectile)`. The first operand is true for essentially any live thrown object, so the intended exclusion of projectiles/effects is bypassed. (_real: hitby (sortingmachinery.dm:35) condition (!QDELETED(source)) || (...) due to && precedence; first operand true for any live thrown object, so projectile exclusion is bypassed; Bumped (line 19) correctly excludes projectiles._)
- **code/modules/research/tg/experisci/experiment/instances/spectral.dm:13** [copy-paste/always-false condition] — `if(isobserver(captured_entity) || isliving(captured_entity) || isliving(/obj/effect/shadow_wight))` — the third clause calls isliving() on a bare type PATH literal (/obj/effect/shadow_wight) rather than on an instance. isliving() does istype(x, /mob/living); a type path is never a /mob/living instance, so this clause is always FALSE and is dead code. The intent was almost certainly `istype(captured_entity, /obj/effect/shadow_wight)`. (_real: spectral.dm:13 isliving(/obj/effect/shadow_wight) calls isliving on a type path (always FALSE); shadow_wight (an /obj/effect) thus never satisfies completion. Intent was istype(captured_entity, ...)._)
- **code/modules/resleeving/machines.dm:52** [wrong-variable / arithmetic error] — `H.adjustCloneLoss((H.getMaxHealth() - (H.getMaxHealth()))*-0.75)` computes `getMaxHealth() - getMaxHealth()` = 0, so the whole expression is `0 * -0.75 = 0`. adjustCloneLoss(0) is a no-op. (_real: machines.dm:52 adjustCloneLoss((getMaxHealth() - getMaxHealth())*-0.75) = 0*-0.75 = 0, a no-op; growpod clones get no initial clone damage as intended._)
- **code/modules/rogueminer/zone_console.dm:116-124** [runtime/null-deref] — scan_for_new_zone() does `ZM_target = GLOB.rm_controller.prepare_new_zone()` and immediately dereferences `ZM_target.myshuttle_landmark` (line 120) without a null check. prepare_new_zone() returns null when there are no clean zones available (controller.dm line 182-202 leaves ZM_target unset and returns it). The proc also sleeps for 60 ticks (line 110) before touching shuttle_control, so shuttle_control could have been destroyed in the interim. (_real: scan_for_new_zone nulls shuttle_tag (113) then derefs ZM_target.myshuttle_landmark (120); prepare_new_zone returns null when clean_zones empty (controller.dm 182-202). can_scan gate (78-81) doesn't check clean_zones, so reachable; runtime leaves shuttle broken._)
- **code/modules/spells/general/area_teleport.dm:62-72** [null/empty-deref] — The while loop `while(L.len)` only exits when either a Move succeeds (break) or L is fully emptied via L.Remove(attempt). If every candidate turf fails to Move, the loop drains L to length 0, sets success=0, and then line 72 calls `user.forceMove(pick(L))` on an empty list. (_real: area_teleport.dm:62-72 while(L.len) drains L on all-fail; line 72 user.forceMove(pick(L)) runs pick() on empty list -> runtime, no return._)
- **code/modules/spells/spellbook.dm:21-30** [null-deref/type-check-order] — In /obj/item/spellbook/Topic(), `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = usr` is followed immediately by `if(H.stat || H.restrained())` and later `if(H.mind.special_role == ...)` BEFORE the `if(!ishuman(H)) return 1` guard at line 26. If usr is null or a non-human mob, H.stat/H.restrained() are read on an unvalidated/mis-typed ref, and H.mind may be null. (_real: spellbook.dm:23 reads H.stat/H.restrained() and line 28 H.mind.special_role before the ishuman(H) guard at lines 25-26; Topic is client-reachable._)
- **code/modules/spells/targeted/targeted.dm:121-127** [list-mutation-during-iteration] — /datum/spell/targeted/cast iterates `for(var/mob/living/target in targets)` and inside the loop does `targets -= target` (line 125) to filter out-of-range targets. Removing the current element from the list being iterated shifts the internal index and causes the loop to skip the element immediately following each removed one. (_real: targeted.dm:122-125 for(target in targets) with targets-=target mutates the iterated list; DM skips the element after each removal, defeating the range re-filter._)
- **code/modules/surgery/robotics.dm:562-564** [wrong-ordering/use-before-init] — In /datum/surgery_step/robotics/install_mmi/end_step, `var/obj/item/organ/internal/mmi_holder/holder = null` is declared, then `M.loc = holder` is executed (setting the MMI's loc to null) BEFORE `holder` is actually created by the `new /obj/item/organ/internal/mmi_holder/...(target, 1, M)` calls on the following lines. The `M.loc = holder` assignment uses holder while it is still null. (_real: robotics.dm:562-564 holder declared null then M.loc=holder runs before holder is new'd at 566+; moves MMI to nullspace, dead/incorrect._)
- **code/modules/tgui/helpers.dm:1-26** [runtime/null-deref] — /datum/tgui_say/proc/handle_packets only allocates partial_packets when id == 1 (the first chunk). If a chunked tgui_say packet arrives with id > 1 first (out-of-order delivery, or a malicious/garbled client sending only a later chunk), partial_packets is still null and line 13 `var/list/chunks = partial_packets["chunks"]` dereferences null, runtiming. There is no guard that the first packet was received before indexing partial_packets. ## CONFIRMED — LOW
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/volume_pump.dm:52-54** [copy-paste / wrong type path] — Inside volume_pump.dm a subtype is declared as `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/binary/pump/on` (with icon_state="map_on", use_power=USE_POWER_IDLE) instead of `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/binary/volume_pump/on`. This re-opens the gas pump's `pump/on` (already defined identically in pump.dm) and no `volume_pump/on` subtype is ever created.
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/binary_devices/volume_pump.dm:52-54** [copy-paste / wrong type path] — Inside volume_pump.dm a subtype is declared as `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/binary/pump/on` (with icon_state="map_on", use_power=USE_POWER_IDLE) instead of `/obj/machinery/atmospherics/binary/volume_pump/on`. This re-opens the gas pump's `pump/on` (already defined identically in pump.dm) and no `volume_pump/on` subtype is ever created.
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/trinary_devices/mixer.dm:65** [wrong-variable / copy-paste] — transfer_moles estimate uses `mixing_inputs[air1]` for BOTH the air1 and air2 terms: `(set_flow_rate*mixing_inputs[air1]/air1.volume)*air1.total_moles() + (set_flow_rate*mixing_inputs[air1]/air2.volume)*air2.total_moles()`. The second term should use `mixing_inputs[air2]`.
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/components/trinary_devices/mixer.dm:65** [wrong-variable / copy-paste] — transfer_moles estimate uses `mixing_inputs[air1]` for BOTH the air1 and air2 terms: `(set_flow_rate*mixing_inputs[air1]/air1.volume)*air1.total_moles() + (set_flow_rate*mixing_inputs[air1]/air2.volume)*air2.total_moles()`. The second term should use `mixing_inputs[air2]`.
- **code/ATMOSPHERICS/environmental/LINDA_turf_tile.dm:408-419** [wrong-variable / sign error] — In the `share_end` (run_later) loop of `/turf/open/process_cell`, when `difference < 0` it calls `enemy_tile.consider_pressure_difference(src, difference)` with a NEGATIVE difference. The equivalent branch in the main sharing loop (line 385) correctly negates it: `enemy_tile.consider_pressure_difference(src, -difference)`. consider_pressure_difference() only records when `difference > pressure_difference` (which starts at 0), so a negative value is always discarded.
- **code/__defines/exosuit_fab.dm:46,50** [operator-precedence hazard] — The combined-mask macros EXOSUIT_MODULE_COMBAT (`EXOSUIT_MODULE_GYGAX | EXOSUIT_MODULE_DURAND | EXOSUIT_MODULE_PHAZON`) and EXOSUIT_MODULE_FIGHTER (`EXOSUIT_MODULE_PINNACE | EXOSUIT_MODULE_BARON`) are defined without surrounding parentheses, unlike the other OR-combined defines in the codebase. In any bitwise-test context like `flags & EXOSUIT_MODULE_COMBAT`, this expands to `(flags & GYGAX) | DURAND | PHAZON`, because `&` binds tighter than `|` in DM — the result is a nonzero constant regardless of `flags`, so the test is always-true. Currently the only consumer is `mech_flags`, which is assigned via `|` (where the missing parens are harmless and associative) and is never read in an `&` test anywhere in the tree, so the bug is latent rather than live.
- **code/__defines/math.dm:75** [arithmetic/logic error] — ISINRANGE_EX is documented as the exclusive version of ISINRANGE ("Same as above, exclusive") but its body is `(min < val && val > max)`. The second clause is inverted: it requires val to be GREATER than max instead of less than max. A correct exclusive range check is `(min < val && val < max)`. As written the macro can only return TRUE when min < val AND val > max, i.e. when max < val, which is never the intended 'strictly between min and max' semantics and is effectively almost always FALSE for any sane min<max.
- **code/__defines/talksounds.dm:13** [debug-leftover / chat spam] — /proc/rlist contains `to_chat(world, keys[i])` inside its key-iteration loop, broadcasting every key being processed to the entire world (all connected players).
- **code/_helpers/announcements.dm:38-43** [input-sanitization/logic-nesting] — In send_ooc_announcement(), the `if(encode_text)` html_encode of `text` is nested inside `if(encode_title && title && length(title) > 0)`. So `text` is only HTML-escaped when encode_title is also TRUE and a non-empty title was supplied; a caller passing encode_text=TRUE with no title (or encode_title=FALSE) gets the text inserted unescaped.
- **code/_helpers/atom_movables.dm:38-45** [logic-ordering] — throw_at_random() computes `var/list/turfs = trange(maxrange, src)` on the FIRST line, then only defaults `maxrange = 1` on the line AFTER. When called with maxrange null/0, trange(0, src) yields just src's own turf, which is then removed when !include_own_turf, leaving an empty list and a pick() runtime.
- **code/_helpers/icons.dm:933-938** [inverted condition / dead code] — icon_exists_or_scream() is documented to emit a stack trace the first time a missing file/state is seen. The guard is inverted: `if(!isnull(screams[file])) { screams[file] = TRUE; stack_trace(...) }`. `screams[file]` is only ever assigned inside this block, so on the first call it is null and the block is skipped; since it is never set on the first miss, the block is effectively never entered — the stack_trace is unreachable.
- **code/_onclick/hud/ability_screen_objects.dm:108-109** [wrong-variable / dead guard] — `/atom/movable/screen/movable/ability_master/proc/add_ability(name_given)` begins with `if(!name) return`. `name` is the screen object's own var (initialized to "Abilities"), not the parameter `name_given`. The guard was meant to reject an empty/null `name_given`.
- **code/_onclick/hud/ability_screen_objects.dm:24-35** [lifecycle / wrong Destroy() order] — `/atom/movable/screen/movable/ability_master/Destroy()` calls `. = ..()` on its FIRST line, then afterwards runs cleanup: `remove_all_abilities()`, `ability_objects.Cut()`, nulls `my_mob.ability_master`, removes itself from `my_mob.client.screen`, and nulls `my_mob`. Parent /atom/movable/Destroy already tears the object down before this cleanup executes.
- **code/_onclick/hud/minihud_rigmech.dm:150-156** [null-deref risk] — In `/atom/movable/screen/rig/airtoggle/Click()`, `owner_rig = master_ref?.resolve()` may be null (rig qdel'd / weakref stale), but the next line dereferences it unconditionally: `if(user != owner_rig.wearer)`.
- **code/controllers/subsystems/internal_wiki.dm:1193-1199** [missing return value] — /datum/internal_wiki/page/food/get_print() builds `body` but never returns it (no `return body` at the end), unlike every other get_print() override (ore, material, seed, smasher, chemical, drink, recipe, virus, gene) which all return body.
- **code/controllers/subsystems/radiation.dm:156-174** [division by zero / null-math] — wearing_rad_protected_clothing() computes `protected_limbs/limb_count` where limb_count is incremented once per entry in human.organs. If a human has zero external organs (fully delimbed/special body), limb_count stays 0 and the final division throws a runtime.
- **code/datums/chat_message.dm:344-349** [inverted/wrong condition] — create_chat_message() gates speaker visibility with `if(ismob(speaker) && !runechat_mob) return; else if(!runechat_obj) return`. Because of the else-if, a mob speaker whose runechat_mob pref is ENABLED falls through to the obj branch and is suppressed when runechat_obj is disabled — coupling mob runechat to the obj preference.
- **code/datums/components/antags/changeling/powers/engorged_glands.dm:12-14** [null-deref risk] — changeling_engorgedglands() does `var/datum/component/antag/changeling/comp = is_changeling(src)` then immediately `comp.chem_storage += 30` / `comp.chem_recharge_rate *= 2` with no null check, unlike every other power in this directory which guards `if(!changeling) return`.
- **code/datums/components/species/shadekin/powers/phase_shift.dm:35-36** [null-deref] — var/area/A = get_area(src) then A.flag_check(AREA_BLOCK_PHASE_SHIFT) with no null guard. If the mob is in nullspace (no area), get_area returns null and A.flag_check() runtimes. Other powers in this folder guard get_area results (e.g. dark_respite uses istype(get_area(...))).
- **code/datums/components/species/shadekin/powers/regenerate_other.dm:40-44** [missing re-validation after sleeping input] — After tgui_input_list (a sleeping prompt) selects `target`, the code only checks !target before applying the heal modifier, spending energy, and facing the target. It does not re-validate that target is still adjacent/in view or that SK still has energy.
- **code/datums/components/traits/low_sugar.dm:36** [inverted min/max] — living_guy.hallucination = max(30, living_guy.hallucination+8) uses max(30, ...) as a floor instead of a cap. This mirrors the line 38 pattern and looks like a min() was intended to cap hallucination growth.
- **code/datums/components/xenoqueen.dm:17-25** [missing-return/state machine] — In process(), the DEAD branch (lines 17-19) calls STOP_PROCESSING and clears aura_active but does not return, so execution continues into the range(7) loop and still applies the xenoqueenbuff modifier to nearby allies on the tick the queen dies.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/light.dm:52-56** [null-deref] — End() does `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = A.affected_mob` then dereferences `H.maxHealth`/`H.health` with no null check. End() can be invoked from /datum/disease/advance/Destroy() (which iterates symptoms calling S.End) where affected_mob may already be null (e.g. disease cured/removed before deletion).
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/narcolepsy.dm:78** [always-true-condition] — At stage 1, `if(base_message_chance)` tests the truthiness of base_message_chance (default 10), so the 'You feel tired' message fires every activation instead of probabilistically.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/stimulant.dm:67** [wrong-condition] — At stage 2-3, `if(prob(power) && H.stat)` gates the energetic/jittery effect on `H.stat` being truthy. Since DEAD already returned earlier (line 63) and CONSCIOUS == 0, this only fires when H.stat is UNCONSCIOUS, i.e. when the host is knocked out.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/vomit.dm:47-48** [inverted-condition] — In Start(), `if(A.stealth >= 4) supress_warning = FALSE` sets supress_warning to its default value FALSE. The threshold_descs says the symptom should remain hidden until active at Stealth 4, which requires supress_warning = TRUE.
- **code/datums/diseases/advance/symptoms/weight.dm:65-67** [logic-error] — In the 'Weight Loss' symptom stage 5, the host GAINS nutrition: `M.adjust_nutrition(rand(10, 50))`, with `M.adjust_nutrition(-100)` only applied when `starving` is TRUE. When starving is FALSE (Stealth >= 2) the net effect is a nutrition GAIN of 10-50 on a symptom whose entire purpose is weight/nutrition loss (desc: 'almost unable to gain nutrition').
- **code/datums/diseases/lycancoughy.dm:14, 56-65** [behavior-changing dead code] — `var/barklimit` is declared with no initializer and is never assigned a positive value anywhere in the codebase (only `barklimit--` at line 64). It is therefore always null/0, making `if(!barklimit)` (line 56) always true, so the `else` branch (lines 59-65) that actually makes the host cough up puppies/plushies is unreachable dead code — yet the desc claims 'subject will regurgitate... puppies.'
- **code/datums/diseases/lycancoughy.dm:32, 36, 39, 44, 46, 50, 54, 58, 65** [null-deref risk] — `var/obj/item/organ/external/stomach = H.organs_by_name[pick(BP_TORSO, BP_GROIN)]` (lines 32, 46) is used without a null check. If the human is missing that limb (dismembered torso/groin), the lookup returns null and the subsequent unconditional `stomach.take_damage(...)` calls inside the prob() blocks will runtime.
- **code/datums/gear/loadout_eyes.dm:118-124** [copy-paste bug] — /datum/gear/eyes/janitor (the Contaminant HUD) builds its selector list with "standard Contaminant HUD"=/obj/item/clothing/glasses/hud/health (a Medical HUD path), not /obj/item/clothing/glasses/hud/janitor. The prescription entry correctly uses .../hud/janitor/prescription.
- **code/datums/gear/loadout_suit.dm:688 and 825-826** [duplicate definition / dead code] — /datum/gear/suit/snowsuit/medical is defined twice. The first (line 688) sets path and an allowed_roles list including JOB_SEARCH_AND_RESCUE; the second (line 825) re-opens the same type and overrides allowed_roles to a different list (JOB_FIELD_MEDIC, no JOB_SEARCH_AND_RESCUE). Whichever compiles last wins, silently discarding the first allowed_roles.
- **code/datums/ghost_query.dm:95** [wrong-units/copy-paste] — The second confirmation prompt uses `tgui_alert_async(..., wait_time SECONDS)`. `wait_time` is already in deciseconds (default 60 SECONDS = 600), and the `SECONDS` macro multiplies by 10 again, yielding 6000 ds (10 minutes). The first prompt (line 69) correctly passes bare `wait_time`.
- **code/datums/helper_datums/events.dm:34-36** [list-mutation-during-iteration] — fireEvent() iterates `for(var/datum/event/E in event)` and, when a handler returns falsey, calls clearEvent() which does `event -= E` on the very list being iterated.
- **code/datums/managed_browsers/feedback_viewer.dm:22-27** [lifecycle/use-after-qdel] — In /datum/managed_browser/feedback_viewer/New(): when the permission check fails it calls `qdel(src)` but does not return, then falls through to `..()`, running parent New() on an object that has already been queued for deletion.
- **code/datums/observation/stat_set.dm:28-31** [runtime/null-deref] — In /mob/living/set_stat(), when the mob's loc is a belly the code does `var/obj/belly/ourbelly = src.loc; if(!ourbelly.owner.client)` — it dereferences `ourbelly.owner.client` without first checking that `owner` is non-null. A belly's `owner` is set from `loc` and is explicitly nulled in belly Destroy() (belly_obj.dm line 314) and guarded elsewhere in that file with `if(!owner)` / `QDELETED(owner)`, proving it can be null.
- **code/datums/position_point_vector.dm:161-165** [wrong-variable] — /datum/point/vector/set_angle(new_angle) guards with `if(isnull(angle)) return` then sets `angle = new_angle`. It checks the existing member `angle` (which defaults to 0 and is never null) instead of the incoming `new_angle`. Compare the sibling proc set_speed(), which correctly checks the incoming arg (`if(isnull(new_speed) ...)`).
- **code/datums/uplink/announcements.dm:81** [copy-paste bug] — `medical.fields["b_dna"] = random_medical_record.fields["b_type"]` copies the blood TYPE into the b_dna field (should almost certainly read `random_medical_record.fields["b_dna"]`). Line 80 already correctly copies b_type into b_type.
