- **code/game/antagonist/alien/borer.dm:63** [runtime / pick-on-empty-list] — When no valid host is found, place_mob() does `borer.forceMove(get_turf(pick(get_vents())))`. get_vents() can return an empty list (it only includes vents whose atmos network has >50 normal members), and pick() on an empty list runtimes.
- **code/game/antagonist/antagonist_helpers.dm:21-22** [null-deref] — In can_become_antag(), the avoid_silicons branch reads `player.current.client.prefs` with no guard that player.current (or its client) is non-null; the earlier `if(player.current)` guard at line 2 does not cover this code path.
- **code/game/antagonist/antagonist_update.dm:86-90** [null-deref] — update_current_antag_max() guards `SSticker && SSticker.mode` for the antag_tags block (line 86), but then unconditionally reads `SSticker.mode.antag_scaling_coeff` at line 90 outside that guard.
- **code/game/antagonist/station/changeling.dm:27** [null-deref risk] — In remove_antagonist(), after `. = ..()`, the code calls `player.current.GetComponent(...)` with no null check on player.current. The sibling cultist.dm guards similar access with `if(player.current && ...)` (cultist.dm:112,119), and print_player_full here also assumes player.current non-null (line 97).
- **code/game/birthday.dm:6** [null-deref risk] — consider_birthday() dereferences `client.prefs` directly (`real_name != client.prefs.read_preference(...)`) with no null check on client, even though the rest of the proc and birthday() use the null-safe `client?.prefs` form (e.g. line 41).
- **code/game/gamemodes/cult/runes.dm:1091-1106** [null-deref / missing istype before subtype access] — armor() does `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = user` with no istype() check, then immediately calls human-only procs (H.equip_to_slot_or_del, H.put_in_hands). If a non-human cultist (e.g. a construct/simple_mob, which can be cultists) invokes the armor rune, H is a mis-cast and the equip calls runtime.
- **code/game/gamemodes/events/dust.dm:10, 28, 55** [null-deref risk / off-by-one] — dust_swarm(strength, list/affecting_z) defaults affecting_z to null. Line 55 `if(!affecting_z.len)` dereferences .len on a possibly-null list (runtime if called without affecting_z, as the legacy `dust_swarm("weak")` call did). Separately, `for(var/i = 0 to numbers)` iterates numbers+1 times (off-by-one) since DM `to` is inclusive and the loop starts at 0.
- **code/game/gamemodes/events/holidays/Holidays.dm:196** [chained comparison / always-false condition] — `if(28 > DD > 20)` is parsed as `(28 > DD) > 20`: the inner comparison yields 0/1, which is never > 20, so the condition is always FALSE. The Thanksgiving (4th Thursday) branch can never execute.
- **code/game/gamemodes/meteor/meteors.dm:440** [wrong-variable / missing argument] — In `/obj/effect/meteor/emp/meatyore/meteor_effect`, `new /obj/effect/gibspawner/human` is created with no location argument, unlike every other meaty meteor_effect which passes `(get_turf(src))`.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/catalog.dm:220-221, 237-238** [null-deref risk] — In tgui_act 'refund_functions' and in attackby, `var/turf/T = get_turf(user/H)` is followed immediately by `T.z in using_map.player_levels` with no null check. If the user is not on a turf (e.g. inside another object/container), get_turf can return null and `T.z` runtimes.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/devices/tesla_armor.dm:10-90** [resource-leak / missing-Destroy] — /obj/item/clothing/suit/armor/tesla schedules `addtimer(CALLBACK(src, PROC_REF(recharge_ready), user), cooldown_to_charge)` (line 46) but defines no Destroy() override to cancel pending timers. The callback holds hard refs to both the armor (src) and the wearer (user).
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spell_objs.dm:130-134** [null-deref] — /obj/item/spell/Destroy() unconditionally calls `owner.unref_spell(src)`. `owner` is only set in Initialize when `isliving(loc)`; if a spell instance is ever created with a non-living loc, owner stays null and this line null-derefs during qdel.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/audible_deception.dm:68-70** [shared-mutable-state] — on_use_cast does `var/list/sound_options = available_sounds` (a reference, not a copy) and then, when a scepter is held, `sound_options["!!AIR HORN!!"] = ...`, permanently inserting the air-horn entry into the instance's `available_sounds` list.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/gambit.dm:55** [copy-paste / arithmetic] — `if(prob(owner.getBruteLoss() + owner.getBruteLoss() * 2))` references getBruteLoss() twice (yielding 3x brute), while the inline comment claims '20 brute means a 40% chance' which would require `getBruteLoss() * 2`. The duplicate call also means burn damage is never considered for picking a heal spell.
- **code/game/gamemodes/technomancer/spells/warp_strike.dm:43-46** [runtime/null-deref (pick on empty list)] — `tele_target = pick(valid_tele_targets)` is called before the emptiness check on line 46 (`if(!pay_energy(2000) || !tele_target)`). valid_tele_targets is built from view(turf,1) filtered by !check_density(); if the chosen target is fully surrounded by dense turfs, the list can be empty and pick() throws a runtime, aborting the cast after the click cooldown was already applied.
- **code/game/jobs/whitelist.dm:297-298** [null-deref] — is_job_whitelisted() calls `var/datum/job/job = SSjob.get_job(rank)` then immediately reads `if(!job.whitelist_only)` with no null check. If `rank` does not resolve to a job datum, `job` is null and `job.whitelist_only` runtimes.
- **code/game/machinery/CableLayer.dm:52** [null-deref] — examine() does `. += "...[cable.get_amount()]..."` without a null check on `cable`. use_cable() (lines 70-77) nulls `cable` when the coil is exhausted/qdeleted, and the layer can run with `cable` null (attack_hand at line 20 explicitly handles `!cable`).
- **code/game/machinery/ai_slipper.dm:31-34** [wrong-variable / no-op bug] — setState(enabled, uses) does `disabled = disabled` and `uses = uses`, assigning each variable to itself. The proc's body is entirely no-op: the parameter `enabled` is never used, and `uses = uses` assigns the parameter to itself (a local var shadowing the instance var) so the instance `uses` is never updated.
- **code/game/machinery/atm_ret_field.dm:100-110** [uninitialized variable / dead condition] — power_change() declares `var/oldstat` (line 100) but never assigns it the previous stat before reading it at line 107 (`if(stat != oldstat && isactive && (stat & (BROKEN|NOPOWER|EMPED)))`). `oldstat` is always null, so the `stat != oldstat` comparison is effectively always true and contributes nothing to the guard.
- **code/game/machinery/atmoalter/canister.dm:297** [colon operator on subtype member] — `var/datum/gas_mixture/thejetpack = W:air_contents` uses the forbidden `:` operator to reach a member instead of istype-casting. W was just confirmed `istype(W, /obj/item/tank/jetpack)` at line 296, so a proper cast is trivial and the `:` bypasses type checking.
- **code/game/machinery/computer/arcade.dm:46-55** [always-false condition / dead code (istype on a path)] — In prizevend(), `prizeselect = pickweight(prizes)` yields a TYPE PATH (prizes keys are typepaths). The branch `if(istype(prizeselect,/obj/random))` then tries to resolve /obj/random entries, but istype() on a path is always FALSE, so the resolve branch is dead and `new prizeselect(src.loc)` is reached for /obj/random keys.
- **code/game/machinery/computer/shuttle.dm:48** [wrong qdel/del usage] — `qdel(src.authorized)` is called on a plain /list (the authorized var is `var/list/authorized = list()`). qdel on a raw list is meaningless/incorrect; the very next line reassigns `src.authorized = list()` anyway, so the qdel is both wrong and pointless.
- **code/game/machinery/cryo.dm:78** [null-deref] — In process(), `if(abs(temperature_archived-air_contents.temperature) > 1)` reads air_contents.temperature outside the `if(air_contents)` guard (lines 73-76 are the only nested block). If air_contents is null this line runtimes; temperature_archived may also still be null on the first relevant tick.
- **code/game/machinery/embedded_controller/mapping_helpers.dm:192-200** [type-safety / wrong cast] — button/setup() sets my_device_type = /obj/machinery/access_button (line 189) but casts my_device to /obj/machinery/airlock_sensor (`var/obj/machinery/airlock_sensor/my_button = my_device`). access_button is not a subtype of airlock_sensor.
- **code/game/machinery/feeder.dm:54-65** [wrong-pattern / no user feedback] — attackby() builds chat-style messages with `. += "..."` (lines 57, 63) as if it were an examine() list, but `.` here is attackby's return value, which is never displayed. The user receives no feedback when inserting a container or when one is already present.
- **code/game/machinery/food_replicator.dm:71** [resource leak] — interact() creates `var/obj/item/reagent_containers/foodItem = new product_path` with no loc (nullspace) purely to read `foodItem.reagents.total_volume`, then on line 92 creates a second instance `new product_path(src)`. The first throwaway instance is never qdel'd.
- **code/game/machinery/pandemic.dm:278-285** [inconsistent-index / null-deref risk] — create_vaccine_bottle(index) uses the raw `params["index"]` directly as a disease id: `var/id = index; var/datum/disease/disease = GLOB.archive_diseases[id]` and then `bottle.name = "[disease.name] vaccine"`. The culture path (create_culture_bottle) instead resolves the id via `get_virus_id_by_index(text2num(index))`. get_by_index("resistances", id) is likewise passed the raw param.
- **code/game/machinery/pointdefense.dm:100-103** [wrong-variable] — In pointdefense_control attackby multitool branch, the duplicate-controller check loops and compares PC.id_tag == id_tag (the controller's current/old tag) instead of new_ident (the tag being assigned). The warning string also references the new network.
- **code/game/machinery/requests_console.dm:202** [unvalidated index / runtime] — tgui_act 'print' does message_log[text2num(params["print"])] with an unvalidated client-supplied index. A missing/non-numeric/out-of-range value (text2num -> null/0 or > len) throws an out-of-bounds list runtime.
- **code/game/machinery/requests_console.dm:244-253** [duplicate global entry / state] — In attackby's multitool branch (re-tagging the department), the code does GLOB.allConsoles += src even though src was already added in Initialize() (line 65) and is never removed before re-adding. This adds a duplicate reference to the same console in the global list.
- **code/game/machinery/suit_storage/suit_cycler.dm:352-359** [null-deref] — tgui_act 'dispense' does helmet.forceMove(...) / suit.forceMove(...) based on the client-supplied params["item"] with no null check. If the requested slot is already empty (helmet or suit is null) the forceMove call runtimes.
- **code/game/machinery/suit_storage/suit_storage.dm:291-296** [resource leak] — In start_UV's super-UV ('destroy everything') branch, HELMET/SUIT/MASK are set to null without qdel(). The objects still reside in the unit's contents (loc == src) but lose their only named references, becoming orphaned until the machine itself is deleted.
- **code/game/machinery/telecomms/machine_interactions.dm:330-332** [unvalidated index / runtime] — tgui_act 'unlink' does if(text2num(params["unlink"]) <= length(links)) then links[text2num(...)]. text2num of bad input yields null (treated as 0 in the comparison, which passes) and the lower bound is never checked, so links[0] or links[null] can be indexed out of bounds.
- **code/game/machinery/telecomms/machine_interactions.dm:357-365** [null-deref] — tgui_act 'buffer' (P.buffer = src) and 'flush' (P.buffer = null) dereference P (the multitool from get_multitool) without the if(P) guard that the 'link' action (line 343) uses. get_multitool can return null (e.g. a cyborg out of range or a pAI).
- **code/game/machinery/virtual_reality/vr_console.dm:323-326** [wrong-variable / dead code] — In enter_vr()'s reconnect branch: `var/mob/living/M = loc` assigns the machine's `loc` (always a turf for an anchored vr_sleeper) to a mob var, then `if(istype(M)) M.revert_mob_tf()`. Since loc is a turf, istype(M) (as /mob/living) is always false, so the avatar's mob-transform is never reverted. The variable is almost certainly meant to be `avatar`.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/mecha_equipment.dm:264-267** [null-deref] — `occupant_message(message)` guards only `if(chassis)` then calls `icon2html(src, chassis.occupant.client)`. If the chassis has no occupant (empty mech), `chassis.occupant` is null and `.client` dereferences null.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/tools/clamp.dm:162-168** [copy-paste / missing else] — In the safety KILL CLAMP isliving branch, `if(chassis.occupant.a_intent == I_HURT)` is a standalone if, followed by `if(I_DISARM) ... else ...`. When intent is I_HURT, the first block runs (obliterate message), then the second if is false and the trailing `else` also runs (step_away + 'tosses like paper'). Both message sets fire for a single I_HURT attack.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/weapons/energy/laser.dm:19-26 and 71-78** [behavior-changing dead/duplicate code] — `/obj/item/mecha_parts/mecha_equipment/weapon/energy/laser/gamma` is defined twice in the same file (lines 19-26 'proof of concept' and lines 71-78). DM compiles both; the last-compiled definition wins, so the first block (different desc and equip_cooldown=5 vs the same value, but a different desc 'extremely effective at punching through armor') is dead/shadowed.
- **code/game/mecha/equipment/weapons/honk.dm:20-33** [wrong control flow (return vs continue)] — In the honker action() loop over `ohearers(6, chassis)`, when a human in range has ear protection (`ear_safety > 0`), the code does a bare `return` (line 25) which exits the ENTIRE proc, rather than `continue`. The first hearer who happens to have ear protection aborts processing of all remaining mobs in range.
- **code/game/mecha/mech_sensor.dm:38-55** [broken state machine / dead code] — give_feedback() declares feedback_timer as a proc-LOCAL var (var/feedback_timer = 0). The anti-spam guard `if(feedback_timer) return` at line 41 is therefore always false (the local was just initialized to 0 two lines above), and the `spawn(5 SECONDS) feedback_timer = 0` at lines 54-55 mutates a local that goes out of scope immediately. The cooldown does nothing.
- **code/game/mecha/mecha.dm:2821-2847** [security / missing permission and range re-validation in Topic] — The remove_passenger Topic branch (unlike the neighbouring req_access/maint_access/set_internal_tank_valve branches) never checks `in_range(src, usr)` and trusts `top_filter.getMob("user")` taken straight from the client-supplied href to act as the operator. It then does a do_after on that spoofable user and force-ejects a passenger.
- **code/game/mecha/mecha_control_console.dm:93** [copy-paste bug / wrong variable] — In /obj/item/mecha_parts/mecha_tracking/tgui_data, `data["cellMaxCharge"] = M.cell.charge` reads the cell's CURRENT charge instead of its max. Line 92 already set cellCharge to M.cell.charge; line 93 should read M.cell.maxcharge.
- **code/game/mecha/working/ripley.dm:44-52, 127-129** [ref leak / missing cleanup] — ripley creates `orescanner = new /obj/item/mining_scanner` in Initialize() (held only by the var, in nullspace, never added to contents/equipment), but Destroy() never qdels or nulls it.
- **code/game/objects/effects/anomalies/_anomalies.dm:49-50** [dead code / lost behavior] — In /obj/effect/anomaly/Initialize: `if(countdown_colour) countdown_colour = countdown_colour` is a self-assignment no-op.
- **code/game/objects/effects/anomalies/anomalies_dimensional.dm:111-117** [unguarded pick() / runtime] — pulse_change(count): `turf = pick(target_turfs)` in a loop with no check that target_turfs is non-empty.
- **code/game/objects/effects/buckling.dm:21-23** [missing input re-validation] — In /atom/movable/attack_hand, when multiple mobs are buckled, `tgui_input_list(...)` result `unbuckled` may be null (dialog cancelled) and is passed straight to user_unbuckle_mob(unbuckled, user); unbuckle_mob() then defaults a null arg to buckled_mobs[1].
- **code/game/objects/effects/decals/crayon.dm:12-32** [wrong-variable/dead-code] — Initialize sets `art_type = type` BEFORE the switch() randomizes the local `type` var (type = "rune[rand(1,6)]" / a random graffiti name). update_icon() then builds icon states from `art_type`, which still holds the un-randomized base value ("rune" / "graffiti"). The randomized result is discarded.
- **code/game/objects/empulse.dm:47-61** [behavior-changing dead code] — The `else if(distance == second_range)` (49-53) and `else if(distance == third_range)` (57-61) branches are unreachable: the immediately preceding `else if(distance <= second_range)` and `else if(distance <= third_range)` already match the equality case. The intended 50/50 falloff at the exact second/third range boundary never runs.
- **code/game/objects/items/devices/radio/jammer.dm:12-17** [null-deref] — is_jammed() iterates GLOB.active_radio_jammers and does `var/turf/Tj = get_turf(J)` then immediately `if(J.on && Tj.z == Tr.z)` without checking Tj for null. A jammer that is on (hence in active_radio_jammers) but currently in nullspace/contents where get_turf returns null will make `Tj.z` a null dereference.
- **code/game/objects/items/devices/tvcamera.dm:347-351** [off-by-one] — In /obj/item/TVAssembly/attackby step 2, cable is consumed twice: `if(!C.use(3))` (line 347) already consumes 3 coils when it succeeds, then line 351 calls `C.use(3)` again, consuming a total of 6. The failure message says 'You need six cable coils', so intent is ambiguous, but the success path double-spends relative to the check.
- **code/game/objects/items/robobag.dm:32** [copy-paste] — In update_icon, the istype chain checks `istype(corptag,/obj/item/clothing/accessory/badge/holo/detective)` twice (OR'd with itself). One of the two was clearly intended to be a different badge type (e.g. another gold-tier badge).
- **code/game/objects/items/sahoc.dm:101-108** [wrong qdel/del usage] — In /obj/item/daredevice/attack_self, case `if(9)` calls `qdel(src)` but the proc continues executing afterward: line 106 sets `luckynumber7`, line 107 sleeps, and line 108 calls `playsound(src.loc, ...)` on the now-qdeleted item.
- **code/game/objects/items/stacks/matter_synth.dm:25-28** [dead/broken logic] — `/datum/matter_synth/proc/emp_act(severity, recursive)` is a fresh proc on a plain /datum (it does not override /atom/emp_act). On entry `.` is null, so `if(. & EMP_PROTECT_SELF)` is always 0 and the early-return guard is dead; the proc always drains charge regardless of any protection flag.
- **code/game/objects/items/target_toy.dm:1-14** [duplicate definition / dead file] — This file and code/game/objects/items/toys/target_toy.dm are byte-for-byte identical and BOTH are #included in deepquarry.dme (lines 1732 and 4768). They redefine the same types /obj/item/storage/briefcase/target_toy, /obj/item/paper/target and its Initialize override.
- **code/game/objects/items/toys/toys.dm:1271-1280** [broken cooldown logic] — The Magic 8-Ball `attack_self` checks `if(!cooldown)` but never sets `cooldown` to a truthy value; it only calls `VARSET_IN(src, cooldown, FALSE, 3 SECONDS)`, which schedules setting it to FALSE (already its value).
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/RMS.dm:206-207** [null-deref] — In randomize(), `var/datum/material/M = get_material_by_name(initial(picked_metal.default_type))` is immediately dereferenced via `if(M.flags & MATERIAL_NO_SYNTH)` with no null check.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/candle.dm:57-66** [use-after-qdel] — In candle/process(), when wax reaches 0 it spawns trash, optionally calls dropped(), then qdel(src) — but execution continues past the qdel to update_icon() and the `if(istype(loc, /turf))` hotspot block, operating on the just-deleted candle.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/chewables.dm:288-300** [null-deref / wrong-type] — lolli/spitout() does `var/mob/living/M = loc` then dereferences `M.can_be_drop_pred`, `M.food_vore`, `M.vore_selected` whenever `victims.len` is non-zero. It does not verify loc is a mob.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/handcuffs.dm:286-289** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In place_legcuffs(), the walk-intent HUD update guards on `target.hud_used` but then dereferences `user.hud_used.move_intent` in the same condition (`if(target.hud_used && user.hud_used.move_intent)`), while the body sets `target.hud_used.move_intent.icon_state`. The condition tests the cuffer's HUD, not the cuffed mob's. The same wrong-variable pattern appears in the bola override at line 342.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/id cards/cards.dm:210-236** [copy-paste typo / dead branch] — In card_fluff/attack_self the band-colour menu offers "tan" (option list at line 210), but the dispatch chain checks `else if(bandchoice == "ran")` (line 227) before adding the "bar-tan" sprite. "tan" never matches "ran", so the tan band can never be applied.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/implants/implant.dm:447-455** [null-deref risk / missing return] — In loyalty/handle_implant, after `if(!ishuman(M)) . = FALSE` there is no `return`, so execution continues to `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = M` and `SSantag_job.get_antag_data(H.mind.special_role)`. `H.mind` is dereferenced without a null check (and for a non-human M, H is an unchecked cast).
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/implants/implantcase.dm:53-61** [dead-code/logic] — In attackby for /obj/item/implanter handling, the else branch (M.imp is empty) then checks `if (imp)` and inside it re-checks `if (M.imp) return` — but M.imp was just established as falsy by the outer else, so this inner `if(M.imp)` is always false and the guard is dead/redundant.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/implants/implantfreedom.dm:51** [null-deref] — `post_implant(mob/source)` calls `source.mind.store_memory(...)` with no null check on `source.mind`. An implanter can implant any /mob/living/carbon, including mindless mobs (no client/mind).
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/implants/implantreagent.dm:42-44** [null-deref] — `use_reagent_implant_egg()` scans the user's organs for a `/obj/item/implant/reagent_generator` into `rimplant`, then unconditionally dereferences `rimplant.empty_message`, `rimplant.full_message`, `rimplant.emote_descriptor` at lines 42-44 without a null check. If the loop finds nothing (implant removed but verb still present), rimplant is null.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/implants/implantuplink.dm:18** [null-deref] — `post_implant(mob/source)` calls `source.mind.store_memory(...)` without checking `source.mind` is non-null; also `tgui_input_list(usr, ...)` uses usr in a proc that is not a verb (post_implant is called from implanter.attack).
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/material/chainsaw.dm:110** [usr-in-non-verb] — `process()` uses `to_chat(usr, ...)` to announce the chainsaw running out of fuel. `process()` runs in the SSobj subsystem context with no usr, so usr is null or an unrelated stale value.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/stunbaton.dm:180-182** [wrong-call / arg mismatch] — `deductcharge(hitcost)` is called in `attack()` (lines 180 and 182) but `/obj/item/melee/baton/proc/deductcharge()` is declared with no parameters and uses the instance var `hitcost` internally. The passed argument is silently ignored.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/tools/weldingtool.dm:500** [null-deref risk] — In `/obj/item/weldingtool/tubefed/process()`, the first block is guarded by `if(mounted_pack)` (lines 492-498), but line 500 then dereferences `mounted_pack.loc` and line 501/505 call `mounted_pack.return_nozzle()` / `mounted_pack.reagents` unconditionally, outside that guard.
- **code/game/objects/items/weapons/weldbackpack.dm:59** [bitwise vs logical operator] — `if(T.welding & prob(50))` uses bitwise AND `&` where logical AND `&&` was intended.
- **code/game/objects/structures/alien/alien.dm:36-53** [copy-paste bug] — /obj/structure/alien/hitby() calls ..() twice: once at line 37 (right after the proc opens) and again at line 52 (near the end). The parent atom/movable/hitby() runs twice per throw impact.
- **code/game/objects/structures/barricades.dm:65, 89-92** [inverted/always-true condition] — Material comparisons use `material == (get_material_by_name(MAT_WOOD) || get_material_by_name(MAT_SIFWOOD))` (and the cloth/syncloth variant). The `||` evaluates to the first truthy material datum, so the right-hand side is just `get_material_by_name(MAT_WOOD)` (or MAT_CLOTH); the second material is never compared.
- **code/game/objects/structures/bedsheet_bin.dm:223** [always-false condition] — In /obj/structure/bedsheetbin/update_icon(), the half-full branch is `else if(amount <= (amount / 2))`, which is always false for any positive `amount` (amount is never <= half of itself).
- **code/game/objects/structures/crates_lockers/__closets.dm:561-573** [wrong-variable / type-confused condition] — In the hidden_vore() verb, after building `var/list/targets = list()`, the empty check is `if(targets == 0)`. A list is never == 0 (an empty list compares falsy in boolean context but not equal to the number 0), so this branch never fires.
- **code/game/objects/structures/crates_lockers/largecrate_yw.dm:244-247, 256-260** [wrong-variable / wrong type path (dead override)] — The randomizing Initialize overrides are defined on `/obj/structure/largecrate/animal/otie/guardbeast/Initialize` (line 244) and `/obj/structure/largecrate/animal/otie/guardmutant/Initialize` (line 256), but the actual mapped/usable crate types are `/obj/structure/largecrate/animal/guardbeast` (line 237) and `/obj/structure/largecrate/animal/guardmutant` (line 249) — note the missing `/otie/` segment. The Initialize is attached to a different, never-used type path.
- **code/game/objects/structures/extinguisher.dm:79-89** [copy-paste/logic bug] — In update_icon(), the suffix selection uses three separate `if` statements instead of an if/else chain. A mini extinguisher matches `if(istype(has_extinguisher, /obj/item/extinguisher/mini)) suffix = "mini"`, but then falls into the next block `if(istype(.../atmo)) ... else suffix = "standard"`. Since a mini is not an /atmo, the `else` overwrites suffix back to "standard".
- **code/game/objects/structures/gargoyle.dm:262** [logic bug / dead code] — `if(istype(W,/obj/item/grab || /obj/item/holder))` applies `||` to two type-path constants. `/obj/item/grab` is truthy, so the expression evaluates to `/obj/item/grab` and the `/obj/item/holder` arm is never considered.
- **code/game/objects/structures/ghost_pods/unified_ghost_hole.dm:115-127** [TOCTOU / null-deref after sleeping input] — create_lurker() reads `M.client.prefs.default_slot` (line 117) and later `M.client.prefs.copy_to(...)` (line 127) without re-validating M.client. It is reached from attack_ghost() after a sleeping `tgui_alert` confirmation; the ghost can disconnect during the prompt, nulling M.client.
- **code/game/objects/structures/morgue.dm:342-351, 281** [wrong-argument] — The crematorium igniter button calls `C.cremate(user)` with a single arg, but cremate is `cremate(atom/A, mob/user)`. This binds A=user and leaves user=null inside cremate, so the access-denied / nuclear-disk `to_chat(user, ...)` warnings go to null.
- **code/game/objects/structures/props/machines.dm:58, 313** [wrong-type-path/dead code] — Two members are declared under the wrong (phantom) type path that lacks the `/machine/` segment: line 58 `/obj/structure/prop/nt_biocan/change_state` and line 313 `/obj/structure/prop/nt_optable/starts_active`. The real types are /obj/structure/prop/machine/nt_biocan (line 54) and /obj/structure/prop/machine/nt_optable (line 297).
- **code/game/objects/structures/signs.dm:1555** [resource / raw icon allocation] — /obj/structure/sign/flag declares var/icon/ripped_outline = icon('icons/obj/flags.dmi', "ripped") as an instance var, allocating a raw /icon object on every flag instance. The var is never read anywhere (rip() builds its own local icon/mask instead).
- **code/game/objects/structures/signs.dm:1998-2006** [behavior-changing dead code (duplicate type def)] — /obj/structure/sign/small/nosmoking is defined twice. The second definition (DESIGNATED SMOKING AREA) overrides the first (NO SMOKING), so the NO SMOKING small sign is unreachable.
- **code/game/objects/structures/simple_doors.dm:282-298** [dead/unreachable code] — /obj/structure/simple_door/uranium defines proc radiate() (marked SIGNAL_HANDLER) but it is never registered to any signal nor called. The uranium subtype starts SSobj processing but does not override process(), so the inherited base process() runs dq_material_radioactivity(material) instead; radiate() never fires.
- **code/game/objects/structures/target_stake.dm:9-53** [missing Destroy cleanup (ref leak)] — /obj/structure/target_stake holds var/obj/item/target/pinned_target but has no Destroy() to null it. If the stake is qdel'd while a target is pinned, the pinned_target reference is left dangling on the deleted stake.
- **code/game/objects/structures/tyr_project_props.dm:33-74** [null/out-of-bounds risk (map-dependent)] — /obj/structure/door/.../keypad declares var/list/code = list() (empty) and never populates it on the base keypad type. check_input() indexes code[i] for i in 1..codelen (6).
- **code/game/objects/structures/watercloset.dm:153** [logic/operator-precedence] — In /obj/structure/toilet/attackby grab handling: `if(!GM.loc == get_turf(src))` parses as `(!GM.loc) == get_turf(src)`. Since GM.loc is a truthy ref, `!GM.loc` is FALSE, and `FALSE == get_turf(src)` is always FALSE, so the guard never fires.
- **code/game/objects/structures/watercloset.dm:427** [logic/operator-precedence] — In /obj/structure/urinal/attackby grab handling: `if(!GM.loc == get_turf(src))` parses as `(!GM.loc) == get_turf(src)`, which is always FALSE for a placed mob, so the position check never triggers.
- **code/game/turfs/flooring/flooring_premade.dm:535** [resource/eager-list] — /turf/simulated/floor/snow declares `var/list/crossed_dirs = list()` as an eager per-instance list. Snow turfs can cover large outdoor areas and most never get a footprint, so the list is usually empty.
- **code/game/turfs/simulated/outdoors/snow.dm:7** [resource/memory] — `/turf/simulated/floor/outdoors/snow` declares `var/list/crossed_dirs = list()` as an eager per-instance list. Snow turfs are mass-placed across whole outdoor/cave z-levels, so every snow tile (and its subtypes like `/snow/sif/planetuse`) allocates a list at init even though it stays empty until someone actually walks on the tile and a footprint dir is recorded.
- **code/game/turfs/simulated/underwater.dm:129-140** [runtime/dead-code] — `/turf/simulated/floor/water/underwater/indoors` declares an instance var `var/atom/movable/weather_visuals/visuals` (line 133), but `handle_water_icons()` (line 135-140) re-declares a local `var/atom/movable/weather_visuals/visuals = new(null)` that shadows it. The instance var is therefore never assigned; the created weather_visuals movable is only referenced via `vis_contents` and the instance var stays null forever.
- **code/game/turfs/unsimulated/sky.dm:54-63** [runtime/null-deref] — /turf/unsimulated/floor/sky/do_fall() picks a destination z with `pick(skyfall_levels)`. If skyfall_levels is empty/unset, pick() returns null, `locate(rand(...),rand(...),null)` returns null, and `candidate.density` on the next line dereferences null. Initialize() only logs an error for an empty skyfall_levels but does not disable does_skyfall.
- **code/modules/admin/admin_tools.dm:104** [wrong-target / dead feedback] — In /datum/player_log_viwer/refresh_data(), on DB query failure it calls `to_chat(src, span_admin("Database query error"))` where `src` is the /datum/player_log_viwer datum, not a mob or client.
- **code/modules/admin/cataloguer_panel.dm:61** [unvalidated href / type confusion] — tgui_act 'show_data' does `displayed_data = locate(params["ref"])` with no type validation. The cataloguer is a player-usable item, so the ref comes from the client. The subsequent tgui_data then reads displayed_data.cataloguers/.name/.value/.visible assuming a /datum/category_item/catalogue.
- **code/modules/admin/verbs/entity_narrate.dm:140-146** [missing return / null-deref after cleanup] — In narrate_mob_args, after resolving the weakref, if `selection` is null the code deletes the name/ref from the holder lists but does NOT return. Execution continues to `if(isliving(selection))` / `else if(istype(selection, /atom))` with a null selection. Also earlier at line 135 `if(!holder.entity_refs[name])` warns but does not return, so a missing name still proceeds to read the (null) weakref.
- **code/modules/admin/verbs/get_player_status.dm:14, 30** [null-deref risk] — Iterates `GLOB.player_list` as `var/mob/living/player` and unconditionally dereferences `player.client.inactivity` (line 14) and again `player.client.inactivity` (line 30). player_list can contain mobs whose client has just disconnected/nulled.
- **code/modules/admin/verbs/playsound.dm:181-182** [null-deref risk] — In /proc/web_sound the playback loop does `var/client/C = M.client` then immediately `if(C.prefs?.read_preference(...))` without a null check on C. Other loops in the same file (stop_sounds at 219-220) correctly null-guard the client.
- **code/modules/blob2/overmind/powers.dm:25-27** [missing return / permission re-validation] — createSpecial() checks `if(B.overmind != src)` and emits the warning "This blob isn't controlled by you." but does NOT return, so execution falls through and the blob is still converted via change_to() at line 41 (the only further gates are an istype/normal check and cost).
- **code/modules/casino/casino.dm:499-502** [use-after-qdel] — insert_chip qdel(cashmoney) on line 501 then calls cashmoney.update_icon() on line 502 (same pattern at 831-834). The icon update targets the just-deleted object.
- **code/modules/casino/casino_prize_vendor.dm:20-26** [wrong-variable] — /datum/data/casino_prize/New(name, path, amt, cost, restriction) sets `src.category = category`, but `category` is not a parameter of New(); it reads the instance var (always null at this point) and assigns it to itself.
- **code/modules/casino/casino_prize_vendor.dm:375-389** [state-machine] — casino_prize_dispenser/vend(): the /obj/item/stack branch `return TRUE` at line 381 returns before `currently_vending = null` (line 387) and before use_power/flick run. After vending a stack prize, currently_vending stays set, which makes attackby() treat the machine as still awaiting payment.
- **code/modules/casino/slots.dm:117-120** [use-after-qdel] — In insert_chip(), when the chip's worth drops to <=0 the code does `user.drop_from_inventory(cashmoney)`, `qdel(cashmoney)`, then `cashmoney.update_icon()` on the just-qdeleted object.
- **code/modules/casino/slots.dm:178-180, 409-411** [dead-code] — Both slot machines check for `symbol1/2/3 == "watermelon"` to award a win, but the rand()->symbol switch tables (slot1/slot2/slot3) can only ever produce cherry, lemon, bell, four leaf clover, seven, diamond, or platinum coin. "watermelon" is never assigned to any symbol var.
- **code/modules/casino/slots.dm:348-351** [use-after-qdel] — Same pattern in station_slot_machine.insert_cash(): `qdel(cashmoney)` on line 350 is immediately followed by `cashmoney.update_icon()` on line 351.
- **code/modules/catalogue/atoms.dm:22-24** [null-deref] — In /atom/proc/can_catalogue(), `var/datum/category_item/catalogue/item = GLOB.catalogue_data.resolve_item(t)` is followed immediately by `if(!item.visible)` with no null/istype guard. resolve_item() (catalogue_data.dm) returns null when no group has an item matching the type's name.
- **code/modules/client/preferences/_pref_sanitizers.dm:224-231** [toctou] — /datum/preference/gear_list/sanitize() iterates `for(var/gear_name in active_gear_list)` while removing entries from active_gear_list (`active_gear_list -= gear_name`) inside the loop, without iterating a Copy().
- **code/modules/client/preferences/types/game/adult.dm:7-9** [null-deref] — tummy_sprites/apply_to_client_updated calls `client.mob.recalculate_vis()` with no null guard on `client.mob`. Every other apply_to_client override in this preference module (misc.dm ambient_occlusion/status_indicators, ui_style.dm, sound.dm) uses the defensive `client?.mob?...` form.
- **code/modules/client/preferences_tgui.dm:108-115** [missing return / behavior bug] — In tgui_act 'resetslot' (and similarly 'copy' at 118-123), when `!isnewplayer(ui.user)` the code warns 'You can't change your character slot while being in round.' but does not return; it then proceeds to the reset/copy confirmation flow and actually performs the reset anyway.
- **code/modules/clothing/accessories/accessory.dm:800-814** [null-deref] — /obj/item/clothing/accessory/collar/shock/receive_signal resolves M from loc/loc.loc; if the collar is not on/in a mob, M stays null, yet line 814 calls `M.Weaken(10)` unconditionally when on==TRUE.
- **code/modules/clothing/accessories/badges.dm:300-301** [missing cleanup / dangling processing] — /obj/item/clothing/accessory/dosimeter/Destroy() only does `return ..()`. The dosimeter calls START_PROCESSING(SSobj, src) in Initialize and may still be processing when qdeleted, but Destroy() never STOP_PROCESSING. It also holds current_film without nulling.
- **code/modules/clothing/accessories/clothing.dm:95-111** [copy-paste/typo bug] — `/obj/item/clothing/accessory/hawaiian_random` (line 95) has no Initialize that randomizes it. The randomizing Initialize procs are written for `/obj/item/clothing/accessory/hawaii/random` (line 98) and `/obj/item/clothing/accessory/hawaii/random_flower` (line 104) — note the base type is misspelled `hawaii` instead of `hawaiian`. These define subtypes of a nonexistent intended base, so `hawaiian_random` never picks a color and the misnamed types are unreachable from the intended spawn path.
- **code/modules/clothing/glasses/glasses.dm:367-369** [wrong API call in examine] — /obj/item/clothing/glasses/sunglasses/bigshot/examine does `. += to_chat(user, span_notice("Alt-click to toggle modes."))`. This both sends an out-of-band chat message every time the item is examined and appends to_chat()'s (meaningless) return value to the examine list instead of appending the span_notice string.
- **code/modules/clothing/glasses/glasses.dm:367-369** [wrong API call in examine] — /obj/item/clothing/glasses/sunglasses/bigshot/examine does `. += to_chat(user, span_notice("Alt-click to toggle modes."))`. This both sends an out-of-band chat message every time the item is examined and appends to_chat()'s (meaningless) return value to the examine list instead of the intended span_notice string.
- **code/modules/clothing/head/misc_special.dm:227-233** [dead code / wasted allocation] — `/obj/item/clothing/head/kitty/update_icon()` builds two new raw `/icon` objects (Blend with hair color, overlay kittyinner) but never assigns or returns the result, so the work is discarded. It also builds raw /icon objects rather than caching appearances.
- **code/modules/clothing/shoes/magboots.dm:67-79** [wrong-type / null-deref] — `mob_can_equip(mob/user, ...)` casts `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = user` with no istype check, then reads `H.shoes`. If a non-human mob attempts to equip (the base proc parameter is a generic mob), `.shoes` does not exist on that type and runtimes.
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/rig/modules/modules.dm:275** [broken logic / no-op] — In stat_rig_module Click() 'select_charge_type' branch: `module.charge_selected = module.charges[module.charges.Find(module.charge_selected)]`. `charges` is an assoc list keyed by short_name strings; `.Find(charge_selected)` returns the index of that key and `charges[index]` returns the same key string. This reassigns charge_selected to itself instead of cycling to the next charge (the AddHref path does cycle correctly).
- **code/modules/clothing/spacesuits/void/void.dm:65** [behavior-changing dead code / examine pollution] — In examine(): `. += to_chat(user, span_notice("Alt-click to relase Tank/Cooling unit if installed."))`. to_chat() returns null, so this appends a null entry to the examine output list `.` while also separately pushing the line to chat via to_chat. Other lines in the same proc append plain strings to `.` (the intended examine mechanism).
- **code/modules/clothing/suits/reactive_armour_dimensional_themes.dm:65-66** [null-deref risk] — place_barrier() dereferences material.name unconditionally (`new barricade(source, material.name)`). The base /datum/armour_dimensional_theme and the /safe and /dangerous intermediate types do not set `material`. This only avoids a null-deref because apply_random() (line 11-19) always picks a concrete leaf subtype (natural/snow/radioactive/phoron) that sets material.
- **code/modules/clothing/under/extrauniforms_yw.dm:243-249** [missing-return/null-deref] — /obj/item/clothing/under/yw/victsuit/get_worn_icon_file() returns the grey uniform icon only when (body_type == SPECIES_GREY_YW && !inhands). When body_type == SPECIES_GREY_YW AND inhands is truthy, neither inner branch returns and the outer else (which calls ..()) is not reached, so the proc returns null. The parent /obj/item/get_worn_icon_file never returns null.
- **code/modules/clothing/under/solgov.dm:234-235** [copy-paste/comment bug] — Line 234 `//obj/item/clothing/under/solgov/utility/army/command` is a commented-out type-path (the leading `/` is part of the `//` comment), but the tab-indented `starting_accessories = list(/obj/item/clothing/accessory/solgov/department/command/army)` on line 235 is NOT commented out. In DM, that indented var assignment binds to the most recently opened type block, which is `/obj/item/clothing/under/solgov/utility/army` (defined at line 228).
- **code/modules/combat_ai/ports/slime_state.dm:173** [dead/unreachable code (behavior regression)] — In on_hear_say()'s "stop" command handling, the leader-follow branch tests `if(can_command(speaker) == SLIME_COMMAND_FRIEND || leader == speaker)`. But can_command() (lines 106-115) can only ever return SLIME_COMMAND_OBEY (1), SLIME_COMMAND_FACTION (2), or FALSE — it never returns SLIME_COMMAND_FRIEND (3, defined in code/__defines/mobs.dm:437). So the `can_command(speaker) == SLIME_COMMAND_FRIEND` sub-condition is permanently false and unreachable.
- **code/modules/communicator/exonet_log_panel.dm:3-41** [resource/memory (ref leak)] — /mob/observer/dead caches a /datum/exonet_log_panel in dq_exonet_log_panel_cache (created in show_text_messages), but neither /mob/observer/dead/Destroy() (defined in communicator.dm:364 and observer.dm:543) ever qdel()s it. The panel datum holds a back-reference to the ghost (host) and is only nulled-out from its own Destroy(), which is never invoked.
- **code/modules/customitems/item_spawning.dm:50-64** [always-true condition / dead code] — `apply_to_item(obj/item/item)` does `if(!istype(item))` where `item` is already declared `/obj/item`. `istype(item)` with no type arg tests against the declared type `/obj/item`, which is true for every non-null obj/item; and `!item` was already returned at line 43-44. So `!istype(item)` is always FALSE and the simple-icon branch at lines 51-53 is unreachable. The intended check was almost certainly `istype(item, /obj/item/clothing)` (the original pattern: clothing items get inhand-inheritance handling, non-clothing items just get a flat icon/icon_state).
- **code/modules/detectivework/tools/rag.dm:222-241** [use-after-qdel / missing return] — In /obj/item/reagent_containers/glass/rag/process(), the first branch `if(!can_ignite()) extinguish()` has no `return` after it. extinguish() (lines 207-220) can `qdel(src)` when `burn_time <= 5`. After that qdel, process() continues to `if(isliving(loc))` (loc may now be null) and then `reagents.remove_reagent(...)` / iterating `reagents.reagent_list` on a qdeleted object whose reagents may be gone.
- **code/modules/emotes/definitions/audible.dm:82-86** [wrong-variable] — The /datum/decl/emote/audible/beep definition sets `emote_message_3p = "You beep."` on line 84 and then immediately overwrites it with `emote_message_3p = "beeps."` on line 85. Line 84 was clearly meant to be `emote_message_1p`. The first assignment is dead and the first-person message is lost.
- **code/modules/emotes/definitions/audible_snap.dm:35-36** [null-deref] — In /datum/decl/emote/audible/snap/do_extra, `bad_luck = unlucky_human.GetComponent(/datum/component/omen)` may return null (a TRAIT_UNLUCKY human need not carry an omen component), but the next line `if(bad_luck.evil)` dereferences it with no null guard. The comment even says 'going to make sure they got the EVIL version' yet no existence check is done.
- **code/modules/emotes/definitions/synthetics.dm:82-89** [wrong-variable] — In /datum/decl/emote/audible/synth/ding, line 84 `emote_message_1p = "You ding at TARGET!"` is immediately overwritten by line 85 `emote_message_1p = "You ding."`. Line 84 was meant to be `emote_message_1p_target`. The targeted first-person message is silently dropped, so emote_message_1p_target is never set despite a 3p_target existing.
- **code/modules/emotes/definitions/synthetics.dm:91-98** [wrong-variable] — In /datum/decl/emote/audible/synth/microwave, line 93 `emote_message_1p = "You make microwave noises at TARGET!"` is overwritten by line 94 `emote_message_1p = "You make microwave noises."`. Line 93 was meant to be `emote_message_1p_target`; the targeted first-person message is lost.
- **code/modules/event/event_container.dm:88, 90, 112** [duplicate assoc-list keys] — Several event_meta role-weight/min_jobs lists contain duplicate keys: line 88 `DEPARTMENT_ANY = 15 ... DEPARTMENT_ANY = 2`; line 90 `DEPARTMENT_MEDICAL = 50, DEPARTMENT_MEDICAL = 2`; line 112 passes `min_jobs = ...` twice.
- **code/modules/event/horde_infestation.dm:90-94** [null-deref] — In end(), `if(!metroid_area)` only qdels/continues when the metroid also has no turf. If the metroid has a turf but get_area() returned null, control falls through to line 94 `area_names |= metroid_area.name`, dereferencing a null metroid_area.
- **code/modules/event/metroid_infestation.dm:51-55** [null-deref] — Same pattern as horde_infestation end(): when metroid_area is null but the mob has a turf, execution falls through to line 55 `area_names |= metroid_area.name` and dereferences null.
- **code/modules/eventkit/collector_event/nukies_maker/nukies_collector.dm:21** [copy-paste/wrong-type-path] — The update_icon override is declared on the base type `/obj/structure/event_collector/update_icon()` instead of the subtype `/obj/structure/event_collector/nukies/update_icon()`. It sets icon_state to "equipment_empty"/"equipment_[current_step]".
- **code/modules/eventkit/generic_objects/generic_item.dm:61** [operator-precedence] — `if(!H.eyecheck() <= 0)` parses as `(!H.eyecheck()) <= 0` because `!` binds tighter than `<=`. The intended check is `if(H.eyecheck() <= 0)` (no eye protection). The same construct appears in generic_structure.dm line 62.
- **code/modules/events/ray_migration.dm:11** [arithmetic/dead-scaling] — `ray_cap = 1 + 1 ** severity` — `1 ** anything` is always 1, so ray_cap is always 2 regardless of severity. The trailing comment '(2, 3, ?)' shows the intent was for the cap to scale with severity, which it does not.
- **code/modules/events/rogue_drones.dm:18-19** [runtime/empty-pick] — `for(var/i=0, i<num, i++)` spawns drones via `new(get_turf(pick(possible_spawns)))`, but `possible_spawns` (filled only with landmarks named "carpspawn") is never checked for emptiness before the loop. When `num` is non-zero and no carpspawn landmarks exist, `pick(possible_spawns)` runtimes on an empty list.
- **code/modules/events/shark_migration.dm:11** [arithmetic/dead-scaling] — `shark_cap = 1 + 1 ** severity` — `1 ** severity` is always 1, so shark_cap is always 2 regardless of severity, despite the comment '(2, 3, ?)' implying severity scaling.
- **code/modules/flufftext/hallucination_events.dm:103-113** [null-deref] — remove_hallucination_item() resolves the stored client weakref `var/client/C = CW?.resolve()` then unconditionally dereferences it: `C.screen -= itm` (line 111) with no null check. CW?.resolve() returns null if the client has disconnected/logged out.
- **code/modules/food/food/cans.dm:21-22** [broken state machine / missing START_PROCESSING] — In /obj/item/reagent_containers/food/drinks/cans/attack_self, the TRAIT_UNLUCKY branch does `shaken += 10` but never calls START_PROCESSING(SSobj, src). START_PROCESSING is only called in the separate I_HURT shake branch (lines 17-18) and only guarded by `if(!shaken)`.
- **code/modules/food/food/cans.dm:40-43** [performance / process() not scaled by subsystem wait] — /obj/item/reagent_containers/food/drinks/cans/process() decrements `shaken` by a fixed 1 per call with no use of the subsystem wait / seconds_per_tick, so the settle time is tied to SSobj tick rate rather than wall-clock time.
- **code/modules/food/food/drinks/cup.dm:10-48** [dead/shadowed code (duplicate definition)] — /obj/item/reagent_containers/food/drinks/cup and its on_reagent_change() are defined here, and the SAME type plus on_reagent_change() is defined again in code/modules/food/food/drinks/drinkingglass.dm (lines 53-102). deepquarry.dme includes cup.dm (line 2764) before drinkingglass.dm (line 2765), so the drinkingglass.dm version wins and this file's on_reagent_change() (the one that calls ..()) is completely shadowed/dead.
- **code/modules/food/food/snacks.dm:1566** [dead/stray type definition] — Line 1566 declares `/obj/item/reagent_containers/food/snacks/ber` as a bodiless type, immediately before the berryclafoutis/berry Initialize. It appears to be a truncated/typo leftover of `berryclafoutis`.
- **code/modules/food/food/snacks.dm:4229** [broken asset reference] — In /obj/item/pizzabox/update_icon() the tag overlay is built with `image("food.dmi", icon_state = "pizzabox_tag")`, passing a bare string "food.dmi" rather than the icon file resource `'icons/obj/food.dmi'` used elsewhere in the proc.
- **code/modules/food/food/snacks.dm:5152-5154** [resource/state leak] — apply_coating() permanently raises `reagents.maximum_volume` (`reagents.maximum_volume += extra`) to make room for the batter coating, and never restores it.
- **code/modules/food/food/snacks.dm:7435-7448** [wrong-variable copy-paste bug] — /obj/item/reagent_containers/food/snacks/canned/tomato sets `package_open_state = "tomato-open"`, but it is a canned item (inherits `canned = TRUE`). uncan() only reads `canned_open_state`, which is left null here.
- **code/modules/food/food/snacks.dm:90** [usr misuse / wrong-mob / null-loc hazard] — On_Consume(mob/living/eater, mob/living/feeder) spawns `new /obj/item/reagent_containers/food/rawnutrition(usr)` using `usr` as the spawn location instead of the plumbed `eater`/`feeder` args.
- **code/modules/food/kitchen/cooking_machines/fryer.dm:158-169** [null-deref risk] — finish_cooking() does `var/datum/reagent/our_oil = oil.get_master_reagent()` and then dereferences `our_oil.id` (line 165) without a null check. get_master_reagent() returns null if the oil reservoir is empty.
- **code/modules/food/kitchen/microwave.dm:504** [operator-precedence / wrong message] — wzhzhzh() builds the audible message as `"You hear a " + audible_action ? audible_action : "[src]" + "."`. Because `+` binds tighter than `?:`, this parses as `("You hear a " + audible_action) ? audible_action : ("[src]" + ".")`. The ternary condition is therefore the always-truthy concatenated string and the result is always `audible_action`, which is null by default for most microwaves.
- **code/modules/food/kitchen/smartfridge/smartfridge.dm:171-179** [wrong-behavior / misleading message] — The gripper branch of attackby() tells the user "You use \the [B] to put \the [wrapped] into \the [src]." but never actually stocks `wrapped` (no accept_check/stock call), then returns FALSE. The item is left in the gripper.
- **code/modules/gamemaster/event2/events/command/raise_funds.dm:39, 42** [arithmetic / divide-by-zero] — end() computes `money_at_end / money_at_start` (line 39 in log text and line 42 `budget_shift = money_at_end / money_at_start`). If count_money() returned 0 at start (no station/department account funds), this divides by zero and runtimes.
- **code/modules/gamemaster/event2/events/engineering/blob.dm:139-140** [inverted condition] — In announce(), the loop tracking the worst blob uses `if(danger_level > blob_type.difficulty) danger_level = blob_type.difficulty` while danger_level starts at 0. With positive difficulty values, `0 > difficulty` is never true, so danger_level never updates and stays 0 (the comment explicitly says 'The highest difficulty is used').
- **code/modules/holodeck/HolodeckControl.dm:197-201** [uninitialized-variable / inverted-condition] — power_change() declares `var/oldstat` but never assigns it the pre-call value before invoking `..()`. The intent was to snapshot `stat` before `..()` changes it. As written, `oldstat` is always null, so `stat != oldstat` is effectively `stat != null` (true whenever stat is nonzero).
- **code/modules/holodeck/HolodeckObjects.dm:456-463** [missing return / use-after-qdel] — /obj/machinery/readybutton/attack_hand: when `get_area(src.loc)` returns null it calls `qdel(src)` but does not return, then continues with `if(eventstarted)`, `ready = !ready`, `update_icon()`, and iterates `for(... in currentarea)` over the null area on a qdeleted object.
- **code/modules/hydroponics/fruit_spawner.dm:13-15** [dead-code / wrong-logic] — Initialize locates a closet on the turf (`var/obj/structure/closet/C = locate() in T`) then calls `S.harvest(T || C, 0, 0, 1)`. Since `T` (get_turf(src)) is essentially always non-null, `T || C` always evaluates to `T`; the closet `C` is computed but never used.
- **code/modules/hydroponics/seed_mobs.dm:12-22** [use-after-sleep / null-deref] — handle_living_product() schedules `spawn(75)` and then dereferences `host` (`host.ckey`, `host.client`, `host.death()`, `host.set_stat()`, `host.visible_message()`, `get_turf(host)`) without re-validating that `host` still exists / hasn't been qdel'd during the 7.5s delay.
- **code/modules/hydroponics/spreading/spreading_growth.dm:45-120** [should_not_sleep] — /obj/effect/plant/process() calls `sleep(rand(3,5))` inside the spread loop (line 110). A subsystem process() that sleeps stalls the SSplants fire and relies on the post-sleep QDELETED(src) guard for safety.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/core/integrated_circuit.dm:149-153** [null-deref risk] — In tgui_act, `pin = locate(params["pin"]) in inputs + outputs + activators` may be null (bad/missing href). If `params["link"]` is also present, line 153 does `locate(params["link"]) in pin.linked`, dereferencing pin.linked when pin is null.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/core/integrated_circuit.dm:149-153** [null-deref risk] — In tgui_act, `pin = locate(params["pin"]) in inputs + outputs + activators` may be null (bad/missing href). If `params["link"]` is also present, line 153 does `locate(params["link"]) in pin.linked`, dereferencing pin.linked when pin is null.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/subtypes/manipulation.dm:83** [logic/dead-code] — The 'banned closet on same turf' guard uses (/obj/structure/closet in range(0, our_position)) — a type path tested for membership in a list of atoms, which is always FALSE.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/subtypes/output.dm:56-59** [null-deref risk / inverted ternary] — screen/large/do_work() uses `var/obj/O = assembly ? loc : assembly`. When `assembly` is null this evaluates to `assembly` (null), and the next line calls `O.visible_message(...)`, dereferencing null. The medium screen above (line 45) correctly uses `assembly ? assembly : src`.
- **code/modules/integrated_electronics/subtypes/smart.dm:251-261** [dead/unreachable copy-paste code] — pathfinding_locomotion/do_work() ends with copy-pasted leftover logic from a different (gated) circuit. After the real movement logic already returned its activate_pin calls, it does `pull_data()` then reads `get_pin_data(IC_INPUT, 2)` even though this circuit only declares input pin 1 ("target"). The `if(enabled_input)` branch overwrites OUTPUT 1 with input pin 1 and fires activate_pin(3) — but enabled_input is always null (no pin 2), so the branch is unreachable; the else branch just re-runs push_data().
- **code/modules/library/lib_machines.dm:557, 569** [type-safety (: operator)] — bookbinder/attackby() reads `b.dat = O:info` and `b.pages = O:pages` using the `:` operator to reach paper/paper_bundle members on a generically-typed `obj/item/O`. The CLAUDE.md standard forbids `:` on subtype access in favor of istype()+cast.
- **code/modules/looking_glass/lg_area.dm:44, 55** [null-deref] — Initialize only logs when our_landmark is missing (`if(!our_landmark) log_mapping(...)`), but begin_program() does `our_landmark.take_image(newimage)` and end_program()'s spawned block does `our_landmark.drop_image()` with no null guard (contrast Entered/Exited which use `our_landmark?.`).
- **code/modules/looking_glass/lg_console.dm:108, 127-135, 153** [null-deref] — Initialize logs a mapping error but leaves my_area null when the console isn't inside a matching looking_glass area (`if(!istype(my_area)) log_mapping(...)` with no return/disable). tgui_act "immersion" calls `my_area.toggle_optional(...)`, load_program/unload_program call `my_area.begin_program/end_program`, and Destroy() calls unload_program() -> `my_area.end_program()`, all without a null/istype guard.
- **code/modules/maint_recycler/code/maint_recycler.dm:463-468, 500-503** [wrong-argument / dead parameter] — tgui_act calls `canRecycle(ui.user)` without the `potentialValue` argument, so inside canRecycle `potentialValue` is null and the cap check `granted_points[user.key]+potentialValue > point_cap` adds null (treated as 0). The intended pre-recycle cap check (does the about-to-be-earned value push the user over point_cap?) never actually accounts for the pending value.
- **code/modules/materials/materials/organic/cloth.dm:169-170** [copy-paste/wrong-variable] — /datum/material/fibers sets `sheet_singular_name = "fiber"` on line 169 and then immediately reassigns `sheet_singular_name = MAT_FIBERS` on line 170. MAT_FIBERS expands to "fibers" (the plural-looking name). The second assignment silently overrides the first.
- **code/modules/materials/sheets/organic/tanning/tanning_rack.dm:17-22** [runtime/dangling-ref] — /obj/structure/tanning_rack holds a hard ref `drying` to a /obj/item/stack/wetleather. process() dereferences drying.wetness every tick with only a truthiness check. If that wetleather stack is qdel'd elsewhere while still assigned to `drying` (set_amount(0) qdels a stack — see stack.dm set_amount), `drying` becomes a stale ref to a deleted datum and process()/examine() would runtime on access.
- **code/modules/materials/sheets/supermatter.dm:20-21** [signals/correctness] — /obj/item/stack/material/supermatter/proc/radiate() is annotated with SIGNAL_HANDLER on its first line, but it is never RegisterSignal'd to anything; it is only ever called synchronously from process() (line 17). The annotation is misleading and wrong for a proc invoked from the processing loop.
- **code/modules/media/mediamanager.dm:80** [null-deref risk] — /mob/proc/stop_all_music does `client?.media.stop_music()`. The `?.` only guards `client` being null; if `client` is non-null but `client.media` is null, `.stop_music()` is invoked on a null datum and runtimes. Other procs in this file consistently use the safer `client?.media && client.media.<x>` form (see /mob/proc/update_music at line 76).
- **code/modules/media/walkpod.dm:184** [null-deref risk / operator precedence] — tgui_data computes `data["percent"] = playing ? min(100, round(world.time - media_start_time) / current_track.duration) : 0`. When `playing` is true the expression dereferences `current_track.duration`; if current_track is ever null while playing is set, this divides by null and runtimes. Also note `round(...)` binds only `world.time - media_start_time`, so the division by duration happens outside the round (likely not the intent of a percentage).
- **code/modules/mining/drilling/drill.dm:201-202** [wrong-variable] — The harvest-clamp uses the machine's object contents length: `if(contents.len + total_harvest >= capacity) total_harvest = capacity - contents.len`. Mined ore is not stored as objects in `contents` (it is accumulated into the `stored_ore` assoc list and tracked by `current_capacity`); `contents` only holds the cell and component parts. The storage-full guard one block up correctly uses `current_capacity >= capacity`.
- **code/modules/mining/machinery/machine_unloading.dm:40-94** [wrong-variable / dead code] — The ore-box unloading loop declares `var/i = 0` and the staggering guard `if (i>=3) return`, but `i` is never incremented inside the loop, so the guard is unreachable and the 'staggered' behavior is dead. Additionally, the guard returns out of process() entirely on the first iteration if it ever triggered, rather than just breaking, which would abandon the rest of the ore types.
- **code/modules/mining/mineral_effect.dm:17-22** [null-deref] — /obj/effect/mineral/Initialize confirms loc is a mineral turf, then reads `var/datum/ore/M = min_turf.mineral` and immediately dereferences `M.display_name`, `M.name`, `M.reagent` without checking M is non-null. A mineral turf can have a null `mineral` var.
- **code/modules/mob/_modifiers/changeling.dm:43-44** [null-deref] — changeling/tick() does `var/datum/component/antag/changeling/comp = L.GetComponent(...)` then unconditionally reads `comp.chem_charges`/`comp.chem_storage`. GetComponent() can return null (component removed), and tick() does not re-validate it the way check_if_valid() does.
- **code/modules/mob/_modifiers/horror.dm:648-651** [null-deref] — equip_flesh_armor() enters its 'take off existing armor' branch if ANY of wear_suit/head/shoes/gloves matches its type, but the visible_message immediately interpolates `M.wear_suit.name` (twice). If the match was on head/shoes/gloves while wear_suit is null, `M.wear_suit.name` null-derefs.
- **code/modules/mob/dead/corpse.dm:138-139** [type-safety / : operator] — `M.w_uniform?:sensor_mode = corpsesensormode` uses the `:` operator to reach `sensor_mode`, which only exists on `/obj/item/clothing/under`, not the generic `/obj/item/clothing` type of w_uniform. If a corpse landmark ever sets corpseuniform to a non-`under` clothing path, this writes to a non-existent member (runtime) with no istype guard.
- **code/modules/mob/dead/observer/observer.dm:540-541** [resource / eager per-instance list] — `/mob { var/list/following_mobs = list() }` allocates a list on EVERY mob instance even though it is empty for the vast majority of mobs (only populated when a ghost orbits/follows them). update_following() and the ghost follow paths already guard with `for(... in following_mobs)`, which tolerates null.
- **code/modules/mob/hear_say.dm:280** [off-by-one / always-empty result] — In hear_sleep(): `if(copytext(heardword,1, 1) in punctuation)` — copytext with start=1 and end=1 always returns an empty string "" (BYOND copytext end index is exclusive). So the leading-punctuation strip never fires; it should be `copytext(heardword,1,2)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/carbon_powers.dm:8** [wrong min/max (arithmetic)] — toggle_active_cloaking() uses `alpha = max(alpha + 100, 255)` when becoming visible. max() of (alpha+100) and 255 returns at least 255 and can exceed it (alpha+100 when alpha > 155). The intent is to raise alpha toward full opacity capped at 255, which should be `min(alpha + 100, 255)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/human.dm:907** [copy-paste bug] — In morph(), after prompting for a new hair color into `new_hair`, the apply block is gated by `if(new_facial)` instead of `if(new_hair)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/species/lleill/lleill_abilities.dm:269-279** [always-true-condition] — In lleill_contact(), `var/list/targets = list()` is allocated then emptiness is tested with `if(!targets)` (line 277). A list created via list() is always truthy even when empty, so this check never fires.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/species/lleill/lleill_hud.dm:47** [null-deref] — add_lleill_ability() (defined on /atom/movable/screen/movable/ability_master) does `if(my_mob.client)` without checking my_mob is non-null. The parallel shadekin version (shadekin_hud.dm line 47) explicitly guards `if(my_mob && my_mob.client)` with a comment that mannequins reach this before my_mob is assigned and runtime otherwise.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/species/lleill/lleill_items.dm:138-146** [always-true-condition] — In /obj/item/glamour_face/attack_self(), `var/list/targets = list()` then `if(!targets)` (line 146). The list is always truthy when empty, so the 'no appropriate targets in range' guard never triggers.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/species/station/alraune.dm:392-394** [float-equality logic bug] — fruitgland.process() gates its empty/full chat warnings on exact float equality: `if(reagents.total_volume == reagents.maximum_volume * 0.05)` and `... == reagents.maximum_volume`. Reagent volumes accumulate in fractional steps (generated_reagents add 2 per tick against a 250 volume), so total_volume rarely lands exactly on `maximum_volume*0.05` (=12.5) or exactly on maximum.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/unarmed_attack.dm:69-71** [null-deref risk] — In apply_effects() BP_TORSO branch, `var/turf/T = get_step(get_turf(target), ...)` then immediately `if(!T.density)`. get_step() returns null at the map edge, so T can be null and T.density runtimes.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/update_icons.dm:471-473** [null-deref risk] — In update_hair(), `if(head && (head.flags_inv & BLOCKHEADHAIR)) { if(!(hair_style.flags & HAIR_VERY_SHORT)) ... }` dereferences hair_style.flags before hair_style is null-checked. hair_style comes from `GLOB.hair_styles_list[h_style]` and is only validated at the later `if(hair_style && ...)` on line 475.
- **code/modules/mob/living/carbon/taste.dm:65-68** [unreachable / dead code] — Intensity selection checks `if(percent > minimum_percent * 2 || percent == 100) intensity_desc = "the flavor of"` then `else if(percent > minimum_percent * 3) intensity_desc = "the strong flavor of"`. Any percent > 3x minimum also satisfies > 2x, so the first branch always wins and the `> minimum_percent * 3` ("the strong flavor of") branch is unreachable.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/ai/death.dm:15-21** [dead code / broken async] — Inside `for(var/obj/machinery/ai_status_display/O in GLOB.machines)` the line `spawn( 0 )` is on its own with no following indented block, so it spawns an empty thread. The intended body (`O.mode = 2`, the aicard istype/update_icon block) runs synchronously in the loop instead of being deferred. Additionally the `if (istype(loc, /obj/item/aicard))` block checks the AI's `loc` but sits inside the per-machine loop, so it is re-evaluated once per status display for no reason.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/ai/icons.dm:152-156** [copy-paste bug] — /datum/ai_icon/Static sets `alive_icon = "ai-static"` then on the next line `alive_icon = "ai-static-crash"`, overwriting the alive icon. The second assignment was almost certainly meant to be `dead_icon = "ai-static-crash"` (every other entry uses dead_icon for the *-crash sprite).
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/dogborg/dog_modules.dm:213-217** [wrong qdel/del usage] — In /obj/item/robot_tongue/afterattack, the food-eating branch uses `del(target)` to destroy the eaten food item, unlike every other branch in the same proc which uses qdel(target).
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/dogborg/dog_sleeper_types.dm:93** [copy-paste/mis-parenthesized condition] — In /obj/item/dogborg/sleeper/compactor/brewer/inject_chem, the chem check reads `if((chem in injection_chems) + REAGENT_ID_INAPROVALINE)`. The parentheses are wrong: it computes (0 or 1) then adds a string reagent id, instead of the intended `if(chem in (injection_chems + REAGENT_ID_INAPROVALINE))` used in the base proc (dog_sleeper.dm line 390). The brewer also has injection_chems = null.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/drone/drone_console.dm:86-100** [unvalidated tgui_act href] — The 'resync' and 'shutdown' actions do `var/mob/living/silicon/robot/drone/D = locate(params["ref"])` then dereference D.stat and call D.law_resync()/D.shut_down() without validating that the located object is actually a drone (or non-null). The ref comes from client-supplied tgui params.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_gripper_handling.dm:439** [misuse of istype (type arg is an instance)] — consolidate_stacks() compares stacks with `if(istype(stack_to_consolidate, stack))` where `stack` is a stack *instance* from the loop, not a type path. istype()'s second argument is expected to be a type path.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_items.dm:515-517** [null-deref / wrong-type assumption] — In /obj/item/borg/combat/shield/process(), the overload-recovery branch does `var/mob/living/user = src.loc` then `user.visible_message(...)` and `user.update_icon()` assuming loc is a mob. The shield lives in module.modules (loc = /obj/item/robot_module datum) until equipped onto a mob. If overload recovery fires while loc is not a mob, these calls runtime.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_modules/station.dm:367-368, 435-436, 531-532, 609-610, 841-842, 936-937** [null-deref] — Several respawn_consumable() overrides dereference the result of `locate() in src.modules` without a null check before use: surgeon/crisis/research syringe `S.mode` (367, 435, 841), security flash `F.broken` (531-532), janitor lightreplacer `LR.Charge` (609-610), and drone lightreplacer `LR.Charge` (936-937). If the located item was removed/qdel'd (e.g. dropped, decompiled, or never present in a subtype), these run a null deref each processing tick. Note the immediately-following taser/spray locates in the same procs ARE null-guarded, showing the pattern was intended.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_modules/syndicate.dm:164-165** [null-deref] — combat_medic/respawn_consumable() does `var/obj/item/reagent_containers/syringe/S = locate() in src.modules` then immediately `if(S.mode == 2)` with no null check on S.
- **code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_ui_module.dm:49** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In tgui_static_data() the crisis-mode notice for shells does `to_chat(src, span_red("Crisis mode active..."))` where `src` is the /datum/tgui_module, not a mob. The parallel non-shell branch (line 54) correctly uses `to_chat(R, ...)`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/alien animals/jellyfish.dm:117-129** [unbounded global counter drift] — GLOB.jellyfish_count is incremented in Initialize() (line 120) but only decremented in death() (line 128). Any jellyfish destroyed without going through death() (admin qdel, round cleanup, or any qdel path that doesn't route through death) leaks the counter upward; simple_mob/Destroy() does not touch it.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/alien animals/jellyfish.dm:117-129** [unbounded global counter drift] — GLOB.jellyfish_count is incremented in Initialize() (line 120) but only decremented in death() (line 128). Any jellyfish destroyed without routing through death() (admin qdel, round cleanup, gib paths that bypass death) leaks the counter upward; simple_mob/Destroy() never decrements it.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/alien animals/stardog.dm:1261-1264** [list mutation during iteration] — do_setup() iterates `for(var/obj/effect/dog_teleporter/T in dog_teleporters)` and inside the loop does `dog_teleporters -= T` (removing the current element) for stale entries.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/alien animals/succlet.dm:131-132** [behavior-changing dead code / broken delay] — death() does `spawn(25)` on its own line with no indented body, then `qdel(src)` at the same indentation as `spawn`. The spawn block is empty, so the intended 2.5-second delay before deletion is a no-op and qdel(src) runs immediately after the shriek sound.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/hyena/hyena.dm:47-48** [inverted values] — melee_damage_lower = 12 and melee_damage_upper = 6 — the lower bound is greater than the upper bound (comment says 'not as high damage'). Most other mobs keep lower <= upper.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/passive/mouse.dm:53-54** [copy-paste-bug] — `pain_emote_1p` is assigned twice in a row (lines 53 and 54); the second assignment overwrites the first. The second was almost certainly meant to be `pain_emote_3p` (it uses third-person 'squeaks'/'squiks' while the first uses first-person 'squeak'/'squik').
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/pets/cat.dm:257-268** [TOCTOU/input-revalidation] — cat/attackby naming: after the sleeping `tgui_input_text()` returns, the code re-reads neither `named` nor whether tmp_name is empty before doing `name = tmp_name; named = TRUE`. Two users naming simultaneously both pass the initial `if(named)` check, and an empty/whitespace input (which sanitizeSafe can yield) blanks the cat's name.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/sif/frostfly.dm:75-79** [resource-leak] — frostfly Initialize() creates `smoke_special = new` (a /datum/effect/effect/system/smoke_spread/frost) but there is no Destroy() override nulling/qdeling it. Same pattern in moth.dm tymisian (smoke_spore = new at line 120 with no Destroy).
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/sif/siffet.dm:50** [dead-code/logic] — In IIsAlly(), `if(!. && !istype(L, /mob/living))` can never be true: the proc argument L is already typed `/mob/living`, so `!istype(L, /mob/living)` is always FALSE for any non-null L. The intended 'treat non-mobs as ally' branch is unreachable.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/space/carp.dm:396-397** [copy-paste-bug] — In carp/station Initialize, `if(body_color == "yellow") color = "#00FF00"` assigns pure green (#00FF00) for the 'yellow' case. The other branches map names to roughly matching hues; yellow should be a yellow hex.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/bullet_heck/bullet_patterns.dm:1** [malformed proc declaration] — `/mob/living/simple_mob/mechanical/mecha/eclipse/proc/singleproj/(atom/target, next_cycle)` has a stray trailing slash after `singleproj` before the argument list (`singleproj/(...)`), unlike every other proc in the file.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/gateway/imperion.dm:568** [typo / wrong-value] — In /datum/modifier/aura/despair/tick(), `holder.adjustFireLoss(0,1)` passes two arguments (0 and 1) where the surrounding code clearly intends a single value of 0.1 (compare `holder.adjustBruteLoss(0.1)` on the line above). The comma is a typo for a decimal point.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/horror/Smiley.dm:35-49** [copy-paste / dangling type] — The bullet_act/attack_hand/hitby/attackby sound-effect procs are defined on `/mob/living/simple_mob/horror/Helix/...`, a type that is never declared anywhere in the codebase (grep confirms Helix only appears in this file). They were clearly meant to be `/mob/living/simple_mob/horror/Smiley/...`, matching the sibling files (Steve.dm, Willy.dm, bradley.dm) that correctly define these on their own type.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/mechanical/ward/monitor_ward.dm:37** [resource/memory + ref leak] — `var/list/seen_mobs = list()` is an eager per-instance list (usually small/empty) and holds direct references to living mobs. It is pruned in detect_mobs() only when a tracked mob leaves vision, but there is no Destroy() override to clear it, and a tracked mob that is qdel'd while still in view stays referenced.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/occult/constructs/artificer.dm:46-47** [dead assignment / copy-paste] — The `caster` subtype sets `projectiletype` twice on consecutive lines: `/obj/item/projectile/energy/inversion` is immediately overwritten by `/obj/item/projectile/beam/inversion`. Line 46 is dead.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/slime/slime.dm:270-272** [resource/memory (eager per-instance list)] — `var/allowed_attack_types = list(/obj/item/melee/baton/slime, /obj/item/slimepotion)` is an instance var initialized with an eager list. It is a constant lookup table (only iterated read-only in attackby), so every slime instance allocates its own identical copy.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/bigdragon.dm:931-961** [copy-paste validation bug] — In `import_style()`, the 12th style token is validated with `(input_style_list[12] in ear_styles)` but is then assigned to `eyes` (line 961). It should be validated against `eye_styles`, not `ear_styles`.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/dominated_brain.dm:40-45** [missing return after qdel] — `/mob/living/dominated_brain/Life()` does `if(!isliving(loc)) qdel(src)` immediately followed by `if(!ckey) qdel(src)` with no `return` between them; after the first qdel execution continues and can call qdel(src) a second time on the same (now deleting) object.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/gateway/candy.dm:696** [dead/garbage code / compile hazard] — The file ends with a stray lone `v` on its own line (verified byte sequence `\n\nv\n` at EOF). This is leftover garbage, not valid intended code — at root scope DM treats a bare `v` as a spurious empty path/var declaration.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/goia.dm:549** [wrong-variable / copy-paste bug] — In import_style(), the spike style is assigned with `input_style_list["spike"] = input_style_list[18]` instead of `goia_overlays["spike"] = input_style_list[18]`. It writes a string key into the numerically-indexed splittext() result list and never updates goia_overlays, so the imported spike marking style is silently dropped while every other slot (ears, spots, claws, etc.) is set on goia_overlays correctly.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/macrophage.dm:42** [wrong-variable / type mismatch] — `vore_bump_chance = "attempts to absorb"` assigns a descriptive emote string to a numeric probability var. The string clearly belongs to `vore_bump_emote`. As written the bump chance is a non-numeric value and the intended bump emote is never set (default used).
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/oregrub.dm:124-128, 141-145** [off-by-one] — Both death() ore-drop loops use `var/i = rand(min_ore,max_ore)` then `while(i>1) { ...; i-- }`, which iterates only `i-1` times. This drops one fewer ore than the rolled amount (and can drop as few as min_ore-1).
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/solargrub_larva.dm:131-135** [performance/icon-rebuild] — generate_machine_effect() builds a raw `/icon` via `new /icon(...)` plus two `Blend()` calls per machine type. While cached per `M.type` in GLOB.grub_machine_overlays, the cached value is a raw /icon used directly as an image source and broadcast per-player; raw /icon objects are heavy compared to cached appearances.
