- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/solargrub_larva.dm:90** [arithmetic/logic] — `if(machine_effect && SSair.times_fired%30)` gates re-sending the machine overlay on `times_fired % 30` being non-zero — i.e. it re-broadcasts the image to every player on 29 of every 30 ticks and SKIPS the one tick that is a multiple of 30. This is almost certainly inverted from the intent of 'roughly every 30 ticks'.
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/vore/weather.dm:249** [inverted/always-true condition] — In summon_puddles(): `if(L.stat != DEAD || !IIsAlly(L))` applies the `jelly_fish` debuff modifier. The OR makes this true for almost everyone (any non-dead mob, including allies). It looks like it was meant to target living non-allies (`L.stat != DEAD && !IIsAlly(L)`).
- **code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/xenomorph.dm:52-57** [resource/ref-leak] — Six action datums are instantiated as instance-var initializers (`var/datum/action/innate/.../build_action = new`) on every `/mob/living/simple_mob/xeno_ch`. There is no Destroy() override nulling/qdeling them, so each xeno deletion leaks six action datums (which hold a `parent_xeno` back-ref).
- **code/modules/mob/living/status_indicators.dm:67** [copy-paste/wrong-variable] — `current_x_position -= (icon_expected_width / 2) * (get_icon_scale_y() - 1)` uses the Y scale factor to compute an X-axis (horizontal) offset. The surrounding comment references the horizontal shift from update_transform() scaling, which is driven by X scale; this should almost certainly be `get_icon_scale_x()`.
- **code/modules/mob/new_player/login.dm:73-75** [dead/unreachable code (off-by-one range)] — In version_warnings(), `if(1566 to 1568)` contains `if(world.byond_build == 1569)`. 1569 is outside the 1566-1568 case range, so this inner branch can never be reached; the `problems` string for that case is never set.
- **code/modules/mob/new_player/sprite_accessories_hair.dm:235-241** [copy-paste bug] — The type path /datum/sprite_accessory/hair/bowlcut2 is defined twice. Lines 235-237 set name="Bowl 2"/icon_state="hair_bowlcut2"; lines 239-241 re-open the same path with name="Bowl, Overeye"/icon_state="hair_overeyebowl". The later block overrides the earlier one on the shared type path, so "Bowl 2" is silently dropped.
- **code/modules/mob/new_player/sprite_accessories_hair.dm:843-846 and 1006-1009** [duplicate definition] — /datum/sprite_accessory/hair/newyou is defined twice with identical content (name "New You", icon_state "hair_newyou"). The second definition (lines 1006-1009) is a redundant re-open of the same type path.
- **code/modules/mob/new_player/sprite_accessories_markings.dm:335-343** [copy-paste bug] — The type path /datum/sprite_accessory/marking/lowercheek_left is defined twice. The first block (lines 335-338) is "Lower Cheek Left" with icon_state "lowercheek_l"; the second block (lines 340-343) reuses the SAME type path lowercheek_left but sets name="Lower Cheek Right" and icon_state="lowercheek_r". In DM the second definition re-opens the same type and overrides the first, so the left-cheek variant is silently lost and only one datum exists (named "Lower Cheek Right"). The intended path was almost certainly /datum/sprite_accessory/marking/lowercheek_right.
- **code/modules/mob/say.dm:569-571** [missing return / null-deref risk] — In psay(), the `if(!isliving(src))` branch calls `say(message)` but does not return. Execution falls through to `var/mob/living/M = src` and then reads M.absorbed / M.vore_organs, which are /mob/living members not present on a non-living src type.
- **code/modules/mob/say.dm:569-571** [missing return / null-deref risk] — In psay(), the `if(!isliving(src))` branch calls `say(message)` but does not return. Execution falls through to `var/mob/living/M = src` and then reads M.absorbed / M.vore_organs, which are /mob/living members not present on a non-living src type.
- **code/modules/mob/say.dm:682-684** [missing return / null-deref risk] — In pme(), the `if(!isliving(src))` branch calls `me_verb(message)` without returning, then continues into living-only code (`var/mob/living/M = src`; M.absorbed / M.vore_organs), which can runtime for a non-living src.
- **code/modules/mob/say.dm:682-684** [missing return / null-deref risk] — In pme(), the `if(!isliving(src))` branch calls `me_verb(message)` without returning, then continues into living-only code (`var/mob/living/M = src`; M.absorbed / M.vore_organs), which can runtime for a non-living src.
- **code/modules/news/news_init.dm:12-39** [dead-code/behavior] — /datum/lore/news searches network_channels for a channel named "Vir News Network" (line 16) to set station_newspaper, but New() only creates a "Station Announcements" channel (line 4); the "Vir News Network" CreateFeedChannel call is commented out (line 5). station_newspaper therefore stays null and fill_codex_news() always early-returns with a log_runtime failure.
- **code/modules/nifsoft/nif_softshop.dm:105** [operator-precedence] — `if(!H.nif || !H.nif.stat == NIF_WORKING)` parses as `!H.nif || ((!H.nif.stat) == NIF_WORKING)`. It only behaves correctly by accident because NIF_WORKING is #defined to 0; the intent is `H.nif.stat != NIF_WORKING`.
- **code/modules/overmap/bluespace_rift.dm:1-43** [resource/ref-leak] — /obj/effect/overmap/bluespace_rift pairs two rifts via mutual `partner` refs in pair(), but has no Destroy() to null out the partner's back-reference. When one rift is qdel'd, its partner still holds a hard ref to the (deleted) rift in `partner`.
- **code/modules/overmap/ships/computers/ship.dm:96-99** [runtime/null-deref] — /obj/machinery/computer/ship/look(mob/user) reads `linked.real_appearance` without a null check on `linked`, whereas the paired unlook() (line 102) explicitly guards `if(linked && ...)`. The asymmetry indicates look() can be reached with a null `linked`.
- **code/modules/paperwork/photocopier.dm:100-141** [TOCTOU / null-deref risk] — /obj/machinery/photocopier/copy_operation sleeps (sleep(11), etc.) inside the copy loop while reading `copyitem`. The 'remove' tgui_act can null `copyitem` (and put it in the user's hands) during those sleeps; subsequent istype()/copy() calls then operate on a null/elsewhere item.
- **code/modules/paperwork/stamps.dm:114-128** [resource leak] — /obj/item/stamp/chameleon/attack_self does `new stamp_type` for every subtype of /obj/item/stamp to build a name->instance picker list, but never qdel()s the unchosen instances.
- **code/modules/pda/messenger.dm:39-41** [null-deref risk] — In /datum/data/pda/app/messenger/update_ui the loop dereferences `PM.toff` (and `PM.m_hidden`) where `PM = P.find_program(...)` may be null; the guard `if(!P.owner || PM.toff || ...)` does not null-check PM (unlike available_pdas() which guards `!PM`).
- **code/modules/pda/utilities.dm:182-185** [logic bug / lost data] — /datum/data/pda/utility/scanmode/notes/scan_atom appends padding to an existing note (`notes.note += "<br><br>"` when length>0) but the next line unconditionally overwrites it: `notes.note = formatted_scan`.
- **code/modules/power/batteryrack.dm:126-133** [null-deref risk (missing guard, inconsistent with sibling)] — remove_charge() calls `var/obj/item/cell/CL = get_most_charged_cell()` then immediately `amount -= CL.use(amount)` without a null check. get_most_charged_cell() returns null when internal_cells is empty. The sibling add_charge() (lines 112-117) explicitly guards its equivalent with `if(!CL) return`, but remove_charge() does not.
- **code/modules/power/gravitygenerator.dm:71-83** [null-deref risk] — /obj/machinery/gravity_generator/part/attackby (line 72), attack_hand (line 78) and set_broken (line 82) dereference `main_part` without a null check, while part/get_status (line 75) correctly uses `main_part?.`. When the main generator is destroyed it sets each part's `main_part = null` (line 141) before qdel'ing them, so an interaction with a lingering part whose main_part is null will runtime.
- **code/modules/power/lighting.dm:1205-1219** [resource leak / double allocation] — /obj/machinery/light/Initialize creates an emergency cell unconditionally at lines 1208-1209 (`if(start_with_cell && !no_emergency) cell = new/obj/item/cell/emergency_light(src)`), then in the non-construct else branch creates ANOTHER cell at lines 1218-1219 with the same condition, overwriting the `cell` reference without qdel'ing the first one.
- **code/modules/power/sensors/powernet_sensor.dm:203** [division-by-zero runtime] — In return_reading_text(), the grid-load percentage is computed as `round((powernet.load / powernet.avail) * 100)` with no guard for `powernet.avail == 0`. The sibling proc return_reading_data() explicitly guards this exact division (lines 261-264), but the HTML text path does not.
- **code/modules/power/singularity/act.dm:23** [wrong-argument order (copy-paste)] — In /mob/living/carbon/human/singularity_act(), the call is `investigate_log(I_SINGULO,"has been consumed by a singularity", I_SINGULO)` — the message text is in the second argument and the I_SINGULO category define is passed as the first (message) argument. Compare the correct form at line 9: `investigate_log("has been consumed by a singularity", I_SINGULO)`.
- **code/modules/power/singularity/particle_accelerator/particle.dm:47** [: operator subtype access] — `A:energy += energy` uses the `:` operator to reach the `energy` var on the bumped atom A. Although the enclosing `if` already istype-checks A against the_singularitygen / singularity / particle_smasher, the value is never cast to a typed var before the `:` access.
- **code/modules/power/supermatter/setup_supermatter.dm:218-224** [copy-paste bug] — In /obj/effect/engine_setup/smes/activate(), the output branch compares the output target against the INPUT max: `if(target_output_level > S.input_level_max)` (then sets output_level = output_level_max), whereas it should compare against `S.output_level_max`. The else branch correctly uses output_level_max.
- **code/modules/power/weather.dm:44-66 (specifically 52)** [always-true condition (type mismatch in comparison)] — In /datum/weather_holder/change_weather, `old_weather` is set to the previous current_weather (a /datum/weather DATUM), while `new_weather` is the incoming weather IDENTIFIER (a string key into allowed_weather_types). The guard `if(new_weather != old_weather)` compares a string against a datum, so it is effectively always true.
- **code/modules/projectiles/ammunition.dm:286** [wrong-API / examine misuse] — In /obj/item/ammo_magazine/ammo_box/examine(), the alt-click hint is added via `. += to_chat(user, span_notice("Alt-click to extract contents."))`. examine() should append a string to the `.` list; instead this directly to_chat()s the message (sent immediately, outside the examine block) and appends to_chat's return value (null) to the examine list.
- **code/modules/projectiles/dnalocking.dm:18-22** [runtime/null-deref] — get_dna(mob/user) casts `var/mob/living/M = user` without istype and then reads `M.dna.unique_enzymes` at line 22 before the ishuman() check on line 25. The give_dna verb is `set src in usr`, so usr could be a mob with no dna (non-carbon), making M.dna null and the .unique_enzymes access a null deref.
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/energy/cyborg.dm:178-184** [runtime/null-deref] — jaws/small/attack_self casts `var/mob/living/silicon/robot/R = user` with no istype check, then reads `R.emagged || R.emag_items` and later `R.sprite_datum.dogborg_sprites`. If attack_self is ever invoked with a user that is not a silicon robot (the parent attack_self returning falsey lets execution continue), these reads runtime; R.sprite_datum could also be null.
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/projectile/automatic.dm:174** [type-as-string] — /obj/item/gun/projectile/automatic/wt550 sets `ammo_type = "/obj/item/ammo_casing/a9mmr"` as a string literal instead of a type path. `ammo_type` is consumed in projectile.dm Initialize via `ispath(ammo_type)`, which returns false for a string, so the value is silently inert.
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/projectile/automatic_serdy.dm:1514-1522** [behavior-changing dead code] — Two distinct subtypes are both declared as `/obj/item/gun/projectile/shotgun/doublebarrel/rosewood_weathered`. The second definition (lines 1519-1522, icon dshotgunPW, variant PW) silently overrides the first (lines 1514-1517, icon dshotgunPR, variant PR), so the 'weathered rosewood' variant is unreachable and only the 'weathered oak' one exists.
- **code/modules/projectiles/guns/projectile/dartgun.dm:232** [inverted/nonsensical-condition] — In dartgun/tranq/update_icon(), the branch `if(!ammo_magazine.stored_ammo || ammo_magazine.stored_ammo.len)` is logically broken (it's true whenever there's any ammo or no stored_ammo list), but since every branch of the if/else if/else assigns the identical `icon_state = "tranqgun"`, the whole conditional is inert dead code.
- **code/modules/random_map/noise/noise.dm:164-167** [duplicate/inverted condition - dead branch] — The smoothing step has `else if(map[current_cell] < total) map[current_cell]+=cell_smooth_amt` immediately followed by `else if(map[current_cell] < total) map[current_cell]-=cell_smooth_amt`. The second `else if` repeats the first condition, so its decrement branch is unreachable. It was clearly meant to be `map[current_cell] > total` (when the current value is above the target, step it down).
- **code/modules/reagents/machinery/dispenser/cartridge.dm:73** [null-deref / ordering bug] — The guard `if(!target.reagents.total_volume && target.reagents)` dereferences `target.reagents.total_volume` BEFORE the `target.reagents` null-check on the right side of the `&&`. The operands are in the wrong order: the existence check is useless because the deref already happened.
- **code/modules/reagents/machinery/dispenser/cartridge.dm:81** [type safety (`:` operator on subtype member)] — `var/trans = target.reagents.trans_to(src, target:amount_per_transfer_from_this)` uses the `:` operator to reach `amount_per_transfer_from_this`, which is not a member of the declared arg type `/obj/target` but of `/obj/structure/reagent_dispensers`. This bypasses DreamChecker's compile-time member validation.
- **code/modules/reagents/machinery/dispenser/supply.dm:391** [off-by-one] — When screwdrivering the cup dispenser off the water cooler, `for(var/i = 0 to cups)` iterates `cups + 1` times (inclusive both ends), spawning one more `/obj/item/reagent_containers/food/drinks/sillycup` than the `cups` counter says are present.
- **code/modules/reagents/reactions/instant/instant.dm:1505-1509, 1546-1550** [dead/duplicate code] — /datum/decl/chemical_reaction/instant/tercozolam is defined twice with identical id (REAGENT_ID_TERCOZOLAM), result, required_reagents and result_amount. The first block (1505) is missing a name and the second (1546) is a verbatim duplicate introduced during the hard-fork de-suffix merge.
- **code/modules/reagents/reagents/food_drinks.dm:5328, 5374, 5410, 5422, 5442, 5456, 5480, 5494, 5517** [dead-code/behavior] — Multiple /datum/reagent/ethanol subtypes (galacticpanic, italiancrisis, shroomjuice, russianroulette, lovemaker, glowingappletini, scsatw, honeyshot, redspaceflush) declare `var/adj_dizzy = N` as a new instance var. The ethanol base type (defined in dispenser.dm) has no `adj_dizzy` var and never reads it — ethanol's affect_blood/affect_ingest use hardcoded make_dizzy(18)/make_dizzy(6). These declarations are copy-pasted from /datum/reagent/drink (which does consume adj_dizzy).
- **code/modules/reagents/reagents/food_drinks.dm:6757-6761** [null-deref] — /datum/reagent/ethanol/crevice_spike/affect_ingest iterates `for(var/datum/reagent/R in M.ingested.reagent_list)` with no null guard on `M.ingested`, and also does not call `..()`. Sibling procs in this same file consistently guard with `if(M.ingested)` (e.g. sodaoil at 2801, dyloteane at 6189) before touching `M.ingested.reagent_list`.
- **code/modules/recycling/disposal_machines.dm:160-166** [null-deref risk] — In attackby, the holder branch does `var/mob/victim = holder.held_mob` then immediately `if(victim.client)` and `victim.forceMove(src)` without verifying `victim` is non-null.
- **code/modules/research/message_server.dm:132-133** [wrong-variable / dropped argument] — send_rc_message(... priority = 1) constructs `new/datum/data_rc_msg(recipient,sender,message,stamp,id_auth)` passing only 5 args; the `priority` argument is never forwarded, so data_rc_msg.priority is left at its default and the priority->string mapping in data_rc_msg/New never runs.
- **code/modules/research/tg/experisci/experiment/types/physical_experiment.dm:8-24** [ref leak/dangling signal] — currently_scanned_atom holds a ref to a scanned atom and subtypes RegisterSignal on it (e.g. arcade_winner/teleporting register COMSIG_* on currently_scanned_atom), but the datum has no Destroy() to unregister/null it and does not register COMSIG_QDELETING on the watched atom. If the scanned atom is qdel'd while being watched, the experiment retains a hard ref to it and a stale signal registration.
- **code/modules/research/tg/machinery/mech_fabricator.dm:269-272** [runtime/null-deref] — process() computes `var/turf/exit = get_step(src, drop_direction)` then dereferences `exit.density` (lines 272, 285, 317). get_step() returns null when the machine sits on a map edge with drop_direction pointing off-map, making `exit.density` a null-dereference runtime that recurs every process tick. build_part() has the same pattern at lines 240-241.
- **code/modules/research/tg/techwebs/__techweb_helpers.dm:52** [wrong-variable/display bug] — `var/research_line = "[(i in SSresearch.point_types) || "ERRORED POINT TYPE"]: [pointlist[i]]"` uses `(i in SSresearch.point_types)` which evaluates to 1 (TRUE) for a valid point type, so the line renders as `1: <value>` instead of the point type's display name. The correct sibling proc techweb_point_display_generic (lines 43-44) uses `SSresearch.point_types[i]` to fetch the label.
- **code/modules/resleeving/autoresleever.dm:238-249** [behavior-changing dead code] — `spawn(0)` on line 238 and line 245 have empty bodies: the following lines (239+, 246+) are at the same indentation as the `spawn`, so they are NOT inside the spawn block. The intended 'wait for NIF to install' deferral never happens; NIF software restore runs synchronously inline.
- **code/modules/resleeving/computers.dm:266-270** [runtime/null-deref] — The "ejectdisk" tgui_act branch calls `disk.forceMove(get_turf(src))` and `disk = null` with no `if(disk)` guard, unlike the "coredump" branch which checks `if(disk)`. A client-supplied tgui action can invoke ejectdisk when disk is null.
- **code/modules/rogueminer/asteroid.dm:65-70** [copy-paste bug] — /datum/rogue/asteroid/proc/spot_remove() calls `work.Add(thing)` instead of `work.Remove(thing)`. It is a copy-paste of spot_add()'s body; the proc that is supposed to remove an entry from the asteroid map actually adds another copy.
- **code/modules/salvage/salvage.dm:7-8** [dead code / copy-paste] — /obj/item/salvage sets `throw_speed = 1` on line 7 then `throw_speed = 4` on line 8 — the same var assigned twice; the first assignment is dead. Likely the second was meant to be `throw_range`.
- **code/modules/samples/container.dm:30** [unit confusion] — afterattack() checks `if(contents.len >= max_storage_space)` (also samples.dm line 193). It compares item COUNT (contents.len) against max_storage_space, which is a w_class capacity sum (ITEMSIZE_TINY*8). It only coincidentally works because ITEMSIZE_TINY==1 makes both equal 8; the storage_slots var (8) is the correct count limit.
- **code/modules/scripting/Implementations/_Logic.dm:222-224** [arithmetic] — n_ceil(num) returns `round(num)+1` for all numeric input, so ceil() of an already-integer value is off by one (e.g. n_ceil(5) returns 6, not 5). It also doesn't actually ceil non-integers correctly (n_ceil(5.2) returns round(5.2)+1 = 6 which is right, but n_ceil(5.0)=6 is wrong).
- **code/modules/scripting/Implementations/_Logic.dm:284-289** [resource/log-spam] — string_replacetext() calls log_world("## MISC ...") three times per replacement inside the main replacement loop (left-over debug logging).
- **code/modules/spells/targeted/ethereal_jaunt.dm:94-95** [null-deref] — In /obj/effect/dummy/spell_jaunt/relaymove(), `var/turf/newLoc = get_step(src,direction)` can return null at a map edge, and the next line `if(!(newLoc.flags & NOJAUNT))` dereferences newLoc.flags.
- **code/modules/tables/flipping.dm:8** [null-deref] — In /obj/structure/table/proc/straight_table_check, line 8 reads `T.material.name == material.name` without guarding `T.material`. A neighbouring table (e.g. a bench frame or an unplated table frame, both of which have `material = null`) makes `T.material.name` a null dereference. The sibling proc unflipping_check (line 53) and flip() (line 94) both guard `T.material` before reading `.name`, so the omission here is inconsistent.
- **code/modules/telesci/hyper_pad.dm:12-34** [resource-leak/dangling-ref] — /obj/machinery/hyperpad/centre registers itself into the static assoc list `mapped_hyper_pads[map_pad_id] = src` in Initialize and spawns child `/obj/machinery/hyperpad` pads into `linked` (with `new_pad.primary = src`), but the type defines no Destroy() override. On qdel, the global static `mapped_hyper_pads` keeps a hard ref to the deleted centre, and the eight child pads (and their `.primary` back-refs) are neither cleaned nor qdel'd.
- **code/modules/tgs_commands/vorestation.dm:288-289** [dead-code] — In /datum/tgs_chat_command/ticketreply/Run, `action` is taken from splittext(params, " ") so it is always a text string, yet the code guards with `if(isnum(action)) return "Second param must be the action type."`. isnum() on a string is always FALSE, so this validation branch is unreachable/dead and never catches a numeric action.
- **code/modules/tgui/modules/appearance_changer.dm:1032-1036** [signals] — /datum/tgui_module/appearance_changer/vore/update_active_camera_screen() overrides the base proc which is registered as a signal handler via RegisterSignal(owner, COMSIG_MOVABLE_ATTEMPTED_MOVE, PROC_REF(update_active_camera_screen)) (line 676/1149), but the vore override is missing the SIGNAL_HANDLER macro on its first line (the base at line 892 has it). When the vore subtype's owner moves, this override is the proc invoked by the signal, and it lacks the SIGNAL_HANDLER declaration (and could legally sleep), violating the signal-handler contract.
- **code/modules/tgui/modules/law_manager.dm:183-187** [null-deref] — is_special_role(user) reads `user.mind.special_role` without verifying user.mind is non-null. It is reachable via is_malf() from tgui_act on actions like add_ion_law/add_inherent_law/edit_law.
- **code/modules/tgui/modules/law_manager.dm:49** [wrong-variable/always-true-condition] — The add_supplied_law guard reads `supplied_law_position >= 1 && MIN_SUPPLIED_LAW_NUMBER <= MAX_SUPPLIED_LAW_NUMBER && is_malf(...)`. The middle term compares two compile-time constants (15 <= 50) which is always true and ignores the user-controlled `supplied_law_position`. The intended upper-bound check on the position is missing.
- **code/modules/tgui/modules/rcon.dm:16** [arithmetic] — `number_pages = (length(known_SMESs) + SMES_PER_PAGE - 1) / SMES_PER_PAGE` uses DM float division, so number_pages can be fractional (e.g. 5 SMES -> 1.75... actually (5+3)/4=2.0; 6 -> (6+3)/4=2.25). The ceil idiom only works with integer division, which DM does not do for `/`.
- **code/modules/turbolift/turbolift_map.dm:186** [wrong-variable/type-mismatch] — For internal (interior) lift doors, `newdoor.lift = cfloor` assigns a `/datum/turbolift_floor` to the door's `lift` var which is typed `/datum/turbolift` (the controller). The controller correctly tracks the door via `lift.doors += newdoor`, but the door's own back-reference points at the floor instead of the controller.
- **code/modules/ventcrawl/ventcrawl_verb.dm:5-7** [wrong-call / behaving-incorrectly] — ventcrawl() calls `start_ventcrawl()` to pick a specific pipe, but then invokes `handle_ventcrawl()` with no arguments. handle_ventcrawl(atom/clicked_on) expects the chosen pipe; called with no arg, clicked_on is null and the proc falls back to its own range(1) search, discarding the player's pipe selection.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/living.dm:989-995** [null-deref risk] — In handle_eat_minerals(), `H` is `var/mob/living/carbon/human/H = src`. The eligibility gate at line 885 is `adminbus_eat_minerals || (istype(H) && H.species.eat_minerals)`, so a non-human mob with `adminbus_eat_minerals` set bypasses the istype(H) guard. If such a mob eats a 'WTF' mineral, lines 989-995 call `H.Weaken`, `H.Confuse`, `H.druggy`, `H.make_dizzy` on a null-cast H.
- **code/modules/vore/eating/soulcatcher.dm:40-45** [null-deref] — In /obj/soulgem/deserialize(), when apply_stored_belly() fails it falls through to `owner.recalculate_vis()` (line 45). `owner` is only assigned in Initialize when `ismob(loc)`; if the gem is deserialized while not located in a mob, owner is null and this dereferences null.
- **code/modules/vore/resizing/resize.dm:173** [operator precedence] — `if((allow_stripping == TRUE) && (size_difference >= 0.3) || (size_difference <= -0.3))` — `&&` binds tighter than `||`, so this parses as `((allow_stripping && size_difference>=0.3)) || (size_difference<=-0.3)`. The negative-difference (shrink) branch fires even when allow_stripping is FALSE.
- **code/modules/vore/smoleworld/smoleworld.dm:15** [eager per-instance list (lazylist anti-pattern)] — `/turf/simulated/floor/smole` declares `var/list/crossed_dirs = list()` as an eager instance list. Every smole floor turf allocates a list on init even though it is only populated when a giant-sized mob steps on it, which is rare.
- **code/modules/vote/vote_verb.dm:44, 48** [unvalidated input after tgui_input] — `c3 = tgui_input_list(...)` may return null if the admin cancels, and it is assigned directly to `V.vote_result_type` without a null check, overwriting the datum's default result type with null.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/boulder.dm:21-22** [null-deref risk] — On core_sampler attackby, `src.geological_data.artifact_distance` and `artifact_find.artifact_id` are dereferenced, but neither `geological_data` nor `artifact_find` is initialized in Initialize() (only icon_state/excavation_level are). If the boulder was not assigned these by an external spawner, this runtimes.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/effects/cell.dm:40, 43-45** [arithmetic/logic error] — For silicon DRAIN, line 40 uses `min(C.maxcharge, C.charge - amount)` to subtract, allowing charge to go negative (should be max(0, ...)). For non-silicon movables (lines 43-45) the cell is ALWAYS charged regardless of the `effect` argument, ignoring drain entirely.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/effects/gravitational_waves.dm:41** [arithmetic-error] — DoEffectAura(): seconds_since_last_pull = max(0, round((last_wave_pull - world.time) / 10)). Since last_wave_pull is always <= world.time, (last_wave_pull - world.time) is <= 0, so the expression is always clamped to 0. The operands are reversed; it should be world.time - last_wave_pull to measure elapsed time.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/finds/find_spawning.dm:15-18** [dead-code/copy-paste] — get_responsive_reagent(): ARCHAEO_CULTBLADE appears in two consecutive switch branches. The first (line 15: 'if(ARCHAEO_CULTBLADE, ARCHAEO_SOULSTONE, ARCHAEO_TOME) return REAGENT_BLOOD') wins, so the second branch (line 17: 'if(ARCHAEO_CULTBLADE, ARCHAEO_TELEBEACON, ARCHAEO_CULTROBES) return REAGENT_ID_POTASSIUM') can never be reached for ARCHAEO_CULTBLADE.
- **code/modules/xenoarcheaology/tools/suspension_generator.dm:142-145** [null-deref] — emag_act() dereferences cell.charge, but cell can be null: attack_hand() (line 39-49) removes and nulls cell when the panel is closed. Emagging the generator after the cell has been removed runtimes on cell.charge.
- **code/modules/xenobio/items/slimepotions.dm:126-130** [missing re-validation after sleeping input] — In /obj/item/slimepotion/docility/attack, after AI.remove_target() the code calls tgui_input_text(user, ...) (a sleeping input) and then unconditionally sets M.name and M.real_name from the result without re-checking that M (and user) still exist or are valid.
- **maps/common_submaps/gateway/snowfield.dm:510** [wrong-variable / usr misuse] — In /obj/item/gun/launcher/scopedrocket/attackby(obj/item/I, mob/user) the 'too many rockets' else-branch calls to_chat(usr, span_red(...)) using usr, while every other to_chat in the same proc correctly uses the user argument.
- **maps/~turfpacks/packs_sim/turfpacks_sim_tiled.dm:921-925** [copy-paste/missing-field] — /turf/simulated/floor/tiled/freezer/cold/turfpack/TURFPACK_PACKNAME omits the `temperature = TURFPACK_TEMP` line that every other entry in this and the sibling turfpack files includes (it sets only oxygen/nitrogen/phoron/carbon_dioxide).

## REJECTED as false positives (23)
- code/_helpers/priority_queue.dm:38-41 — bubble_up() computes the parent index as `index / 2`, which is fractional for odd `index` (e.g. index 3 -> 1.5) and is then used directly as a list subscript `array[index / 2]` and assigned back to `index`. The sibling heap in legacy_tg_path.dm correctly uses `round(index * 0.5)` for the parent, and this file's own bubble_down() uses integer `index * 2`. Relying on BYOND silently flooring fractional list indices is fragile and inconsistent. → REJECTED: false: bubble_up uses array[index/2]; BYOND silently floors fractional list subscripts so floor(i/2) is the correct heap parent and behavior is correct. Pure fragility/consistency nit, not a defect.
- code/game/machinery/contraband_scanner.dm:36-48 — contraband_count is incremented every Crossed but only reset to 0 inside the alarm branch (line 43 condition `contraband_count && last_trigger < world.time - cooldown`). If contraband is detected while still within the cooldown window, the count is left non-zero. The next mob to cross — even carrying nothing — finds contraband_count already > 0 and, once the cooldown has elapsed, triggers a false alarm. → REJECTED: false: line 34 returns early when in cooldown BEFORE any counting, so contraband_count cannot accumulate during cooldown; the described stale-count/unbounded-growth mechanism does not occur.
- code/game/machinery/transportpod.dm:100-107 — In build(), `var/turf/T = get_turf(current_cell)` is computed before the null check `if(!current_cell) continue`, and then `T.ChangeTurf(...)` is called. If `locate(x,y,1)` returns null, get_turf(null) returns null and `T.ChangeTurf()` would null-deref (the null guard runs after T is already dereferenced on the next line only after the continue, but T can still be null if current_cell is non-null yet get_turf yields null). → REJECTED: false: current_cell = locate(x,y,1) is itself a turf-or-null; get_turf of a turf returns itself, of null returns null, so T is null only when current_cell is null, which the continue guards. The non-null-cell/null-turf case is impossible.
- code/game/objects/items/toys/toys.dm:2736-2745 — `/obj/item/toy/monster_bait/afterattack(atom/A, mob/user)` casts `var/mob/living/simple_mob/M = A` and immediately reads `M.z`, `get_dist(user,M)` and then `M.vore_active` BEFORE the `if(!istype(M)) return` guard on line 2741. It also ignores the `proximity` flag entirely (signature drops it). → REJECTED: false: M.vore_active is read at line 2743, AFTER the if(!istype(M)) return at 2741, so it is guarded. Lines before guard only read M.z (valid /atom var) and get_dist (accepts any atom); no wrong-type vore_active deref.
- code/modules/blob2/core_chunk.dm:115-117 — regen() creates a new core with `new (get_turf(src))` (no overmind arg) then immediately reads `NC.overmind.blob_type` and `NC.overmind.blob_core.update_icon()`. A freshly-created core sets its overmind in LateInitialize (it returns INITIALIZE_HINT_LATELOAD when unplaced and overmind-less), so NC.overmind is null at the moment these lines run if late init has not completed. → REJECTED: false: base /obj/structure/blob/core is ai_controlled=TRUE; runtime new triggers LateInitialize synchronously (atoms_initializing line 33) and the ai_controlled branch assigns overmind/blob_core synchronously before line 116 runs.
- code/modules/blob2/overmind/types/ectoplasmic_horror.dm:34-59 — on_pulse (factory branch) and on_chunk_tick create /datum/beam drain beams stored in active_beams. The beam is only qdel'd inside the spawn(9 SECONDS) closure, and only if the guard (B && drain_beam) passes. If the blob/factory B is destroyed (or the spawn's condition fails) before the timer fires, the beam is never qdel'd and remains referenced in active_beams. There is no Destroy()/cleanup that clears active_beams or its beams. → REJECTED: false: beams created with time=10 SECONDS self-qdel via Start()'s endtime loop (beam.dm line 48), and listclearnulls(active_beams) at tick start cleans them; no lifetime-long ref leak.
- code/modules/blob2/overmind/types/roiling_mold.dm:29-31 — find_target sets L = locate() in (view(...) - view(2,...) - previous_targets), which can be null, then immediately calls check_trajectory(L, B, PASSTABLE) with a possibly-null L at line 31. → REJECTED: false: check_trajectory guards if(!istype(target)...) return 0 (trace.dm line 4), so null L yields 'null in 0' = FALSE, no runtime; only benign null added to previous_targets, bounded by tries>3.
- code/modules/casino/casino_clothing.dm:74-78 — /obj/structure/stripper_pole/proc/dance() toggles `layer` between ABOVE_MOB_LAYER and BELOW_MOB_LAYER. The pole is defined with `layer = BELOW_MOB_LAYER`, but attack_hand calls dance() unconditionally each click; the toggle has no relation to whether a mob is actually on the pole, so layering can desync (pole ends up ABOVE_MOB_LAYER with nobody dancing). → REJECTED: false: code is in casino_items.dm lines 74-78 (not casino_clothing.dm), and the per-click layer toggle is an intentional cosmetic flip, not a defect — pure cosmetic nit.
- code/modules/clothing/masks/hailer.dm:97-102 — `/obj/item/clothing/mask/gas/sechailer/emag_act(mob/user)` returns without ever chaining `..()` on either branch (sets safety=0 and returns implicitly; else `return`). emag_act overrides typically need to return the parent result so the emag interaction is registered/consumed properly. → REJECTED: false: base /atom/emag_act just `return -1` and does no consumption/feedback, so not chaining ..() loses no behavior; claimed harm doesn't exist.
- code/modules/lore_codex/codex_tree.dm:29-37, 152-154 — Topic() handles href_list["target"] by doing `var/datum/lore/codex/new_page = locate(href_list["target"])` then `go_to_page(new_page, FALSE, usr)`. go_to_page only checks `if(new_page && istype(D))` where D is the CURRENT page; it never validates that new_page is actually a /datum/lore/codex before storing it as current_page["[user]"]. A client can craft a byond:// href with any datum ref. → REJECTED: false: display() re-validates at line 99 'if(!istype(D))' before reading D.data/.name/.parent — an arbitrary non-codex datum stored via go_to_page fails istype and is reset to home, so no runtime / no arbitrary-datum view.
- code/modules/mob/dead/observer/free.dm:42-43 — `if(GLOB.PDA_Manifest.len) GLOB.PDA_Manifest.Cut()` wipes the ENTIRE station PDA crew manifest cache whenever any single player uses the 'Quit This Round' verb, rather than removing only that player's entry. The surrounding code carefully removes only `src.real_name` from the medical/security/general data_core records, so cutting the whole manifest is inconsistent and destructive. → REJECTED: false: PDA_Manifest is a GLOBAL_LIST_EMPTY cache that datacore.dm explicitly documents is 'zeroed out upon any change'; Cut() is the idiomatic invalidation used by cryopod/card/medical too, and get_manifest_list() lazily regenerates it from updated records — not destructive.
- code/modules/mob/living/melee_swing.dm:100-117 — begin_melee_swing() does an Adjacent(target) check before the do_after windup, but after the windup it only re-checks that the weapon is still in hand (lines 101-103). It then resolves the swing against whoever is in the swing tiles. This is mostly intentional (the design lets victims dodge), but `target` itself is never re-validated for QDELETED after the windup before do_attack_animation(target)/face logic, and zone_sel is read without re-checking the mob is still conscious. → REJECTED: false: do_after cancels on attacker incapacitation/move, weapon re-validated lines 101-103; resolve runs against tile victims not target; qdeleted target harmless to do_attack_animation. Style/completeness nit
- code/modules/mob/living/silicon/robot/robot_ui.dm:132-142 — The `toggle_module` tgui_act case does `var/obj/item/module = locate(params["ref"])` and, if it merely istype()s as /obj/item, calls R.activated(module)/R.uneq_specific(module)/R.activate_module(module) without verifying the located object actually belongs to R.module.modules. The sibling `activate_module` case (line 144-147) does validate ownership via `module.loc == R`, but toggle_module does not. → REJECTED: false: toggle_module's downstream procs all re-validate ownership — activate_module (inventory.dm:341) checks `locate(O) in module.modules/emag`, uneq_specific only acts on module_state_1/2/3, activated() is read-only. A foreign /obj/item ref cannot be equipped/manipulated; no behavior change.
- code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/animal/passive/cockroach.dm:56 — Crossed(atom/movable/AM) override does not call ..(), so any parent Crossed() behavior (atom/movable signal/Crossed handling, e.g. enter triggers) is suppressed on every cross of a cockroach tile. → REJECTED: false: Crossed override (line 56) doesn't call ..(), but this matches the squish-code design (sibling simple_mob Crossed overrides also omit it); intentional, not a behavioral defect of note.
- code/modules/mob/living/simple_mob/subtypes/humanoid/pirate.dm:159-167 — Under the 'Shield Pirate' section, the bullet-block proc is defined on `/mob/living/simple_mob/humanoid/merc/melee/sword/bullet_act` instead of `/mob/living/simple_mob/humanoid/pirate/shield/bullet_act`. The shield pirate's attackby block is correctly defined (line 146) but its bullet_act block is not. Separately this re-defines merc/melee/sword/bullet_act, which already exists in mercs.dm (line 116). → REJECTED: false: pirate.dm is only 17 lines (handcannon/captain projectiletype + a commented block). No lines 159-167, no shield pirate or sword bullet_act here. Wrong/stale file cited.
- code/modules/persistence/graffiti.dm:16-22 — /datum/persistent/graffiti/CheckTurfContents iterates `for(var/obj/effect/decal/writing/W in .)` — it loops over the return value `.` (which is null at entry) instead of the turf argument `T`. → REJECTED: false: graffiti.dm has NO CheckTurfContents proc and no `for(... in .)` loop; only loop is Initialize :20 `for(... in loc)` which is correct. Misattributed/fabricated.
- code/modules/reagents/reagent_containers/spray.dm:236 — RegisterSignal(src, COMSIG_MOVABLE_ATTEMPTED_MOVE, /obj/item/reagent_containers/spray/chemsprayer/hosed/proc/update_hose) passes a raw proc type-path as the callback instead of using PROC_REF(update_hose) like every other RegisterSignal call site in the codebase (e.g. cards.dm:281, machinery_power.dm:85, recursive_move.dm:133). → REJECTED: false: raw proc-path in RegisterSignal is a style/standards deviation (PROC_REF preferred) but works at runtime — other sites (shield_omni:79, ore_bag:148) do the same; no behavior change
- code/modules/recycling/disposal_outlet.dm:51-56 — In attackby, the welder-deconstruct branch checks `if(!W.isOn())` and shows a warning but does not `return`. Execution falls through to `if(W.remove_fuel(0,user))` and proceeds to slice the floorweld even though the welder is off. → REJECTED: false: remove_fuel (weldingtool.dm:185) returns 0 immediately when !welding, so the deconstruct block doesn't run with an off welder. Missing return only causes a redundant warning, not deconstruction.
- code/modules/recycling/recycling.dm:34-47 — attackby on /obj/machinery/recycling has `if(!isliving(user) ... )` guard but then does `var/mob/living/M = user` and calls `M.drop_from_inventory(O)` after `can_accept_item`. The early `default_*` deconstruction calls run before the isliving filter? No—precedence is fine; but the real issue: the deliveryChute/crusher path `take_item` (line 103) calls `sleep(5 SECONDS)` while `working=TRUE`, and during that sleep the machine can be deconstructed (default_deconstruction_*) by another user since `working` is only checked at attackby entry, not re-validated; the qdel'd src then continues executing forceMove on `get_step(src,dir)` against a destroyed object. → REJECTED: false: deconstruction is via attackby which is gated by 'if(working) return' (recycling.dm:31); can_accept_item also returns !working. No deconstruction or feeding occurs during the sleep.
- code/modules/recycling/v_garbosystem.dm:78-81 — process() does `affecting = loc.contents - src` then `spawn(1)` iterates `affecting`. `loc` is dereferenced without a null check, and `affecting` is a shared instance var reassigned every process tick while prior spawn(1)/spawn(15) blocks may still be reading it. → REJECTED: false: process() runs on a turf-loc'd machine; nullspace machines stop processing via base Destroy/STOP_PROCESSING. spawn(1) completes within one SSmachines tick (2s wait) so affecting is not raced. loc-null path not realistically reachable.
- code/modules/vore/eating/panel_databackend/vorepanel_set_attribute.dm:697-701 — `b_autotransferwait` reads `var/autotransferwait_input = params["val"]` directly (no `text2num`) then computes `autotransferwait_input*10` inside `sanitize_integer`. Every other numeric handler in this file uses `text2num(params["val"])` first. If tgui ever delivers `val` as a string, the `isnum()` check returns FALSE harmlessly, but if it passes a non-numeric truthy value the arithmetic `*10` would runtime. → REJECTED: false: the `*10` at L701 only runs after the isnum() guard at L699-700 returns FALSE for non-numbers, so a string/non-numeric val is cleanly rejected before any arithmetic. Pure style inconsistency, no runtime.
- code/modules/vore/eating/transforming.dm:2-7 — `/obj/belly/proc/put_in_egg` casts `var/mob/living/carbon/human/O = owner` and immediately reads `O.vore_egg_type` and uses `O` in messages without verifying owner is a human (or even non-null). Bellies can be owned by non-human mobs. → REJECTED: false: vore_egg_type is defined on /mob/living (living.dm L10), not human; reading O.vore_egg_type on a non-human /mob/living owner does NOT runtime. The sole caller (bellymodes_datum.dm:232) is commented out.
- code/modules/vore/mob_tf.dm:226 — `if(size_multiplier != 1 || icon_scale_x != 1 && center_offset > 0)` mixes `||` and `&&` without parentheses. It parses as `size_multiplier != 1 || (icon_scale_x != 1 && center_offset > 0)`, which is likely not the intended grouping for the set_dir transform-refresh guard. → REJECTED: false: precedence is well-defined and the only consequence is a possibly-redundant update_transform(TRUE) call on set_dir; no correctness defect demonstrated, claim is speculative ('likely not intended').
